Zabuli 1

CITABU CILONGOZI

(Zabuli 1–41)

Nyemo ye mbeka

1 Yali ciba munhu mono si yakuzityatya,

zinghani ze wanhu wabi,

hamba kutyatya mikalile,

ye wanhu wahodanji,

hamba kwilumbaniza ne wanhu wono,

wakumubedegala Mulungu.

2 Langa yakuwonela nyemo mu wogopa

wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu,

no kuwihwesa wuwo, misi ne cilo.

3 U munhu we liwala lilyo,

yehwanile mubici wono muhadikwe

behi no muvwilo we malenga,

wono wulelelaga mana mu madaha gayo,

ne mahamha gayo hamba si ganyalaga yaye.

Nayo kamoteswaga muli zose zono ketumaga.

4 Ninga wanhu wabi vyo si wenha

nha vivyo hamba hadodo,

wene wehwanile mawaje gono

gakupemheluswa ne mbeho.

5 Wanhu wabi, Mulungu kowaling'aniza,

kanyhi wotemanulwa ne mhuga ya we ling'ani.

6 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha kawatongaga we ling'ani, ninga iwanhu wabi,

imikalile yawo yowongomeza.


Zabuli 2

U Mutemi muhagulwa wo Mulungu

1 Alu socici wanhu we makungugo,

wakuleta zo wutilibule?

Nhawule wanhu wakwihehemeza bwete?

2 Watemi wa mononze wakwiwikaniza,

wawaha nawo wakwiheha hamonga,

ho wamubedegale Muwaha Mulungu,

hamonga no mwitilwa mafuta wakwe muhagulwa!

3 Wakutya, “Alenyi ciyiwuse mu wulongola wawo,

kanyhi cigawopole ne manyolo gawo!”

4 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu mono yali ku wulanga,

yakuwaseka no kuwezwajiza.

5 Holeka kolonga nawo kwe'kuwo,

no kuwogofya ko wukali.

6 “Namutemya mwitilwa mafuta wangu,

yatemile ku Sayuni,

litunda lyangu lyono lyelile!”

7 Nayo mwitilwa mafuta yakutya,

“Nowanza wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu.

Muwaha Mulungu yanonjela yakatya,

‘Agwe wuli mwanangu, zuwali nghulela.

8 Ale wulombe kuli'ne

nane nokupela makungugo gawe wuhazi wako,

ne yinze nayo yose yiwe sawo yako.

9 Nagwe kowapondanjiza ne nghome ya cuma,

kanyhi kowatulanga nha nyungu yo muwumbi!’ ”

10 Holeka 'lu anye nyo watemi,

mwitumamilaje mahala,

hulicizenyi uwukagwe wuno,

anye mo wawaha we yinze.

11 Ale, mumwitumamilaje Muwaha Mulungu kwe cogopo,

mucicime kanyhi mumufugamile,

12 hono mulece kunoza nhavivyo,

mwene kosimba nanye mokongomezwa,

hambi likuwo lyakwe nha lyakaga nhinicizo.

Nyemo mbaha kuli wose wono wakwihundika muli mwene!


Zabuli 3

Kulomba wutazi mwipango lye hakali mitondo

(Zabuli ya Dawudi lono yamutizaga Abusalomu)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

i walugu wangu nha wenji,

kanyhi wenji muno wono wali suta nane!

2 Howali wenji muno wono wakutya,

“Ati agwe go Mulungu sikosuma kunhaza!”

3 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuli ngula yangu,

nane nghwilumba no kutuga wuluwuce ku soko yako.

4 Nane nghumulilila Mulungu kwe'zi, ho yanhaze,

nayo kamhitulaga mu wulawa mwi'tunda lyakwe lyono lyelile.

5 Holeka alu nane nosuma

kugona nhongo no kwinuka kanyhi.

Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha yakunghamisa.

6 Nane hamba sinowogopa maelufu ge wanhu,

wono wamhilime kose kose.

7 Wuze 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Wumhonye, gwe go Mulungu wangu!

Hambi nha yo gwegwe mono kuwabenaga

nzagasa i walugu wangu wose,

kanyhi kuwabenangaga meno gawo i wanhu wabi,

ho walece kunhamisa.

8 Agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

yo gwegwe du mono kuponyaga,

ale wuwamotyaje wanhu wako.


Zabuli 4

Kulomba wutazi mwipango lye cinyamihe

(Kuli mwimbizi, hamonga ne vitumamilwa vye zinghuzi vye masumbi Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Mulungu, go mudimi wangu!

Kolo nghukulomba, wuhulicizaje.

Lono nali nili mu wugaye, agwe wukanhaza,

holeka wumbonele lusungu,

wuhulicize wulombe wangu.

2 Mwene anye nyo wanhu,

alu mojendelela kunhovula sunga nili?

Alu ku sokoci mwendile makani go wulele,

no kulonga wulambi?

3 Mumanye nha vyono, Muwaha Mulungu yakondya,

kwihagulila we ling'ani wakwe.

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha kahulicizaga ane lono nghumulomba.

4 Mucicime kwe cogopo, mulece kwituma zo wubi.

Mwihwesaje muno muno,

lono muli mu vitanda vyenyu mutulile.

5 Mulavye makumbiko go wiling'ano,

akuno mukumwitumbila Muwaha Mulungu.

6 Kowali wenji wono walombaga wakutya,

“Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wucimotyaje muno muno!

Kanyhi wucilanje ko lusungu!”

7 Kumhela nyemo mbaha mu nhumbula yangu,

kuciza wene wawo,

hamba wawe wena wuhemba winji hamonga ne divayi.

8 Na lono nghutambalala mu citanda,

ngonaga nhongo ko mulamu muno,

hambi nha yo gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuli wene,

mono kundimaga nikala mholile.


Zabuli 5

Kulomba wudimwe mwi'pango lye zimbali

(Kuli mwimbizi, hamonga ne zifilimbi Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu

wuhulicize zinghani zangu,

wuhulice zono nghuzihila mu nhumbula yangu.

2 Wuhulicize cililo cangu,

'gwe Mulungu go mutemi wangu,

hambi nha yo gwegwe du mono nghulombaga.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu kulihulikaga

lizi lyangu hakali mitondo muno muno,

nane nghulavizyaga likumbiko lyangu

hakali mitondo, hanyuma niwa nghulanjiliye.

4 Agwe yo gwegwe Mulungu mono siwendile wubi,

no munhu muhodanji sikokwikala kuli'gwe.

5 Ne wedunhumuzi si wokwima hali'gwe,

agwe nha wuwasulile wose wono wetumaga wubi.

6 Nagwe wukuwa kuwongomeza wose,

i walonga zo wulambi,

kanyhi kuwakomalaga i wawulaji wose,

hamonga ne wakonji wose.

7 Ninga ku so winji wo lusungu lwako,

ane nosuma kwinjila mu Nyumba yako,

nane nohemba mu Nyumba yako yono yelile kwe cogopo.

8 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wunhongaje mu ling'ani yako,

hambi walugu wangu nha wenji muno,

holeka wundajile calinze inzila yako hamwande yangu.

9 Izinghani ze walugu wangu,

si za kwitumbilwa yaye,

hambi wuhile wawo, nha wukuleta wongomeze du.

Mu zinhumbula zawo wamemile wubi wose,

ne mimelo yawo, wenha citenjelo cono cili mwazi,

wekalaga wakulonga zo wulambi du.

10 Gwe go Mulungu, wuwalipicizize mu wubi wawo,

wagwe so wuhile wawo wubi,

wuwagwize weneco wuwawinjile kutali,

ku soko ye masutya gawo menji,

hambi nha wakondya kukubedegala agwe.

11 Ninga wose wono wakwihundika Muli'gwe,

wasecelele, wembe ko wusangalale vilo vyose,

wuwadimaje wose wono wakwendile gwegwe,

ho wasangalale ku soko yako.

12 Hambi 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha kumumotyaga munhu we ling'ani,

kanyhi kumucitilaga nha ngula,

ku soko yo lusungu lwako.


Zabuli 6

Kulomba wutazi mwi'daha lyo wugazice

(Kuli mwimbizi: Hamonga ne vitumamilwa vye zinghunzi vye masumbi nha vyono winha wimbe wa Shemininti Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kunhacila kwe likuwo lyako,

hamba wulece kundipiciziza mwi'kuwo lyako.

2 Wumbonelaje lusungu, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi ane nha nili musocele,

holeka wumhonye 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha nghucicima sunga mo mu mafupa mugati.

3 Nghugazika muno mu nhumbula yangu,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

alu kocela kunhaza sunga nili?

4 Wunangaje, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu

wumhonye nhumbula yangu,

wumhonye mwi'fwa so lusungu lwako.

5 Hambi kuko ku yinze ye wafu,

nha kusina mono kakukumbucilaga yaye,

hamba hasina munhu kuko mono kosuma kukulumba.

6 Nghataye muno muno ku soko ye cililo cangu co wusungu,

vilo vyose ku cilo nghucisuluziza citanda cangu mahozi,

na mu wulile wangu, nghuwusuluziza muto wangu.

7 Ne meso gangu gakatala so wukalale,

gavimba ku soko yo wugazwe ne walugu.

8 Muwuce ha mwande yangu,

anye wose mo wetuma wubi!

Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha yacihulika cililo cangu.

9 U Muwaha Mulungu kawuhulika wulombe wangu,

u Muwaha Mulungu kavumila wulombe wangu.

10 Ne walugu wangu wose wokolwa cevu,

kanyhi wogaya muno,

wowuya ne nyuma no kuwona cevu nhinicizo.


Zabuli 7

Wulombe wo munhu mono yakwiwonelwa

(Cililo ca Dawudi ku soko ya Kushi, wo mulango wa Benyamini)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

Mulungu wangu, ane nghwitumbiye gwegwe,

umhonye kuli wose wono wakunhyatilila,

wumbuse muli wene.

2 Na hono wulece kunozeza nhavivyo,

wene wokuza wahawanje nha simba,

wanghwejele kutali, kono kowa kusina mono komhonya.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

Mulungu wangu!

Hono niwe nitumile yinji ya muli zizi,

na hono niwe niyitipiza ko wubi miwoko yangu,

4 na hono yiwe lunji

nimutendela vibi muyangu,

hamba lunji niwe nimutamisa

mulugu wangu mbula soko,

5 Ale hodu lece mulugu wangu yanhyatilile, yanyibate,

yawukanyanjile wumi wangu, yambulaje.

6 Ale, wuzize mu wukali wako 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulimile likuwo lye walugu wangu.

Winuce 'gwe go Mulungu wangu, hambi 'gwe,

nhondo zose nha kusakaga ling'ani yifisilizwe.

7 Wugakonghole hali gwegwe makungugo gose,

nagwe ho wugateme ku wulawa ku wulanga.

8 Gwegwe, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kugaling'anizaga makungugo,

ale wuning'anizizaje nane mu nghango yo wiling'ano wangu,

mu nghango yo wutelevu wangu, wono nina nawo.

9 Wavu we wanhu wabi wudume,

wumukamye munhu we ling'ani,

'gwe go Mulungu mono kwitumaga ling'ani,

mono wuzimanyile zose ze cinyele za mu zinhumbula,

ne mahweso ge wanhu.

10 U Mulungu, yo ngula yangu yono yikunyhitila

mwene kawaponyaga i wanhu watelevu.

11 U Mulungu, yo hakimu we ling'ani,

mwene kamulipicizaga munhu mubi nhondo zose.

12 Ne wanhu nawo hono walece kupituka,

u Mulungu kowunola mumaje wakwe,

kowutawika wupinde ku wulunji wawo,

13 nayo kowikaniziza lukulu vitwalo vyakwe,

vyo kuwulajila,

no kuzikangaliza mu moto zisonga zakwe.

14 Ninga i wanhu wabi wamemile wubalya,

wegute wubi, nawo wolela wukonji,

wahimbaga yikombo yitali.

15 Ne mitego yono wawatejile wayawo,

wakwigwiza kanyhi mumo weneco.

16 No wubi wawo, wowawuyila weneco du,

kanyhi wokwitamisa weneco no wukali wawo.

17 Nane nomukumya Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko yo wuswanu wakwe,

kanyhi nokwimba zo lukumyo lwakwe Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye.


Zabuli 8

U lukumyo lwo Mulungu ne lilumbo lyo munhu

(Kuli mwimbizi: Nho wimbe wa Gititi Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe Mulungu, go Muwaha wetu,

litagwa lyako nha licizize mono'nze yose!

No lukumyo lwako nha luficile ko ku wulanga,

2 Ne milomo ye wana wadodo na wono wakongha,

yose yikukukumya gwegwe.

Agwe wizenjeye ligomawakali, kuwemila walugu wako,

nagwe kuwapezyaga i walugu wako.

3 Na lono nghuwulanga mwanya,

u mulimo we vidole vyako go mweneco,

sunga no mwezi ne zinyelezi vyono waviwumba,

4 hambi munhu co cici 'gwe go Mulungu,

sunga agwe wumwihwese?

Hambi munhu co cici,

sunga agwe wumucelelele?

5 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

wamupela lukumyo ne lilumbo

hadodo du, yehwane na gwegwe go Mulungu.

6 Nagwe wukamupela wulajilize

muli milimo yako yose.

Wukaviwika viwumbwa vyose hasi yo

wulajilize wakwe,

7 zingholo, ne zing'ombe sunga

ne vilimu vyose vya mu mbago.

8 Zindeje ne zisomba, sunga

ne viwumbwa vyose vya mwi'lenga,

9 Gwe Mulungu go Muwaha wetu,

litagwa lyako nha licizize kononze kose!


Zabuli 9

Lilumbo kuli Mulungu so ling'ani yakwe

(Kuli mwimbizi: Nho wimbe wa Muti-labeni Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe Mulungu go Muwaha wetu,

ane nokukumya gwegwe ko mutima wangu wose,

kanyhi nokwanza ze metumo gako go wizine.

2 Nane nosecelela no kusangalala ku soko yako,

nokwimba zo lukumyo lwako,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu Mono Wulawiliciye Muli Vyose.

3 I walugu wangu mwali mweza kwidoma nawo,

wakawuya ne nyuma, wakagwa,

no kwajililila lukulu.

4 Kunhetela no kumhela ling'ani yangu,

wikaye mwigoda lyako lyo wutemi,

wukuling'aniza kwe ling'ani,

5 Nagwe kuwatacila we makungugo,

kuwongomeza i wanhu wabi,

kuwapatila lukulu litagwa lyawo cibitilila.

6 I walugu wongomela, wajililila lukulu.

Nagwe kugamwagula ne makaya gawo,

nawo wakondya kusemelwa lukulu.

7 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yatemile cibitilila,

yaliwicile ligoda lyakwe lyo wutemi,

ho yasume kuling'aniza.

8 Nayo yakuyiling'aniza yinze kwe ling'ani,

kanyhi yakugaling'aniza makungugo mu wulunji.

9 Muwaha Mulungu, wo wutizizo kuli munhu,

mono yakwiwonelwa,

kanyhi ho hanhu hono hapolile,

mu vilo vyo wugaye.

10 Nawo wose wono walivumilaga litagwa lyako,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wawo wo wakwitumbilaga gwegwe,

hambi 'gwe nha si kumutagaga yaye,

u munhu wo wose mono yakukulondola gwegwe.

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yo yayitemile Sayuni,

holeka mumwimbilaje nyimbo ze lilumbo,

kanyhi muwanzizaje wanhu wose ze metumo gakwe.

12 U Mulungu kawakumbucilaga wose wono wakugazwa,

kanyhi hamba si kacisemwaga cililo ca wono wakwiwonelwa.

13 Wumbonele lusungu 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Lanje ta wugaye wono nghugazwa na wono wasulile,

wumbuse mu wulawa mu vilungo vye lifwa,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

14 Ho simulile zo lukumyo lwako,

hali wanhu wa Yelusalemi,

kanyhi ho sangalale vyono wakondya kumhonya.

15 I wanhu we makungugo wahimba likombo,

ninga kanyhi wagwila weneco mumo,

wegwiza mu mutego wono wawutejile weneco.

16 U Muwaha Mulungu kelajila mu ning'aniza yakwe ye ling'ani,

Ne wanhu wabi wegwiza weneco mu metumo gawo mabi.

17 I wabi wose wobita ku yisi ye wafu,

heya, wanhu wose wono wakumubeza Mulungu.

18 Langa u munhu muciwa, hamba si kosemwelwa lukulu,

u witumbilo wo muciwa,

hamba si wokwajililila lukulu.

19 Ale, wuze, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Wulece kumuleceleza munhu ho yewone nha yasuma.

Wugakongholezezaje makungugo

hali gwegwe, wugaling'anize.

20 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuwogofyaje makungugo,

ho wamanye vyono wene, wanhu du.


Zabuli 10

Wulombe wo munhu mono yakwiwonelwa

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, so cici wuli kutali?

Nhawule wukwihundika lono cili mu wugaye?

2 I wabi wakwidunhumula no kuwatyatilila i waciwa.

Ninga 'gwe lece, mitego yawo yiwagwize weneco.

3 U munhu mubi kedunhumulilaga mu masulumizi gakwe mabi,

nayo we masulumizi,

kamukanaga Muwaha Mulungu no kumubeza.

4 U munhu mubi, mu wulawilila mu widunhumuzi wakwe,

hamba si kamucelelelaga Mulungu,

ne mahweso gakwe gose katezaga,

“Hasina Mulungu.”

5 U munhu mubi katugaga cambuzi nhondo zose,

hamba si kayimanyaga ne ning'aniza yo Mulungu,

kanyhi kawabedegalaga i walugu wakwe wose.

6 Nayo kehwesaga mu nhumbula yakwe,

“Ane si nokwihwelela cinji,

hamba sinotuga magayo mu mikalile yangu.”

7 Mulomo wakwe wumemile wulijite no wukonji mupelu,

ku wulonga makani mabi na ge cikomaza.

8 Yendile kwihundika mu vikaya,

ho yawasepe no kuwawulaga cinyele,

wanhu wono wasina yisutya lyolyose.

Kawasepaga wanhu wono wasoceye,

9 kawazumhilaga nhinicizo ku wulawa mumo,

mu wihundiko wakwe nha simba.

Katejelaga ho yawebate waciwa,

nayo kawagwizaga ko mutego wakwe, kawa kuwasola.

10 Kamuzumhilaga musocele no kumugwisa hasi,

yakugwiswa ko wukali wo munhu mubi.

11 Nayo munhu mubi kehwesaga mu nhumbula yakwe yakutya,

“U Mulungu si kacelelelaga cinji,

yafufunize, hamba si yakumbona yaye.”

12 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuze wuwalipicize,

kanyhi wulece kuwasemwa wono wali mu wugaye.

13 Gwe go Mulungu,

alu so cici u munhu mubi yakukubedegala,

no kutya, “Hamba si kosuma kundipiciza yaye?”

14 Na kumbe agwe ho kuwawonaga wono,

wali mu wugaye hamonga ne magaziko,

nagwe wiwikanize nhondo zose kuwataza.

Nayo mono yasoceye kakwitumbilaga gwegwe,

nayo mwana mono yasina walezi katugaga wutazi kuli'gwe.

15 Alu wuvibenanjizaje vilungo vye wabi,

wuwudumyaje wubi wose ho wulece kwijela kanyhi.

16 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yo mutemi,

cibitilila ne cibitilila!

Ne makungugo gono si gamumanyile mwene,

gokwajililila lukulu,

hamba kwijela kanyhi mu yisi yakwe.

17 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kubocelaga cililo co musocele,

kumuhulicizaga no kumusikamya mu nhumbula yakwe.

18 Kowataza i wana wono wasina walezi,

na wono wakwiwonelwa,

munhu we nghuli ho yalece kuwogofya kanyhi.


Zabuli 11

Kumwitumbila Muwaha Mulungu

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 U Muwaha Mulungu wo wutizizo wangu

Alu anye mosuma nhawule kunyitya,

“Ale wuguluce nha ndeje sunga ko kwi'tunda?

2 Hambi wulanje ta i wabi

vyono wakuwukunga wupinde,

wakondya kutawika zisonga mu lukunzi lwo wupinde,

ho wawagume i wanhu waswanu mu wutitu.

3 Alu hono musinji wuhodangwe,

u munhu muswanu ho kotenda nhawule?”

4 U Muwaha Mulungu,

yali mu Nyumba yakwe yono Yelile,

ne ligoda lyo wutemi lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

lili ku wulanga.

Meso gakwe gakuwalanga wanhu wose,

nayo yazimanyile zose zono wakwituma.

5 Muwaha Mulungu kawapimaga we ling'ani ne wabi,

nayo kawasulaga muno muno i wanhu wakali.

6 Nayo kowalagaziza i wabi,

makala go moto ne cibiliti,

ne mbeho nghali muno muno,

yowa co cilipicizo cawo.

7 U Muwaha Mulungu, mwene yo we ling'ani,

nayo yendile ling'ani,

ne wanhu wono wakwituma ze ling'ani,

wawo wo wokwikalana nayo.


Zabuli 12

Kulomba wutazi

(Kuli mwimbizi: Nho wimbe wa Sheminithi. Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Ale, wucitaze, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Hambi wono wakogopile gwegwe nha wamalika,

i wehuwilwa wose nha wakondya kwajililila lukulu.

2 Wose welonjelaga nghani za wulambi du,

ne zinghani zawo zose za wiyendezi du kanyhi za wukoci.

3 Gwe Muwaha, wuyidindaje milomo yiyo yo wulambi,

wuzinyamazye zinimi zizo zono zilongaga zo widunhumuzi.

4 Wakutya, “Kwe milomo yetu, cina wusume,

nase colonga nha vyono cendile nani 'mbi mono kocisuma?”

5 Ninga u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Ku soko vyono wasocele wakwiwonelwa,

ne waciwa wakung'ung'ulika, alu neza kuza,

nane nowapela wuwo mulamu wono wakuwulanjilila.”

6 Wihuwizo wo Muwaha Mulungu wa mbeka,

kanyhi welile,

winha fweza yono yilenguywe mu moto,

winha fweza yono yilenguywe nghanghulo mupunghati.

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wucikamisaje,

no kucicitila kuli lulelo lulu.

8 I wabi wakupilima hanhu hose,

no wajilile wukulumbizwa muli wanhu.


Zabuli 13

Kulomba wutazi

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kosemwa 'ne lo sunga nili?

2 Kokwihundika hali'ne lo sunga nili?

Nokwikala no wugazice mu mutima wangu sunga nili lo?

Nokwikala no wukalale vilo vyose,

mu nhumbula yangu sunga nili lo?

I walugu wangu wajendelele kuwusangalalila wugazice wangu,

sunga nili lo?

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wangu, wunangaje,

wumhitule wunyenzelule meso gangu,

ho ndece kugona nhongo zo kufwa.

4 Wulece kulumbila lukulu i walugu wangu wutuje

cilejeho co kutya, “Langa ta, camusuma ayuno!”

Wulece kuwalecela cilejeho,

ho walece kuwusangalalila wugwe wangu.

5 Ninga ane nilwitumbiye lusungu lwako,

ne nhumbula yangu yiyisangalalile mhonelo yako.

6 Nane nokwimbila gwegwe du go Muwaha Mulungu,

ko so wuswanu wako wa kuli'ne.


Zabuli 14

U wubi we wanhu

(Zab 53)

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 I walele welonjelaga caka wakutya,

“Hasina Mulungu.”

Wose wahodanji, wetuma zinghani ze cikomaza,

holeka hasina hamba monga mono yakwituma ziswanu.

2 U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwasunjilila wanhu,

ku wulawa ku wulanga,

ho yawone hono ho yuli yunji we mahala,

hono ho yuli munhu mono yakumulondola Mulungu.

3 Wose wapatuka, wose watipiye,

hasina mono ketumaga ziswanu, hasina hamba monga.

4 Alu Mulungu nayo yakutya, “Mwene wawo wono

wakwituma makani wubi, alo wasina wizuce lo?

Wono wakuwahiziza wanhu wangu,

hamba si wakuhemba ane yaye.”

5 Baho 'lu langa, wocicima ko woga,

hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yali hamonga na wose wono wali ling'ani.

6 I wanhu wabi, wahodangaga,

wuhile wo munhu muciwa, ninga Muwaha Mulungu,

wo wutizizo wakwe.

7 Yali yiwe ciba le,

hono mhonelo ya Isilayeli yilawe ku Sayuni!

Wolowa wusangalale wuwaha kuli Isilayeli,

kolo Muwaha Mulungu yakuwagomolela

wumoteswe wawo kanyhi,

i wanhu wa Yakobo wosangalala,

wanhu wa Isilayeli wowona nyemo.


Zabuli 15

Mikalile yo munhu yono yikumwendeza Mulungu

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nani 'mbi mono kosuma kwikala mwi'hema lyako?

Hamba nani mono kosuma kwikala mwi'tunda lyako,

lyono lyelile?

2 Yo munhu du mono yasina wuhoswe,

mono ketumaga ze ling'ani nhondo zose,

mono kalongaga ze mbeka kulawa mu nhumbula yakwe.

3 Munhu mono si muheha wanhu.

Kanyhi yo munhu mono si kanozezaga muyagwe vibi,

hamba kumuvila muyagwe makani go wulambi.

4 Kanyhi munhu yuyu kawabedegalaga wose,

wono Mulungu yakuwalema,

langa kawapelaga cogopo wose wono,

wamogopilo Mulungu.

Nayo kawufisilizaga wihuwizo wakwe,

hamba wuwe wukumuletela wambuce.

5 Kanyhi mwene kakopeshaga hamba kudaya cambuzi yaye,

hamba si kabocelaga mafupo ho yamutamise munhu,

mono yasina wuhoswe, no munhu we liwala lilino,

kowa yakamile cibitilila.


Zabuli 16

Wulombe wo witumbilo

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Wunghamisaje 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghwihundika muli gwegwe.

2 Nimulonjela u Muwaha Mulungu,

“Agwe yo gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu wangu,

sina cinji ciswanu, langa hono si gwegwe du.”

3 Kuli ze milungu yo wulambi yono yili mononze,

ane nehwesaga vyono yili vilungo!

4 Ninga wono wakuyihemba milungu yinji,

wakwiyiletela weneco wugaye wuwaha.

Nane sinokwiyinjiziza lukulu mu makumbiko gawo.

Hamba ne milomo yangu siyotambulila lukulu,

matagwa ge milungu yiyo.

5 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ane nghuhaguye gwegwe du,

yo gwegwe du mono kumhelaga visakwa vyangu vyose,

no wumi wangu wuli mu miwoko yako.

6 Wumhelile ciwagwa ciswanu muno muno, heya,

na vyose vyono kumhelaga nha viswanu vicizize.

7 Nane nghumukumya u Muwaha Mulungu,

mono kanhongaga sunga na mu cilo,

ciselinji cangu cikuwa kunyijiza.

8 Holeka nane nghumuwika Muwaha Mulungu,

hamwande yangu nhondo zose,

nayo vyono yali hamonga nane,

holeka hamba sinosinjisika yaye.

9 Holeka nosangalala no kusecelela,

kwe nhumbula yangu yose.

Nane nokwikala mholile muno.

10 Hambi nha wunyhitiye ne vilungo vye lifwa,

hamba sikomuleka mono yelile wako yawolele mu citenjelo.

11 Agwe kondajila nzila yono yibitile ku wumi,

hali'gwe yimemile nyemo,

na mu muwoko wako mo mozili ziswanu ze cibitilila.


Zabuli 17

Wulombe wo munhu muswanu

(Ulombe wo Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuhulicize cililo cangu ce mbeka,

ubocele wulombe wangu wa mu nhumbula,

wono wusina wulambi.

2 Yo gwegwe mono kunhetelaga,

agwe kuwonaga ze ling'ani.

3 Agwe wuyimanyiye lukulu nhumbula yangu kunyiziza ne cilo,

kunyihenga hamba kuwona cinji cibi muli'ne.

4 Kuli ze metumo ge wanhu, ane sinongaga zimbi,

nha vyono wanozaga wanji,

nilibate lilajilizo lyako,

singhutyatya nzila ze wawulaji.

5 Nhyatyaga nzila yako nhondo zose,

nghali si ninayileka hamba hadodo.

6 Nane nghukucemeleza, 'gwe go Mulungu,

hambi nha kuhulicizaga, holeka wuteje likutu lyako,

wuzihulicize zinghani zangu.

7 Wuwulajilaje lusungu lwako, wuwakomboze kuli

walugu wawo, wose wono wakutiziza kuli'gwe.

8 Wunghamisaje, nha vyono kuyikamisaga nenje ye liso lyako,

wuhundice mu mubeho we mabawa gako,

9 ho niwe kutali no wihomi we wabi i walugu wamhilime,

10 wasina lusungu hamba hadodo,

wamemile nghani zo widunhumuzi.

11 Alu wanhejile wamhilime kose kose wakuhila wangwise hasi.

12 Wewikanize wahawanje nha simba,

nha vyono nyana ye simba yisepaga mhelembo yayo.

13 Wuze, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu mwihome nawo,

wuwagwise hasi wumhonyaje kuli wanhu wabi ko mumaje wako.

14 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumhonyaje no muwoko wako kuli wanhu wawo,

i wanhu wono walangaga za mu sawo ya mononze yiyi du.

Wuwalipicizaje kwe magayo gono wawawicila, gawe

gawakwiliye ne wana wawo,

wawalecele sunga ne wezukulu zawo.

15 Ninga ane nokuwona, hambi nha sina wusutye,

nane kolo neza kwinuka,

nowa nina nyemo ku soko yo kukuwona.


Zabuli 18

Lwimbo lwa Dawudi lwo wusume

(2Sa 22:1-51)

1 Nghwendile, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wangu,

yo gwegwe go Vilungo vyangu!

2 Muwaha Mulungu, gwegwe wo wutizizo wangu,

ne ligomawakali lyangu.

Yo gwegwe Mulungu wangu, ne litalawe lyangu,

nghutiziza kuli mwene ho niwe mholile,

mwene yo ngula yangu,

yo Mukombozi wangu mono yali vilungo.

3 Nane nghumulilila Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yanojeye lukumyo, yanhaze,

nane nghuponywa kuli walugu wangu.

4 Lifwa lyali limhilime mbavu zose,

ne mawingo go wongomele gali gangwiliye,

5 ne zimbali ze lifwa, zali zimhilime,

vidoga vye mitego ye citenjelo, vyali vinanjile.

6 Nghamulilila Muwaha Mulungu mu wugaye,

nghamucemeleza Mulungu wangu yanhaze.

Nayo yakalihulika lizi lyangu mu Nyumba yakwe,

cililo cangu cikamuficila mu makutu gakwe.

7 Baho yisi yikasinjisika no kucicima,

ne misinji ye matunda nayo yikayumbayumba,

vyono Mulungu yali yakolwa likuwo.

8 Lyosi likakomoka kulawa mu zimhula zakwe,

no moto mukali wukalawila mu mulomo wakwe,

ne makala go moto gakalipuka kulawa kuli mwene.

9 Yakawudigula wulanga,

yakatima hono hasi mwi'vunde lititu.

10 Yakagulucila mucanyha ye likelubi,

yakajendela mumo mu mabawa ge mbeho musunho.

11 Yakegubika citi mbavu zose,

ne mavunde mazito ge malenga gakamupilima.

12 I mamuli gakamulika,

ha mwande yakwe ne mvula ye mabwe,

hamonga ne malambi go moto,

vyose vikalawila kulawa mwi'vunde,

13 Muwaha Mulungu yakadidima kulawa ku wulanga,

ne lizi lya Mono Yalawiliciye likahulikwa.

Yikatonya mvula ya mabwe ne makala go moto.

14 Yakawaguma walugu wakwe kwe zisonga zakwe,

yakaweyagaza, yakawamulisiza limuli, yakawawinga.

15 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

lono wawatacilila walugu wako,

wali weza kuwogofya kwe likuwo lyako,

ivisolo vye lilenga vyagubulwa, ne misinji ye yinze,

nayo yikejela calinze.

16 U Mulungu yakagolola muwoko wakwe,

ku wulawa ku wulanga,

yakanyibata yakanimula mu wulawa mu malenga menji.

17 Yakamhonya kuli walugu wangu,

wono wali vilungo muno,

yakamhonya kuli walya wono wasulile,

hambi nha wali wanyhizize vilungo 'ne.

18 Wahungucila lono nali nili mu wugaye,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

Muwaha Mulungu yanyhitila kuli wene.

19 Yanimula, yakambika hono hapolile,

yanhaza vyono yali yendezwe nane.

20 Muwaha Mulungu yamhela cihumbilizwa,

vyono 'ne netuma zo muling'ano

yahumbiliza vyono nali sina wuhoswe.

21 Ane niyityatyaga nzila yo Muwaha Mulungu du,

hamba nghali namuleka Mulungu wangu.

22 Nigebate malajilizo gakwe gose,

nghali si ninaleka kuwutyatya wutonji wakwe.

23 Sina wusutye hali mwene,

nghwilanjilila ho ndece kusutya.

24 Muwaha Mulungu yakumhela cihumbilizwa ku soko,

vyono nghwituma ziswanu,

mwene yamanyile vyono ane sina wuhoswe.

25 Agwe wuli mwihuwilwa kuli wono wehuwilwa,

kanyhi wuli muswanu kuli wanhu waswanu.

26 Agwe nha wukwilaniciye kuli wono wakwilaniciye,

ninga wuli mukali kuli wanhu wabi.

27 Agwe kuwaponyaga wenenenyhezi,

no kuwagwisa wedunhumuzi.

28 Gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go Mulungu wangu, yo gwegwe mono kumulicilaga,

yo gwegwe mono kumbusizaga citi cangu.

29 Ko wutazi wako, nghuwahoma walugu wangu,

kumhelaga vilungo vyo kuzumhila lijinji.

30 Mbeka metumo gakwe gasina wuhoswe!

No wihuwizo wo Muwaha Mulungu wa mbeka,

mwene yo ngula ya wose wono wamwitumbiye.

31 Alu nani 'mbi kanyhi Mulungu, hamba nani 'mbi,

kanyhi mono lye 'talawe lyo wutizizo,

hono si gwegwe du go Mulungu wetu?

32 U Mulungu yo kamhelaga vilungo,

yo kayinozaga nzila yangu yiwa yipolile.

33 Kayinozaga migulu yangu yiwa yikamile nha ye mbala,

kawa kunyimya nghamile mucanyha ye litunda.

34 Kanyijizaga zinzila zo kwihoma ne wulugu,

miwoko yangu yisume kuwukwega wupinde we shaba.

35 Kumhela ngula yako yo kumhonezya,

no muwoko wako wo kulume wunyibate.

No wutazi wako, wukumbilusa.

36 Kumbusizaga vimizi vya mu nzila,

nane nghali sinagwa hamba hadodo.

37 Nane nghuwatyatilila i walugu wangu no kuwebata,

hamba sinowuyila lukulu ne nyuma,

sunga niwongomeze.

38 Nane nghuwagwisa hasi hamba siwokwinuka kanyhi,

wakugwa hasi yangu, wasumwa.

39 Agwe yo gwegwe mono wukumhela vilungo,

vyo kwihomela ne walugu,

kuwagwisaga walugu hasi yangu.

40 Wawanoza walugu wangu watize,

na awalya wono wasulile, nghawongomeza.

41 Wazoza izozo yiwaha,

ninga hakawa hasina hamba yumonga wo kuwaponya,

wamulilila Muwaha Mulungu,

ninga nayo hamba kuwahuliciza yaye.

42 Nane nghawatindya wakawa nhe nghundi,

lyono likupemheluswa ne mbeho,

nghawa kuwagumila kunze nhe madabaga mu nzila.

43 Agwe kumhonya kuli wabedegazi,

no kunoza niwe mutemi kuli makungugo.

Ne wanhu wono si niwamanyile wo wonyitumamila.

44 Ne wajenzi wonyiziza kwe cogopo,

nawo wohulika kamonga du,

kolo wakuhulika i zangu.

45 Wajenzi nawo, wusugu wawo womalika,

wolawila wakucicima mu magomawakali gawo,

no kunyiziza ko woga wuwaha.

46 U Muwaha Mulungu yo mumi!

Yalumbwe, u mudimi no wihundiko wangu.

Ale, mumukumyaje Mulungu mono yakumhonya.

47 Mwene kamhelaga wusume kuli walugu wangu,

yakugawika makungugo hasi yangu.

48 Yakumhonya kuli walugu wangu,

yakumhela wusume kuli wono wakumhingamhinga,

yakunyhitila kuli watamisi.

49 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane nokulumbila haciligati ye wanhu we makungugo,

nokwimbila zinyimbo zo kukukumya.

50 U Mulungu kamupelaga mutemi wakwe cisumisumi ciwaha,

kamulajilaga u muhagulwa wakwe wuswanu wakwe.

Heya, wendo kuli Dawudi,

hamonga no lulelo lwakwe sunga ku cibitilila.


Zabuli 19

Lukumyo lwo Mulungu kuli viwumbwa

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Wulanga wukulajila lukumyo lwo Mulungu,

no wulanga nawo wukwanza i milimo ye miwoko yakwe.

2 Misi nayo yikuyilonjela i misi ye mitondo,

cilo cikucimanyiza cilo cono cikutyatya,

3 Ninga hasina wulonje hamba nghani zono zikwitumamilwa,

hamba lizi lyono likuhulikwa.

4 Hamba nhavivyo,

lizi lyazo litandaye mononze mose,

ne zinghani zayo zificile sunga ko ku mhelo ye yinze.

U Mulungu yaliwiciye lizuwa wikalo walyo mu mwanya,

5 nalyo lilawilaga kunze lihwanile bwana halusi,

lihwanile i citung'ati mono yewikanize ku wisumanize.

6 Nalyo lisosomocelaga lubavu lumonga,

no kupilima sunga ku lubavu luyagwe,

na hasina cono cosuma kwicita ne livuce lyayo.

Lilajilizo lyo Mulungu

7 I lilajilizo lyo Muwaha Mulungu likwilaniciye,

nalyo limupelaga munhu vilungo.

Nago malajilizo go Muwaha Mulungu gakamile,

gawapelaga mahala i wenenenyhezi.

8 U wutonji wo Muwaha Mulungu, wo wulunji,

wusangalazaga nhumbula, lilajilizo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

lyo lye ling'ani, limwijizaga munhu.

9 U wogopo Muwaha Mulungu, yo nghani yono yelile,

cogopo co kumogopa Muwaha Mulungu cikalaga cibitilila,

ne malajilizo go Muwaha Mulungu, go go wulunji,

gose gali ling'ani.

10 Maswanu muno si nha zahabu,

gali muzinzi si nho wuci muswanu muno muno.

11 Gakunyijiza ane no mwitumami wako, vyono,

hono nghugatyatya,

gakundetela wuhumbilizwe wuwaha.

12 Ninga 'lu nani 'mbi,

mono kewonaga i masutya gakwe mweneco?

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumhonye ne masutya gono sinigamanyile.

13 Wunyhitilaje no mwitumami wako,

kuli masutya go kusutya ne nyono,

wulecele lukulu kuvumila gago gateme muli'ne.

Na baho ho nowela nghwilaniciye,

hamba si nowa no wusutye wuwaha yaye.

14 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nghukulomba zinghani zangu,

hamonga ne mahweso gangu, vikwendeze gwegwe,

mono wuli 'talawe lyo wutizizo wangu,

kanyhi go Mukombozi wangu!


Zabuli 20

Kulomba wusume

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 U Mulungu yakuhulicize lono wuli mu wugaye,

i litagwa lyo Mulungu wa Yakobo,

lyeze wo wutizizo wako.

2 Yakuletele wutazi ku wulawa hono helile hakwe,

yakusikamye kulawa mwi'tunda lya Sayuni.

3 Yagakumbucile makumbiko gako gose,

yalibocele ne likumbiko lyako linyanywe.

4 Yakupele vyono wukuhila mu nhumbula yako,

yagafisilize i mahweso gako gose.

5 Baho, nase ho cosecelela ku soko yo wusume wako,

cozinula zibendela ko kulikumya litagwa lyo Mulungu wetu.

U Muwaha Mulungu yakupelaje visakwa vyako vyose!

6 Alu nakondya kumanya vyono Muwaha Mulungu,

kamupelaga wusume u mutemi muhagulwa wakwe,

kanyhi kamuhulicizaga ku wulawa hono helile hakwe ku wulanga,

kanyhi kamupelaga wusume ko muwoko wakwe we vilungo.

7 Wanji wetumbilaga mituka yo wulugu,

wanji nawo wetumbilaga zifwalasi zawo.

Ninga ase, cimwitumbiye u Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wetu.

8 Wene wawo wekobalaga no kugwa hasi,

ninga ase cikwinuka no kwima wima.

9 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumupelaje mutemi wusume,

kanyhi wucihulicize lono cikukulomba.


Zabuli 21

Wulumbe ku soko yo wusume

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

U Mutemi yakusangalala ku soko ye vilungo vyono wumupeye,

yakusecelela ku soko yo wusume wono wumupelile.

2 Kumufisiliziza wuhile wa mu nhumbula yakwe,

hamba si wumwimile cisakwa cakwe.

3 Kumuziza kuwa kumupela wulugute no wumoteswe,

kumuvwika mu mutwe mukumbo we zahabu yono yilenguce.

4 Yakakulomba wumi, nagwe wukamupela vilo vinji,

vyo wikale cibitilila.

5 Yena lukumyo ku soko yo wutazi wako,

yo gwegwe mono wumupelile lilumbo no wogopwe.

6 Wukumunoza yawe mulugutwe cibitilila,

u wijele wako, wukumumemya nyemo.

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

mutemi yakwitumbiye gwegwe,

nayo kowa yapolile so lusungu lwako,

go Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye Muli Vyose.

8 U muwoko wako wowaficila i walugu wako wose,

u muwoko wako wo kulume,

wowebata wose wono wakusulile.

9 Nagwe kolo wukuza,

kowaticimiza mwi'kozo lyo moto,

i likuwo lyako go Muwaha Mulungu,

lyowongomeza, no moto wowatakamiza.

10 Lulelo lwawo lopatilwa lukulu mono'nze,

ne wana wawo, si wolosigala mu wanhu.

11 Hamba wanoze wusugu,

hamba wawe no wuhile wubi wo kukutamisa,

awo wowa wibahule wa bwete,

hamba siwosuma kwituma cinji.

12 Hambi agwe nha kowakulumhusa lumangho,

ko kuwalunga ne zisonga zako, mu wusu wawo.

13 Wulumbwe 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko ye vilungo vyako!

Nase cokwimba no kuvikumya i vilungo vyako.


Zabuli 22

Cililo co wusungu no lwimbo lwo lukumyo

1 Mulungu wangu, Mulungu wangu!

So cici wukundeka?

Nhawule wuli kutali ku wunhaze,

wuli kutali ne zinghani ze cililo cangu?

2 Mulungu wangu!

Mu misi nghukulilila ninga hamba si wukuhuliciza,

na ku cilo nako nhavivyo, ninga hamba singhutuga wucehe yaye.

3 Hamba nhavivyo, agwe welile;

mono wanhu wa Isilayeli wakukukumya.

4 Ne walezi wetu wali wakwitumbiye gwegwe,

wali wakwitumbiye, nagwe wukawaponya.

5 Wakulilila lono wali wali mu zimbali, wakaponywa,

wakwitumbila, hamba si wawona cevu.

6 Ninga ane nili ng'onyo du,

hamba si nili munhu yaye,

nghubedegalwa no kuhoswa ne wanhu wose.

7 Wose wono wakumbona, wakunzwajiza du,

wakufyonza no kusinjisa mitwe yawo,

8 Wakutya, “Alu, si wali wumwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu,

Nhawule lu si yakukuponya?

Hono Mulungu yawe yendezwe nagwe, ale yakuponye!”

9 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

yo gwegwe mono wandavyaniza mu'nda ya yaya,

no kundima lono nali nghali kongha.

10 Nghwitumbiye gwegwe ku wulawa wulelwe wangu,

ku wulawilila lono nalelelwa,

gwegwe du yo wuli Mulungu wangu.

11 Wulece kuwa wuli kutali nane,

hambi wugaye nha wasejelela,

holeka weze wuli behi,

hambi nha sina mono konhaza.

12 Walugu wenji wamhilime, wenha zinghambaku,

wambaninyhize, wenha zinghambaku zinghali za Bashani!

13 Wakwahama i milomo yawo nha simba,

wewikanize ho wanhowanje caka caka, no kundemulanga.

14 Ane namalika nhe malenga gono gamwajice,

ne mafupa gangu gose gatenguka,

ne nhumbula yangu yeza nha kotya nha yono yenjeduce.

15 Mumelo wangu wanyala nhe lizaye,

no lulimilwangu lukwinamatila mu zinzagasa zangu,

wandeka fwiye mu manghundi.

16 Wihangulize wa wetuma zo wavu, wumhilime,

wamhilime kose kose kotya litewa lya mabwa,

wanhobola miwoko ne magulu.

17 Alu nasigala mafupa gose du,

ne walugu wangu wananjile akuno wakusimanga.

18 Wakugawana vivwalo vyangu mu nzila yo ko kutowa kula.

19 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kuwa kutali nane.

Agwe go mutazi wangu, wiyohaje wunhaze.

20 Wumhonye no mumaje!

Wumhonye wumi wangu ku wulawa mu mabwa gaga!

21 Wumhonye mwi'lomo lye simba,

wumhonye ku wulawa mu mahembe ge mbogo.

22 Nane nowanziza wandugu zangu ze metumo gako,

nokulumbila muciligati co wihangulize wawo.

23 “Ale, mumupelaje lukumyo,

anye mo wetumami wo Muwaha Mulungu!

Nanye mo lulelo lwa Yakobo,

mumutogozaje Mulungu,

nanye mo wanhu wose wa Isilayeli, mumuhembaje.

24 Hambi mwene nha si kawabedegalaga waciwa mu wusocele wawo,

hamba sikagabedegalaga i magazo gawo,

si kalemaga kuwahuliciza yaye,

langa kawapitulaga lono wakumulomba wutazi.”

25 Gwe go Mulungu, kumhelaga wusume wo kukukumya,

hagati yo wihangulize wuwaha we wanhu,

nane nofisiliza wilahize wangu hali wono wakogopile.

26 I waciwa wolya sunga wegute,

nawo wono wakumulondola u Muwaha Mulungu, womukumya.

Ale, wamoteswe cibitilila!

27 Ne yinze yose yokumbucila,

no kumuwuyila Muwaha Mulungu,

ne zimhuga zose ze makungugo, zomuhemba.

28 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha Mutemi,

nayo yagatemile makungugo gose.

29 Ne wanhu wedunhumuzi wose,

womuhemba mweneco du,

na wose wono si wakusuma kwiwumifya,

hamba wahwele, womuhemba mweneco du.

30 Ne zindelo zono zikuza, zomwitumamila,

ne wanhu nawo, woziganula zinghani zo Muwaha Mulungu,

kuli zindelo zono zikuza,

31 Sunga ne wanhu wono wakali nalelwa,

nawo wolololonjelwa nhavi,

“U Muwaha Mulungu yo yawaponya wanhu wakwe!”


Zabuli 23

U Mulungu yo mudimi wangu

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yo mudimi wangu,

holeka sinocepucilwa na cinji.

2 Kanyhehezaga mu wudimilo we mahanze madece,

kanhongaga ku vilamba vyono

visunjile vye malenga maswanu,

3 no kumhela vilungo vipya.

Kanyhi kanhongaga mu nzila yo mugoloka,

nha vyono yehuwiza.

4 Holeka hamba nghole mwi'lolo lyo mwenzenze

we lifwa, sinokogopa cinji, hambi 'gwe,

agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha wuli hamonga nane, ne nghome yako,

hamonga ne shale yako, vikundima.

5 Wukunhenjela ndigwa hamwande ye walugu wangu,

wukumbaka ne mafuta mu mutwe wangu,

sunga ne cikombe cangu wukucimemya twaa no mwitiko.

6 U wuswanu wako hamonga no lusungu lwako, mbeka,

vyowa hamonga nane mu vilo vyose vyo wikale wangu.

Nane nokwikala mu nyumba yo Muwaha Mulungu cibitilila.


Zabuli 24

U Mutemi Mono Yalawiliciye

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Yinze hamonga na vyose vyono vili mugati yayo,

yo sawo yo Muwaha Mulungu,

yinze hamonga na wose wono wekaye mumo, yo sawo yakwe.

2 Hambi mwene nha yo yayizenga yinze mu cisolo ce lilenga,

yakayisimya mu makolongo ge malenga.

3 Alo nani 'mbi mono kosuma kubita mucanyha,

ye litunda lyo Muwaha Mulungu?

Hamba nani mono kosuma kwikala hanhu hakwe hono helile?

4 Yo munhu mono yasina wuhoswe,

ne nhumbula yono yelile.

Mono si yakwiyinjiza mu makani ge caka caka.

Hamba kuhomelela ko wulambi.

5 Mono yena lilumbo linha lilyo,

yuyo yo kotuga wulugute ko Muwaha Mulungu,

Kotuga ling'ani yakwe kuli Mulungu mono kamuponyaga.

6 Wawo wo wanhu wono wakumulondola Mulungu,

wo wanhu wono wakumulondola Mulungu wa Yakobo.

7 Ale, Nyo milango digucenyi,

digucenyi 'nye nyo milango ye katali,

u Mutemi we nhojo ho yenjile.

8 Alo yuyo yo nani lo u Mutemi we nhojo?

Yuyo yo Muwaha Mulungu, we vilungo no Wusume.

U Muwaha Mulungu, citung'ati mu milewo.

9 Digucenyi nyo milango

digucenyi 'nye nyo milango ye katali,

u Mutemi we nhojo ho yenjile.

10 Alo yuyo yo nani lo u Mutemi we nhojo?

Yuyo yo Muwaha Mulungu, we mihanga,

Mwene yuyo yo Mutemi we nhojo.


Zabuli 25

Kulomba wutonji no wudimi

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukulomba kwe nhumbula yangu yose!

2 Ane nghwitumbiye gwegwe, go Muwaha Mulungu wangu,

wulece kundeka nghozwe cevu,

i walugu wangu walece kuwusangalalila wusumwe wangu.

3 Na wose wono wakwitumbiye gwegwe, si wokozwa cevu yaye.

Langa wono wakubedegaye wokozwa cevu.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumanyize nzila yako, heya,

wumhelaje wutonji wako.

5 Wunhongaje mu mbeka yako no kunyijiza,

hambi nha yo gwegwe du go Mulungu mono kumhonyaga,

nane nghwitumbiye gwegwe du vilo vyose.

6 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wukumbucile zo wuwaha wo wuswanu wako,

wulukumbucilaje no lusungu lwako,

hambi nha vikalaga cibitilila na cibitilila.

7 Wulece kuwukumbucila wubi wo wuzelelo wangu,

wunenelaje ne masutya gangu,

wunghumbucilaje so lusungu lwako,

ko wuswanu wako 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

8 Muwaha Mulungu yo muswanu, kanyhi yali ling'ani,

mwene kawejizaga wahodanji zo wutonji.

9 Mwene kawatongaga i wenenenyhezi mu wiling'ano, heya,

kawejizaga wawo inzila yakwe.

10 U Muwaha Mulungu,

kawatongaga ko wihuwilwa na ko wendo,

wose wono webate cilagane cakwe ne malajilizo gakwe.

11 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wunenele masutya gangu gose,

hamba gawe vyono genha ku soko ye lilumbo lye litagwa lyako.

12 Munhu wo wose mono kamogopago Muwaha Mulungu,

yuyo kokwijizwa no Mulungu inzila yo kutyatya.

13 No munhu we liwala lilyo,

yo komoteswa nhondo zose na wo lulelo lwakwe,

woyihala yisi.

14 U Muwaha Mulungu,

yawendile wose wono wakumuhulika,

nayo kawamanyizaga ze cilagane cakwe nhondo zose.

15 Nimulanjililaga Muwaha Mulungu ho yanhaze,

mwene yo kogasopola magulu gangu mu mutego.

16 Hambi 'ne nha nili wene kanyhi soceye,

holeka wunangaje go Muwaha Mulungu wumbonele lusungu.

17 Wumbusize wugazice wa mu nhumbula yangu,

kanyhi wumhonye mu magayo gangu.

18 Wuwulangaje wulungule wangu no wugazwe wangu.

Wunenele wubi wangu wose.

19 Lanje, ta, walugu wangu vyono wenji.

Lanje, ta, vyono wasulile vibi muno.

20 Wuwukamisaje wumi wangu,

wumhonye nghutiziza kwako,

ho ndece kukozwa cevu.

21 U wuswanu wako no wiling'ano wako vinghamise,

hambi 'ne nha nghwitumbiye gwegwe.

22 Gwe Mulungu, wuwaponye wanhu wako Waisilayeli,

wuwaponye mu magayo gawo gose.


Zabuli 26

Wulombe wo munhu muswanu

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wumbalaje'ne sina wubi,

hambi nha nitumaga du ziswanu,

nane nghwitumbiye gwegwe,

hamba sina mahwele yaye.

2 Go Muwaha Mulungu, nghulomba wunjele,

wumhime, wugalanje mahala gangu,

ne nhumbula yangu.

3 Hambi lusungu lwako nha lo lukunongoza nane,

njendelaga mu mbeka yako.

4 Sinikalanaga ne wanhu we caka caka yaye,

hamba sinilunzaga hamonga ne wanhu wakoci.

5 Sinyendile hamba hadodo,

kuhangahanga ne wanhu wono wetumaga zo wavu,

hamba sinokwilunziza lukulu ne wanhu wabi.

6 Ane nghwizuguza miwoko yangu,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ho ndajile vyono ane nili mutelevu,

nghwilunza no wihangulize wo wuhembi,

mu wupilime we cikumbicilo cako,

7 akuno nghwimba no lwimbo lo kukulumbiza gwegwe,

hamonga no kwanza ze metumo gako go wizine.

8 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nyendile wikalo wa mu nyumba yako,

ihanhu hono lwikaye u lukumyo lwako.

9 Wulece kumbaliliza hamonga ne wanyo wubi,

hamba wulece kunhajiliza hamonga ne wanhu wawulaji,

10 i wanhu wono wekalaga wakulya mafupo vilo vyose.

11 Ninga 'ne, nitumaga zo wiling'ano du,

wumbonele lusungu wumhonye.

12 Nimile mholile, kanyhi nghamile,

baho ho nomukumizya Muwaha Mulungu,

mu wihangulize wuwaha we wanhu.


Zabuli 27

Wulombe wo lukumyo

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 U Muwaha Mulungu wo wuzelu wangu,

kanyhi yo mhonelo yangu,

alu kanyhi nimogope nani lo?

U Muwaha Mulungu yo mudimi wangu alu nimogope nani 'mbi?

2 I wanhu wabi hono wasace kuhungucila ho wambulaje,

weneco du, wo wokwikobala no kugwa hasi.

3 Holeka hamba mhilimwe, ne lipuga yiwaha,

ane sinokogopa yaye, hamba wulugu wunyizize,

nojendelela kumwitumbila du Mulungu.

4 Hoyili nghani monga du yono nimulombile Muwaha Mulungu,

nghani monga du yono nghuyilondola, nayo yoyi,

“Nikale mu nyumba yo Muwaha Mulungu,

vilo vyose vyo wikale wangu,

niwuwone wuswanu wo Muwaha Mulungu,

no kuwulondola wutonji wakwe mu Nyumba yakwe.”

5 Na mwi'zuwa lyo wugaye,

mwene kohundika 'ne mu wikalo wakwe,

kanyhi kombika mholile mwi'talawe mucanyha.

6 Nane nowasumiliza i walugu wangu wose wono wamhilime,

nane nomulavizya makumbiko sangalaye mu nyumba yakwe,

nokwimba hamonga no kumusangalalila Muwaha Mulungu.

7 Wuhulice go Muwaha Mulungu lono nghukulilila,

wumbonele lusungu, wumhitule.

8 Nghwihwesa zono walonga wukatya, “Wuze kuli'ne!”

Nane nghukuziza, gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

9 Wulece kwihundika hali'ne.

Hamba wulece kunemela mwi'kuwo ane no mwitumami wako,

agwe nhondo zose, yo gwegwe mono

wuli mutazi wangu holeka wulece kunhaga,

hamba kundeka gwe go Muwaha Mulungu go Muponyi wangu.

10 Wa baba na yaya hamba wandece,

Muwaha Mulungu kojendelela kundima.

11 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wunyijize ze nzila yako,

wunghoze mu nzila yono yigoloce,

hambi walugu wangu nha wenji muno muno.

12 Holeka wulece kundeka, i walugu wangu,

wanhendele nha vyono wendile,

hambi i wahomelezi wo wulambi,

nha wananjile ko wogofye wuwaha.

13 Nane nihuwiye vyono nowuwona wuswanu wo Muwaha Mulungu,

mu wikalo we womi.

14 Wumwitumbile Muwaha Mulungu!

Wuwe ne vilungo, ne nhumbula ye citung'ati!

Heya, wumwitumbile Muwaha Mulungu!


Zabuli 28

Kulomba wutazi

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Nghukulilila gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe gwe 'talawe lyo wutizizo wangu!

Wuhulicizaje hamba wulece kuwa nha si wukuhulika,

ho ndece kwihwana na wono wakubita ku yisi ye wafu.

2 Wuhulicize lono nghukulomba wutazi,

lono nghuyinula miwoko yangu,

niyidomizye hono helile hako.

3 Wulece kuning'aniza hamonga ne wahodanji,

hamonga ne wanhu wetuma zimbi,

iwanhu wono walongaga ziswanu,

ninga mu zinhumbula zawo wamemile lusulo.

4 Wuwalipicize nha vyono genha metumo gawo,

nha vyono genha masutya gono wetumile.

Wuwalipicize mu nghango ye metumo gawo,

wuwatendele nha vyono yiwanojeye.

5 Hambi wene nha si wakugacelelela,

i metumo go Muwaha Mulungu,

hamba kuzimanya kose, zono yetumile.

Holeka ku soko yiyo, nayo kowamwagula,

hamba si kowazenga yaye cibitilila.

6 U lukumyo lweze kuli Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha yawuhulika wulombe wangu.

7 U Muwaha Mulungu vyo vilungo vyangu,

ne ngula yangu,

nane nimwitumbiye mwene kwe nhumbula yangu yose.

U mwene kanhazaga no kusangalaza,

holeka nane nomukumya, no kumwimbila,

zinyimbo zo wusangalale.

8 U Muwaha Mulungu, vyo vilungo vye wanhu wakwe,

nayo kamucitilaga no kumuponya u mutemi wakwe muhagulwa.

9 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuwaponye wanhu wako,

wuwalugute i wanhu wono yo sawo yako,

wuwadime no kuwacitila sunga cibitilila.


Zabuli 29

I lizi lyo Mulungu mu mbeho yo mukukunzo

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Mulavizyenyi lukumyo Muwaha Mulungu,

anye mo viwumbwa vyose vyono vili ku wulanga.

Heya, mumukumyaje no kuwuvumila wuwaha wakwe.

2 Likumyenyi litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

mumuhembe Muwaha Mulungu lono yakwilajila.

3 Lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu likuhulikwa mu malenga,

u Mulungu wo lukumyo yakulavya mabalalusi,

ne lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

likuhulikwa mwi'lenga mucanyha!

4 Lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu lili vilungo,

lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu limemile wuwaha.

5 Lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

libenanjizaga i mibici ye mihagata, heya,

sunga ne mihagata ya ku Lebanoni.

6 Liganozaga i matunda ga Lebanoni gazumhe-zumhe,

kotya nyehe ya ng'ombe, ne matunda ga Helimoni,

nago kotya nyana ye mbogo.

7 Lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu lilavyaga malambi go moto.

8 Lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu lisinjisaga nyika,

u Muwaha Mulungu yakulisinjisa linyika lya Kadeshi.

9 Lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

liyisinjisaga mikambala,

nalyo likung'unhaga mahamha ge mabici mu mbago,

na wono wali mu Nyumba yakwe wakutya,

“Lukumyo lweze kuli Mulungu!”

10 U Muwaha Mulungu yo yatemile lihwazi lye malenga

Muwaha Mulungu yo mutemi, yatemile cibitilila.

11 U Muwaha Mulungu kowapela wanhu wakwe vilungo,

kowalugutiza wanhu wakwe ko mulamu.


Zabuli 30

1 Nghukukumya go Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha wambusa mu mbali,

hamba si wuwaleceleze walugu wangu wasimanje.

2 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wangu,

nakulilila, nagwe wukamhonya.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wakondya kumhonya mu wulawa mu citenjelo,

wakondya kumhonya no kumbusa mu muhinzo,

wo kugwila mugati ye likombo litali.

4 Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu na nyimbo,

anye mo wanhu wakwe wehuwilwa.

Muwukumbucilaje wele wakwe no kumukumya.

5 I likuwo lyakwe nha lili hadodo vivi du,

langa lusungu lwakwe, lwikalaga cibitilila.

I cililo ciwezaga mu cilo du,

ninga hakali mitondo, yiwezaga nyemo.

6 Mu wumote wangu natya,

“Sinosinjisika yaye!”

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko yo lusungu lwako,

wakondya kunghamya nhe'tunda yiwaha.

Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

wukehundika kutali nane,

sunga nane nghawa kwanza kutuga woga.

8 Nghakulilila gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghakunena gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

9 “Alu hono nifwe, agwe kotuga cambuzici?

Alu cowa cambuzici hono ngwile mwikombo?

Hamba manghundi lo, gosuma kukukumya?

Hamba kwanza zo wuswanu wako wuwaha?

10 Hulice go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumbonele lusungu,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wunhazaje!”

11 Gwegwe kuwupitula wukalale wangu,

wuwa wuvina wa nyemo,

kumbusiza wukalale wangu wose,

kumhela nyemo mbaha kuciza.

12 Holeka nane sinonyamala twii, aa ng'o,

langa nojendelela kukwimbila nyimbo zo lukumyo.

Nowa kukulumba cibitilila,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wangu.


Zabuli 31

Wulombe wo kumwitumbilo Mulungu

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Nghutiziza kuli'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

so wutazi wako, ho ndece kukozwa cevu,

wumhonye ko wiling'ano wako.

2 Wuteje likutu lyako, wumhonye mbela!

Wuweze kuli'ne mudimi mono wukamile,

wihundiko wo kumhonya.

3 Heya, hambi agwe nha wuli talawe lyangu,

ne gomawakali lyangu.

Ku soko ye litagwa lyako,

wunongolele, wunhonje.

4 Wundavyanize mu mutego wono wanhejile,

wunyhitile mu mbali.

5 Nghuwuwika muhe wangu mu miwoko yako,

wumhonye 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go Mulungu we mbeka.

6 Nghuwasula wono wakuyihemba milungu yo wudesi,

ninga ane nghwitumbiye gwegwe,

agwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

7 Nosangalala, no kulusecelelela lusungu lwako,

agwe nha kuwuwona wugaye wangu,

kuwumanya no wugazice wangu.

8 Agwe nha si wandeceleza nibatwe ne walugu wangu,

kumbika hanhu hono hapolile.

9 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumbonele lusungu, hambi nha nili mu wugaye,

meso gangu gavimba so wulila muno,

ne vilungo vyangu vyose vyatimba.

10 Hambi wikale wangu nha wukumalicila mu wukalale, heya,

sunga ne myaka yangu nayo, mu cililo.

Nasocela so wugaye sunga ne mafupa gangu gakutama.

11 Sunga ne walugu wangu wose, wakumbedegala, naweza

nha linhu du hali wayangu. Ne wendece wangu,

weza kumbona nili wa kogofya,

nawo lono wakumbona mu nzila, wakunhiza.

12 Nawo wose wakondya kusemwa nha munhu mono yafwiye,

neza nha nili ciya cono citulice.

13 Nghuwahulika walugu wangu wakwipwepwela,

wogofye wumhilime mbavu zose,

wakwihehemeza, ho wambulaje.

14 Ninga ane nghwitumbiye gwegwe,

agwe go Muwaha Mulungu nghutya,

“Agwe yo gwegwe Mulungu wangu!”

15 Nane nhondo zose, nili mu wudimwe wako du,

holeka wumhonye mu miwoko ye walugu wangu,

na muli wawa wono wakungaza.

16 Wunanje ko wuswanu wako no mwitumami wako,

wumhonye ko lusungu lwako.

17 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukulomba wulece kundeka nghozwe cevu,

ninga wuwalekaje i wabi wakozwe cevu,

wajilile kuko ku yisi ye wafu.

18 Wuyidinde milomo ye wanhu wawo walambi,

i wanhu wedunhumuzi kanyhi wewoni,

wono wakuwabedegala i wanhu we ling'ani.

19 Lanje ta, wuswanu wako vyono winji,

wono wuwawiciye wose wono wakogopile!

Kuwanozezaga hali wanhu wose ziswanu,

wose wono wakukuziza.

20 Kuwahundikaga hali'gwe hono hapolile,

wawa wali kutali no wuhile wubi we wanhu.

Kuwahundika hali'gwe wapolile no wuwanji we walugu wawo.

21 U Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!

Hambi mwene nha kandajila zo lusungu lwakwe,

lono nali mhilimwe akuno wakunhowanga.

22 Nane nghawa kogopa,

no kwihwesa vyono wakondya kundeka,

kumbe agwe wali wakondya kucihulika cililo cangu

lono nakulilila wunhaze.

23 Mumwendaje Muwaha Mulungu,

anye mo wanhu wakwe!

U Muwaha Mulungu,

mwene kawadimaga wanhu wose iwehuwilwa.

Ninga i wedunhumuzi, kawalipicizaga

nha vyono yiwanojeye kulipicizwa.

24 Muweze muli vilungo no kuwa

ne nhumbula yono yisina woga,

anye wose mono mumwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu.


Zabuli 32

Wunene no wunenelwe

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Mumoti munhu mono yaneneywe lisutya lyakwe,

munhu mono wubi wakwe wapatilwa lukulu.

2 Yo mumoti munhu mono u Muwaha Mulungu si yakumuwalila,

hamba kugakumbucila masutya gakwe,

u munhu mono yasina wukonji wo wose mu nhumbula yakwe.

3 Lono nali nghali si nanena wubi wangu, mbeka,

nali soceye so wulila misi yose.

4 Wali kundipiciza ku misi na ku cilo,

sunga ne vilungo vyangu vikawa kuwuka muli'ne,

nane nghawa kunyala nha vyono muhangha wunyalaga

mu cibahu, lono mvula yawuka.

5 Nghawa kuwuvumila wubi wangu hali'gwe,

hamba sinawuhundika wavu wangu.

Nghawona viswanu, mvumile wubi wangu hali'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nagwe wukawa kunenela wubi wangu wose.

6 Holeka yimunojeye munhu wo wose mono yakogopile,

yakulombe mwi'daha lyo wugaye,

hamba gaze magaziko menji,

ninga si gomuficila yaye.

7 Agwe yo gwegwe go wutizizo wangu,

kunghamisaga mu wugaye wangu.

Nina wusangalale wuwaha

vyono wakondya kumhonya.

8 Mulungu yakutya, “Nokulajila nzila yono kokola,

na kanyhi, nokwijiza no kukutonga.

9 Wulece kuwa mulele nha vyono yinha fwalasi hamba nyumbu,

yono yiwopelezwaga matandiko ne mikowa mu mulomo,

hono wulece kuyinoza nhavivyo, si yokusejelela yaye.”

10 Nawo i wanhu wabi, wotuga magayo menji,

ninga wono wamwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu,

awo wakudimwa ko lusungu lwakwe.

11 Nanye mo wanhu we ling'ani, musecelele,

no kusangalalila i metumo gono yetumile u Muwaha Mulungu,

anye nanye mo wenenenyhezi,

mutowe nyhenze za nyemo.


Zabuli 33

Lwimbo lwo lukumyo

1 Anye mo wanhu we ling'ani,

musangalalilenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

nanye mo wenenenyhezi, mukumyenyi Mulungu.

2 Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu ko wutowa ndonondo,

mwimbilenyi ne lisumbi lye zinghunzi yikumi.

3 Mwimbilenyi lwimbo lupya,

towenyi zing'oma viswanu, no wusangalale.

4 Inghani yo Muwaha Mulungu yo ye mbeka,

ne nyituma yakwe yose, yo nyihuwilwa.

5 U Muwaha Mulungu,

yendile wiling'ano hamonga no wuling'anizi,

ne yinze yose yimemile lusungu lwakwe.

6 U Muwaha Mulungu,

yawuwumba wulanga ko wulajilize wakwe,

ne vinhu vyose vya ku wulanga,

vyawumbilwa mu wahulile wakwe.

7 Yagakongholela hamonga,

malenga ge lilenga ne visolo vye lilenga,

yakavidindila mu nyumba yo wuwiko we viya.

8 Yinze yose yimogope Muwaha Mulungu!

Nanye mo wanhu wose wa mononze,

mumupelaje Muwaha Mulungu lilumbo lyakwe!

9 Hambi mwene nha yalonga du,

ne yinze yikawa kuwumbwa,

mwene yalajiliza du,

na vyose vikawa kwijela.

10 U Muwaha Mulungu kawuwembulaga wuhile we makungugo,

no kugapata i mahweso gawo.

11 Wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu,

wikalaga cibitilila,

no wudumule wakwe we zinghani wikalaga ko ku zindelo zose.

12 Na mbeka, limotile likungugo lyono Mulungu wayo,

yo yuyu u Muwaha Mulungu!

Wamoti wono Mulungu yawahaguye wawe wanhu wakwe!

13 U Muwaha Mulungu kasunjililaga,

kono hasi ku wulawa ku wulanga,

no kuwawona wanhu wose.

14 Nayo, ku wulawilila mwigoda lyakwe lyo wuwaha,

yakuwalanga no kuwawona wanhu wose wono wekaye mononze.

15 Mwene Mulungu yo muwumbi we zinhumbula ze wanhu wose,

holeka mwene yazimanyile zose zono wakwituma.

16 Hasina mutemi mono kekalaga yapolile,

ku soko yo winji we mapuga ge wasilikale wakwe,

wo wulugu gono yena nago,

hamba citung'ati, si kawasumizaga wayagwe,

ku soko ye vilungo vyakwe mweneco.

17 Hamba fwalasi mu wulugu,

si yokwila yeneco kusuma ne vilungo vyayo viwaha,

hamba si vyosuma kumuponya munhu.

18 U Muwaha Mulungu,

kawalanjililaga wose wono wakumuhulika,

wanhu wose wono wetumbiye lusungu lwakwe.

19 Mwene kawaponyaga mwi'fwa no kuwalisa mu nzala.

20 Ase cimwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu,

mwene yo mukamisi wetu,

kanyhi yo mutazi wetu.

21 Nase cisangalaye ku so mwene,

kanyhi cilitumbiye litagwa lyakwe lyono lyelile.

22 Lusungu lwako luweze muli'se 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha vyono nase cikwitumbiye gwegwe.


Zabuli 34

Lukumyo kuli wuswanu wo Mulungu

1 Ane nomukumya Muwaha Mulungu madaha gose,

hamba si nolecela lukulu kulutambula lukumyo lwakwe.

2 Mutima wangu wokwilumbila muli Muwaha Mulungu,

nawo wono wakwiwonelwa, wahulice, wasangalale.

3 Mumukumyaje Muwaha Mulungu hamonga nane,

wose hamonga cilikumye litagwa lyakwe.

4 Nghamulomba Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo yakahuliciza, yakambusiza woga wangu wose.

5 Mumupitucilaje Mulungu,

ho muwe ne nyemo, ho simokolelwa lukulu cevu.

6 Nayo muciwa yamulilila Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo yakamuhuliciza,

yakamuponya mu wugaye wakwe wose.

7 Nayo malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu,

kawadimaga wose wono wamogopile,

kanyhi kawaponyaga mu zimbali.

8 Jezenyi nyo weneco,

muwone vyono Muwaha Mulungu muswanu!

Wamoti wono wakumwitumbila mwene.

9 Ale, mumogopaje Muwaha Mulungu,

anye nyo wanhu wakwe, wose wono wamogopile,

si wanjilaga cinji yaye.

10 Sunga ne zisimba nazo hanji,

si zitugaga co kulya, ninga wono

wamogopilo Muwaha Mulungu, wakwizize vyose.

11 Nzonyi muhulicize anye nyo wadodo,

nomwijizenyi mumogope Muwaha Mulungu.

12 Wule, wendile wuwe na nyemo mu mikalile yako,

wikale vilo vinji mu wumote?

13 Ale, wulecele lukulu kuzilonga zinghani,

zimbi, kanyhi wulecele lukulu kulonga wulambi.

14 Wilegaje no wubi we mawala gose,

witume ziswanu, wulondole mulamu, wuwutyatye.

15 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha kawalanjililaga i wanhu we ling'ani,

no kuwahuliciza cililo cawo.

16 Ninga iwetuma zimbi kawasininghalilaga,

nawo kolo wafwiza,

si wakukumbucilwa kanyhi yaye.

17 I wanhu we ling'ani,

kolo wakumulilila Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo kawahulicizaga,

no kuwaponya mu magayo gawo gose.

18 U Muwaha Mulungu yali behi na wose wono wena mahwele,

kawaponyaga wose wono wasina witumbilo.

19 U munhu we ling'ani, katugaga magazo menji,

ninga Muwaha Mulungu kamuponyaga muli gose.

20 Muwaha Mulungu kamudimaga muno muno,

munhu we ling'ani, na hasina hamba lifupa lyakwe,

limonga lyono lyobeneka.

21 Wubi wowongomeza i wanhu wabi, na wono wawasulile,

i wanhu we ling'ani, wolipicizwa.

22 Muwaha Mulungu kowaponya wanhu wakwe,

na wose wono wakutiziza kuli mwene, si wolipicizwa yaye.


Zabuli 35

Kulomba wutazi

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wisumanizaje nawo wono wakwisumaniza nane,

wulewane nawo wono cikulewana nane.

2 Wibate ngula hamonga ne cigula,

winuce wuze wunhaze.

3 Wusole mugoha ne nhemo yako,

wihome na wono wakunhyatilila.

Wunonjele nha vyono komhonya.

4 Wawo wono wakulondola wambulaje,

wawone cevu, wapuwe.

Nawo wono wena wuhile wubi kuli'ne wakolwe cevu,

wawuye ne nyuma.

5 Wehwane nhe mawaje vyono gapemheluswaga ne mbeho,

lono malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu yakuwawinga!

6 Ne nzila yawo yeze yimemile citi ne cinheleza,

lono malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu yakuwawinga.

7 Hambi nha wali wanhejile du mbula soko,

kanyhi wakahimbila yikweme, akuno ho wasina soko.

8 Holeka lece wongomele wuwagane musunho,

webatwe mu mitego yawo weneco, wagwile mwi'kombo lyawo weneco!

9 Langa baho nane nhondo,

nomusangalila Muwaha Mulungu,

nosangalala ku soko vyono yamhonya.

10 Nane, ko mutima wangu wose nomutya Muwaha Mulungu,

“Agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

mbeka hasina mono yehwanile gwegwe!

Agwe kuwaponyaga i wasocele mu miwoko ya wono wali vilungo,

i waciwa nawo kuwaponyaga mu miwoko ye wahizi.”

11 I wanhu wabi wakuhomelela ane zo wulambi,

wakusitaka kuli zinghani zono ane si nizimanyile yaye.

12 Wene wakundipa zimbi kuli zangu ziswanu,

nane nekala nili mu wuciwa,

13 Ninga wene lono wali watamigwe,

ane nghavwala magunila,

nghulajila vyono nghalaye,

neyigaza ko wuleka kulya ndigwa.

Nghawa kulomba akuno ninamizye mutwe wangu.

14 Nghawa nha nghumulilila mwendece wangu,

hamba ndugu yangu,

nghawa nha munhu mono yakulila ku soko yo wufwe wo nyina.

15 Lono nali nagwa, wene wakawa kusecelela,

wakawa kwikongholela hamonga, wakusimanga,

Ne wanhu wono si niwamanyile, nawo wakanhowanga,

hasina hamba monga mono yasuma kuwemila.

16 I wanhu wono wendile kuwaseka we vilema,

nawo wakanghang'anhilila meno gawo ko lusulo.

17 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kojendelela kuwalanga du sunga nili?

Wumhonyaje ne milewo yawo,

wumhonyaje wumi wangu kuli simba zizo.

18 Nane nokulumbila mu wihangulize wuwaha we wanhu,

nokukumizya mwi'puga yiwaha lye wanhu.

19 Wulece kuwaleceleza wawo walugu wangu,

wajendelele kusimanga,

na wawo wono wakusula 'ne bwete,

ho wawusangalalile wugaye wangu.

20 Sunga ne zinghani zono wakuzilonga,

si nghani zono zikulawa mu wendo yaye,

wakutula zinghani zo wulambi,

kuli wono wendile mulamu.

21 Ati wakusitaka wazozize du wakutya,

“Ale! Ale! Caziwona zono witumile!”

22 Nagwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wakondya kuyiwona nghani yiyo.

Ale, lu, wulece kunyamala,

go Muwaha Mulungu,

kanyhi wulece kwikala kutali nane.

23 Wuze go Muwaha Mulungu wunhetele,

wuze go Mulungu wangu,

wuzilanje, zinghani zangu.

24 Gwe Muwaha Mulungu,

go Mulungu wangu,

yo gwegwe du mono wuli ling'ani,

holeka wuning'anizaje sina wusutye,

kanyhi wulece kuwaleceleza i walugu wangu wasimanje.

25 Wulece kuwaleceleza walonje watye, “Alu he,

si nhavi vyo cali kuhila!”

Hamba lunji watye, “Alu cakondya kumumalila!”

26 Walekaje 'gwe wawo wono wakusecelela,

so wutamiswe wangu,

wasumwe wose, wakolwe cevu.

Na wawo wose wono wakwiwona nha waswanu wanyhizize,

wakolwe cevu, kanyhi wapuwe.

27 Awo langa wono wakusangalala,

ku soko yo wiling'ano wangu,

waweze na nyemo nyinji,

wewuyajize kulonga kali kenji watye,

“U Muwaha Mulungu yo Muwaha muno!

Kendezwaga no wumoteswe wo mwitumami wakwe.”

28 Ale, baho nane langa, ho nokwanziza zo wuswanu wako,

nane nojendelela kuzilonga zo lukumyo lwako, misi yose.


Zabuli 36

U wubi wo wanhu

1 U wubi wulonganaga no munyo wubi,

mu nhumbula yakwe mugati.

Wukutya, uwogopo Mulungu musina muli mwene.

2 Na vyono kekalaga yakwihwesa zo wiwoni wakwe du,

holeka kehwesaga vyono wavu wakwe,

si womanyika no kulijitwa no Mulungu.

3 Mwene kalongaga nghani za wavu zose kanyhi za wulambi,

yaleka ku witumamila mahala no kwituma ziswanu.

4 Na lono yagonile hose,

mwene kehwesaga wituma zimbi du,

sunga ne mijendele yakwe yose,

musina hamba yinji yono swanu, kanyhi,

sikasumaga kwilega ne nghani yo yose yono 'mbi.

U wuswanu wo Mulungu

5 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

lusungu lwako lutandaye sunga ko ku wulanga,

no wihuwilwa wako wuficile sunga ko ku mavunde.

6 Ne ning'aniza yako,

yihwanile visolo vye lilenga.

Agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kuwadimaga wanhu wose hamonga ne vilimu vyose.

7 No lusungu lwako go Muwaha Mulungu, luwaha muno!

Ne wanhu watizizaga mu mubeho we mabawa gako.

8 Nagwe kuwegutyaga no wugoli wa mu nyumba yako,

kuwang'wesaga mwikolongo lyo wuswanu wako.

9 Agwe yo gwegwe mwamulizo wo wumi,

na mu wuzelu wako, nase cikuwona wuzelu.

10 Wujendelele kuwawonela lusungu wose wono wakumanyile,

kanyhi wujendelele kuwa muswanu, kuli we ling'ani.

11 Wulece kulumbila lukulu,

wanhu wedunhumuzi wahungucile,

hamba i wanhu wabi waswe wakumasha.

12 He! Kumbe i wetuma zimbi wakondya kugwa,

wagwiswa hasi, hamba siwokwinucila lukulu.


Zabuli 37

Ndumilizo ye wabi ne waswanu

(Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Wulece kwigaza ku soko ye wanhu wabi,

wulece kuwawonela wivu i wetuma zimbi.

2 Hambi wene nha wokwajilila musunho nhe mahanze,

wonyala nhe vilimwa videce.

3 Wumwitumbilaje Muwaha Mulungu,

witume ziswanu, holeka kokwikala wupolile mononze.

4 Wuyilondolaje nyemo yako kulawa kuli Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo kokupela vyose vyono wukusaka mu nhumbula yako.

5 Wumulecelaje Muwaha Mulungu mikalile yako yose,

wumwitumbilaje, nayo kokutaza.

6 Nayo kowunoza wuswanu wako wung'alale wuwe winha wuzelu,

ne ling'ani yako yiwe yinha lizuwa lya mu misi mugati.

7 Ale, wutule hali Muwaha Mulungu,

wumulindilaje ko wukangalizi,

hamba wulece kwigaza na awalya wono wakumota muli vyawo,

hamba kuli walya wono wakwitujila vinhu mu zinzila zimbi.

8 Wulecele lukulu kukolwa ne likuwo,

hamba kwihangavya, hambi mu wunoza nha vivyo,

nha muletaga wugazice du.

9 Ne wanhu wose i wetuma zimbi,

nha wokongomezwa,

langa wono wamwitumbiyo Muwaha Mulungu,

wo wohala yisi.

10 Hambi nha cikali cidaha cidodo du,

nayo mubi kowa yajililila lukulu,

nagwe komulondola hanhu hono yali yekaye,

hamba si kohawona kanyhi.

11 Ninga i wapolo wo wohala yisi,

kanyhi wo wowusecelela mulamu we mbeka.

12 U munhu mubi, kamwihwesezaga,

i zimbi du u munhu we ling'ani,

no kumwikang'anhilila meno ko lusulo.

13 Ninga mwene Muwaha Mulungu,

kamusekaga u mubi,

hambi nha yamanyile du vyono u mudumo wakwe weza wuli behi.

14 I wanhu wabi, wakwegaga mimaje yawo,

wakukunga no wupinde wawo,

ho wawawulaje waciwa na wono wasina cinji,

no kuwacinja wanhu we ling'ani.

15 Ninga mimaje yawo yiyo,

yowacinya weneco mu zinhumbula zawo,

no wupinde wawo wobenanjizwa-benanjizwa.

16 Isawo ndodo yo munhu we ling'ani yili ciba,

si nhe sawo nyinji yono wena nayo i wanhu wabi.

17 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha kovibenanjiza i vilungo vye wanhu wabi.

Langa wanhu we ling'ani,

Muwaha Mulungu kawakamisaga muno.

18 Muwaha Mulungu kawakamisaga wanhu wehuwilwa,

no wuhazi wawo wokwikala cibitilila.

19 Hamba vize vilo vibi,

wene si wogazika yaye,

hambi nha wowa wena ndigwa nyinji mu vilo vye lizala.

20 Ninga i wanhu wabi, wokongomela wose,

ne walugu wo Muwaha Mulungu,

wonyala nhe maluwa ga mu mbago, na mbeka,

wokwajililila lukulu nha lyosi.

21 U munhu mubi, kakopaga hamba si kalipaga yaye,

ninga munhu we ling'ani,

kanozaga du lihewa no kulavya bwete.

22 Wono wakondya kulugutizwa no Muwaha Mulungu,

wawo wo wohala yisi.

Ninga wono wakondya kulijitwa no Muwaha Mulungu,

wawo wowinjilwa kutali.

23 U Muwaha Mulungu, yo katongaga zinghanzo zo munhu,

nayo kamudimaga munhu mono yakwendezwa nayo,

24 na hamba yagwe hasi,

si kogwila lukulu yekalilile baho hasi,

vyono Muwaha Mulungu yo yamwibatilize.

25 Ane lu, nakombipa,

no wikale wangu weza wa myaka minji muno,

ninga nghali hamba sininawona munhu,

we ling'ani Mulungu yamulece,

hamba ne wanagwe,

wawe wakwilombeleza ndigwa, aa ng'o.

26 Langa mwene kalavyaga du bwete madaha gose,

na wanji yakuwakopesha,

ne wanagwe wakuwa wulugutwe.

27 Wuwuce mu wubi, witume ziswanu,

holeka nagwe kokwikala mu'si cibitilila.

28 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha yendile wiling'ano,

hamba si kawalekaga wehuwilwa wakwe.

Mwene kawadimaga wawo cibitilila,

ninga zindelo ze wabi, wokongomezwa.

29 We ling'ani, wohala yisi,

nawo wokwikala mumo cibitilila.

30 U munhu we ling'ani,

kalongaga nghani ze mahala.

Kanyhi kalongaga ze ling'ani no lulimi lwakwe.

31 Kawibataga wulajilize wo Mulungu mu nhumbula yakwe,

si kawulekaga yaye hamba hadodo.

32 U munhu mubi, kamusepaga u munhu muswanu,

no kujela kumuwulaga,

33 ninga Muwaha Mulungu sikomuleka,

u munhu muswanu mu vilungo vyo mulugu,

hamba si komuleka yaling'anizwe, hono yasitakwe.

34 Wumwitumbilaje Muwaha Mulungu,

kanyhi wukamise no wutonji wakwe,

nayo kokupela yisi,

nagwe kowawona i wabi vyono wakongomezwa.

35 Nimuwona u mubi yakalipe muno muno,

yetandaze nhe libici lyo muhagata lya Lebanoni.

36 Ninga ha nyuma nali neza kukola baho,

nghamwagana yeza yali hasina,

nghamulondola, ninga hamba siyejela yaye.

37 Ninga lanje ta,

u munhu muswanu munhu we ling'ani,

u munhu mono yendile mulamu,

yo kapegwaga lulelo.

38 Ninga wanyo wubi wose,

wokongomezezwa lukulu,

sunga no lulelo lwawo,

nalo lopatilwa lukulu.

39 U Muwaha Mulungu kawaponyaga we ling'ani,

no kuwadima mwipango lyo wugaye.

40 U Muwaha Mulungu kawatazaga no kuwaponya,

kawaponyaga mu wanhu wabi,

vyono nawo wamwitumbiye mwene.


Zabuli 38

Lilombo lyo munhu mono yali mu wugaye

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kunhacilila mwi'kuwo lyako,

hamba kundipiciziza mu wukali wako.

2 Nagumwa ne zisonga zako,

no muwoko wako wunghandamize vibi.

3 Na ku soko ye likuwo lyako,

ane neza nili mu wusungu wuwaha muno,

hamba na mu mafupa gangu, mweza musina wuswanu,

ku soko yo wubi wangu.

4 No wubi wangu wungubicize, wanhulila,

wawa nha mutegulo muzito muno muno.

5 Ne zinghomele zangu alu zawola,

zeza kunungha, ku soko yo wulele wangu.

6 Nekunga, nenama ho hasi,

nghulila ko wukalale misi yose.

7 Mhungo yanhama, neza nili behi no kufwa,

neza sina hamba wuswanu wowose.

8 Alu natimba, nadejepa muno,

nghuzizidika muno so wusungu wa mu nhumbula.

9 Go Muwaha Mulungu, agwe wuwumanyile wuhile wangu,

hambi cililo cangu nha cili calinze hali'gwe.

10 Ne nhumbula yangu yikukukunza muno,

sunga ne vilungo vyambucila,

ne meso gangu si gakuwona viswanu.

11 Sunga ne wayangu weza kunhiza,

ku soko ye zinghomele zangu,

na we mhuga yangu, nawo wekala kutali nane.

12 Nawo wono wakusaka wambulaje,

vyo wajendeleye du kutega mitego yawo.

Na wono wakunyihweseza zimbi,

nawo wakusaka wambulaje.

Misi yose, wekalaga wakwihwesa vyono wonhendela.

13 Ninga ane, si nghuzihuliciza yaye,

neza nha nili ciziwi,

neza nha mono yali cibwibwi,

mono singhusuma kulonga cinji.

14 Heya, mbeka! Neza nha munhu mono si

yakusuma kupitula hamba cinji,

hambi nha si ya kuhulika.

15 Ninga ane nokwitumbila gwegwe du

go Muwaha Mulungu.

Agwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu

wangu, kohuliciza.

16 Nghulomba I walugu wangu ho walece

kuwusecelelela wugaye wangu,

hamba kwilumbila mu wugwe wangu.

17 Neza nili behi nhondo no kugwa hasi,

hambi nha nili mu wusungu du vilo vyose.

18 Alu nghunena masutya gangu gose,

no wubi wangu wunghalaze muno muno.

19 Ninga walugu wangu,

wali vilungo, kanyhi wakamile.

Kanyhi wo wenji muno, wono wasulile mbula soko.

20 Nawo wawo wono wakungomolela zimbi,

ku soko yo wuswanu wangu,

wakumhingamhinga ku soko vyono ane nitumaga ziswanu.

21 Holeka wulece kundeka 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kuwa wuli kutali nane go Mulungu wangu.

22 Wiyohe, wunhaze mbela,

go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mukombozi wangu.


Zabuli 39

Wunene wo munhu mono yali mu wugaye

1 Ane natya, “Noyilanjilila mikalile yangu,

ho ndece kwituma wubi mu wulonje wangu.

Nowimila mulomo wangu lono wanhu wabi,

wali behi nane.”

2 Nali nha we cibwibwi,

hamba kulonga cinji, nghanyamala,

hamba si natuga ciba, wusungu wangu, wukacila muno.

3 Wusungu wangu wukaka kotya moto mu nhumbula yangu.

Na mu nghango yo wihwese wangu,

na vyo nacila kukalala,

hanyuma nghalonga nghani azi nghatya,

4 “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ndumilizo yangu yowa nili?

Manyizaje i vilo vyo wikale wangu,

nha vyono vikumalika mbela!”

5 Kumbe wunjelanizize vilo vicefu muno vyo wikale!

No wumi wangu kumbe yo nghani ya bwete du hali'gwe,

kumbe, mbeka, wanhu wose wehwanile cihumbi ce mbeho du.

6 Na mbeka, munhu wo wose kapulutaga nho mwenzenze du.

No wuhidimbazi wakwe wose,

wukuwa nghani ya bwete du,

mwene kakongholaga sawo,

hamba kumanya mono koyitumila nani.

7 Nalu, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

alu nghali nindiye cici kanyhi?

Witumbilo wangu nha wuli kuli gwegwe du!

8 Holeka wumhonye mu wubi wangu wose,

hamba wulecele lukulu kuvumila,

kanyhi, i walele waswe wakunhovulanga.

9 Ane nyamaye du, si nghulonga cinji kanyhi,

hambi nha yo gwegwe mono witumile zizo.

10 Holeka wulece kundipiciza kanyhi,

hambi nha neza nili behi no kufwa,

ku soko yo wutowe wako.

11 Na lono wukumulipiciza munhu,

ku soko yo wubi wakwe ko kumukalipila,

mbeka kotya wukuhodanga cinhu cono lipulaputya licendile.

Na mbeka, i wanhu wose wehwanile cihumbi ce mbeho du.

12 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuhulicize wulombe wangu,

hamonga ne cililo cangu,

wulece kunyamalila lono nghukulilila.

Hambi ane nha ninha mujenzi du mono yakupuluta,

nane nili mujenda nzila,

nha vyono wali wenha walezi wangu wose.

13 Wundekaje, nane niwe na nyemo,

lono nghali nafwa, no kuwucila lukulu.


Zabuli 40

Lwimbo lwo lukumyo

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Nghamulindila Muwaha Mulungu ko wukangalile,

nayo yakahuliciza, yakacihulika cililo cangu.

2 Yakanimula mwikombo lye zimbali,

yakambusa mu nhope namu,

yakambika mholile mucanyha ye litalawe,

yakazikamya zinghanzo zangu.

3 Yakanyijiza lwimbo lupya,

u lwimbo lo kumukumya Mulungu wetu.

Nawo wo wenji wono woziwona zizi wokogopa,

baho nawo ho womwitumbilo Muwaha Mulungu.

4 Yo mumoti munhu mono yamwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu,

u munhu mono sivikwilunza ne wedunhumuzi,

wono wakwajilila so wuhemba milungu yo wulambi.

5 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wangu,

wakondya kucinozeza nghani nyinji zo wizine,

ne mahweso gako ku soko yetu, si gakuwalwa yaye,

hamba hasina yunji wo wose mono yehwanile gwegwe.

Na hono nali sume kuziwala zizo zose,

hamba si nali nizimanye liwalo lyayo.

6 Agwe si wendile likumbiko hamba wulavye,

si wendile vilavywa vinyanywe,

hamba cilavywa so wubi, langa wumhelile makutu,

ho nghuhulice.

7 Holeka nane nghatya,

“Nghuza nha vyono nandicilwa mu citabu co wulajilize,

ane niwikanize kuwituma wuhile wako,

nha vyono nandicilwa mu citabu co Wulajilize.

8 Nghusangalala kuwituma zo wuhile wako,

go Mulungu wangu,

no kuwukamisa wulajilize wako kwe nhumbula yangu yose.”

9 Nane niwalonjela mhuga mbaha ye wanhu,

zinghani ziswanu ze mhonelo.

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe wumanyile vyono ane si nanyamala hamba hadodo.

10 Hamba si nayihundika nghani yiyino mu nhumbula yangu,

nha vyono wakondya kumhonya.

Nane vilo vyose nizilongaga zo wihuwilwa wako,

hamonga na zo wutazi wako.

Vyono agwe wuli Mukombozi Mwihuwilwa.

Hamba si nayihundicila i mhuga mbaha ye wanhu,

u wihuwilwa wako,

hamonga no lusungu lwako.

11 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kunyima zo lusungu lwako!

Lihewa lyako hamonga no wihuwilwa wako,

vyeze kunghamisa nhondo zose.

Kulomba wutazi

(Zab 70)

12 Hambi wugaye nha winji wono wumhilime,

si wukusumika hamba kuwuwalwe,

ne masutya gangu gambaninyhize,

hamba si nghusuma no kuwona,

hambi nha masutya menji,

kuciza winji we zimvwili za mu mutwe wangu,

sunga nane nhondo neza sina witumbilo kanyhi.

13 Wumhonye, go Muwaha Mulungu,

gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuze mbela wunhaze!

14 Ho wose wono wena wuhile wo kunyongomeza,

wakozwe cevu, wapuwe!

Wawo wono wakunondola wambulaje,

nawo wawuye ne nyuma, wakozwe cevu.

15 Wawo nawo wono wakunzwajiza,

wakolwe cevu so wulemwe wawo!

16 Ninga wose wono wakukulondola,

wasangalale no kuwa na nyemo mbaha.

Nawo awalya wono wendile ze mhonelo yako, nhondo zose,

weze kulonga wakutya,

“Yalumbwe Muwaha!”

17 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane nili muciwa, kanyhi nili munghwenzele,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

agwe si wunasemwa yaye.

Agwe yo gwegwe go Mutazi wangu no Mukombozi wangu.

Ale wuze, 'gwe go Mulungu wangu, wulece kutinya!


Zabuli 41

Wulombe wa mono yatamigwe

(Kuli mwimbizi: Zabuli ya Dawudi)

1 Yo mumoti munhu mono yakuwataza waciwa,

u Muwaha Mulungu komutaza nayo,

mu vilo vyo wugaye.

2 U Muwaha Mulungu komudima no kumuwika ca wumi,

no kumupela wumoteswe mono'si

hamba sikomuleka mu miwoko ye walugu wakwe.

3 Muwaha Mulungu komutaza mu wutamwa,

no kumuponya mu wutamwa wakwe.

4 Nane nghawa kutya nhavi,

“Ane nghusutizya, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

holeka wumbonele lusungu, wumhonye.”

5 Hambi walugu wangu nha

wakunonga nghani zimbi,

wakuhila nifwe, najililile lukulu.

6 Na lono wakuza kunamusa,

si wawezaga no wuhile wuswanu yaye,

langa wanyihwesezaga zimbi du,

ho wabite wakawalonjele wanji.

7 Nawo wose wono wasulile,

wekalaga wakwipwepwela i zangu,

wakunyihweseza i zimbi zono zosuma kunhamisa.

8 Akuno wakutya,

“Lece ta, wutamwa awu nhondo womuwulaga,

hamba si kokwinucila lukulu kanyhi, mu citanda cakwe!”

9 Sunga no mwendece wangu muwaha mono nali nimwitumbiye,

mono nali kuhangana nayo zindigwa zangu,

nayo yakondya kumhituka no kwihoma nane!

10 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wumbonele lusungu!

Wungomolele vilungo vyangu, nane niwalipicize.

11 Na mu nzila yiyo, mo nomanyila vyono mbeka

wukwendezwa nane.

Hono walugu wangu walece kusumiliza.

12 Kanyhi vyono ane nitumaga ze ling'ani,

holeka agwe kosikamya,

nokumbika hali gwegwe cibitilila.

13 Yalumbwe Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu we Waisilayeli wulawa cibitilila

ne cibitilila! Heya! Heya!


Zabuli 42

CITABU CE KEJETE

(Zabuli 42–72)

Wulombe wo munhu mono yawingwa kwawo

1 Nha vyono nghambaku ya sicilo, yisulumilaga,

malenga ga mu cikolongo, ale,

nhavivyo nane vyo nghukaluciye,

'gwe go Mulungu wangu!

2 Nimukaluciye Mulungu, heya,

nina nyhilu yo Mulungu mumi.

Alu, hamba nomuficila nili ho nimuwone?

3 Mahozi gaweza yo ndigwa yangu ku misi,

na ku cilo, vilo vyose lono wakumbuza wakutya,

“Yali hayi Mulungu wako?”

4 Nina wukalale wuwaha muno,

kolo nghukumbucila zono zapuluta,

vyono nali nilunzize na wanhu wenji muno,

nghuwatonga cikubita ku Nyumba yo Mulungu,

na vyono cali cisangalaye no kwimba zinyimbo zo wulumbe.

Ne lipuga lye wanhu wakunoza sikukuu.

5 Alo nhawule nghalaye nhavivi mu nhumbula?

Hamba nhawule nghugazika nhavivi?

Alu hodu, nomwitumbilo Mulungu nomukumya,

kanyhi Mulungu wangu no muponyi wangu.

6 Nina wukalale wuwaha mu nhumbula yangu,

holeka nghukukumbucila muno gwegwe go Mulungu,

nghukukumbucila ku wulawa mu yisi ya Yoludani,

sunga ku Helimoni, na ku wulawa mwi'tunda lya Mizali.

7 Kandetela mawingo hamonga ne madudu go wusungu,

mawingo hamonga no wuvulumuce wayo we malenga,

vyakondya kungwila.

8 Muwaha Mulungu kamhelaga vye lihewa lyakwe ku misi,

nane ne cilo nghumwimbila lwimbo lo lukumyo,

nghumulomba mwene mono kamhelaga wumi.

9 Nane nghumulonjela Mulungu, umudimi wangu nghutya,

“Alo nhawule wukusemwa?

Alu nha vyo nili mu wukalale du,

ku soko yo wubanwe ne walugu?”

10 Nakondya kutijinhwa ne matovulo gawo,

lono nhondo zose wakumbuzanga wakutya,

“Alu Mulungu wako yali hayi lo?”

11 Alo so cici nghalaye nhavivi mu nhumbula?

Hamba so cici nghugazika nhavivi?

Alu hodu, nomwitumbila Mulungu, nomukumya,

kanyhi, Mulungu wangu, no muponyi wangu.


Zabuli 43

Wulombe wo munhu mono yawingwa kwawo wujendeleye

1 Gwe go Mulungu, wime ku lubavu lwangu,

wunhetele ling'ani yangu,

wunyhitile kuli wanhu wono wakusulile,

i wanhu walambi kanyhi wabi.

2 Agwe Mulungu,

yo gwegwe mono wuli wutizizo wangu,

alu kanyhi nhawule wanhaga kutali?

Alu nha najendelela kugazika muno,

nghugazwa ne walugu wangu?

3 Wundetele wuzelu wako,

hamonga ne mbeka yako,

vinhonje, vifise sunga kwi'tunda lyako,

lyono lyelile, ku wikalo wako.

4 Baho nane go Mulungu,

nobita ha wukumbicilo wako,

nokuza kuli'gwe go Mulungu,

mono wo mwamulizo we nyemo yangu.

Nane nokukumya kwe lisumbi 'gwe go Mulungu,

go Mulungu wangu.

5 Alo socici nghalaye nhavivi mu nhumbula?

Hamba socici nghugazika nhavivi?

Alu hodu, nomwitumbila Mulungu,

nomukumya kanyhi Mulungu wangu,

no muponyi wangu.


Zabuli 44

Kulomba wudimwe

1 Gwe go Mulungu,

ase cihuliciye mu makutu getu sese co weneco,

i walezi wetu wacilonjeye ze metumo mawaha,

gono wagetuma mu vilo vyawo, heya,

metumo gono wagetuma baho katali.

2 Vyono yo gwegwe mweneco,

mono wawawinga wanhu we makungugo ganji,

ne hanhu hawo,

wukawa kuwekaza wanhu wako,

wukawalipiciza we makungugo ganji,

no kuwamotya wanhu wako.

3 Ne wanhu wako hamba si wayisola yisi yiyo,

mu nzila yo mumaje,

hamba si wasuma kuyitema mu nzila ye vilungo,

vyawo weneco,

langa wayitema ku so wusume wako ne vilungo vyako,

vyono 'gwe wali wuli hamonga nawo,

hambi nha wali wuwendile.

4 Hambi nha yo gwegwe go Mutemi wangu,

kanyhi go Mulungu wangu,

mono kuwapelaga cisumisumi wanhu wako.

5 Nase, mu vilungo vyako,

mo cikuwasumila walugu wetu, heya,

sunga cikusuma kuwakanyanjila ku soko ye vilungo vyako.

6 Nane hamba singhwitumbila wupinde wangu yaye,

hambi nha manyile vyono, hamba mumaje wangu,

si wosuma kumhonya yaye.

7 Hambi nha yo gwegwe du,

mono waciponya mu miwoko ye walugu wetu,

wukawasuma wono wali wacisulile.

8 Holeka nase nhondo zose,

cojendelela kwilumbila muli gwegwe, agwe go Mulungu,

kanyhi cojendelela kukulumba sunga cibitilila.

9 Ninga 'lu wacitaga, no kucikoza cevu,

hamba si wukujendana kanyhi ne mapuga getu.

10 Wacileka, ciwatize walugu wetu,

nawo wakawa kuzisola zisawo zetu.

11 Wukacileka ciwulagwe nhe zingholo,

wukawa kuciyagaziza mu makungugo ganji.

12 Wukawaguza wanhu wako ko wuguzi wupelu,

hamba kutuga cambuzi co cose,

13 Sunga ne wekalani wayetu,

wakuziwona zono wucitendeye, nawo lu,

weza kuciseka no kucizwajiza.

14 Alu wacileka cibedegalwe,

ne wanhu we makungugo ganji,

wakusinjisa mitwe yawo ko kucibedula.

15 Misi yose nikaye nghuwona cevu,

no wusu wangu wumemile soni zose,

16 ku soko ye maligo,

hamonga no wubedegalwe we walugu wangu,

hamonga na wono wasulile.

17 Zizo zose zakondya kucagana,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

ase cikali sinakusemwa yaye,

hamba kubena cilagane cako.

18 Ase cikali si nawa wanhu,

wono si cili wehuwilwa kuli'gwe,

hamba cikali sinawubedegala wutonji wako.

19 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

wacileka cisina wutazi wo wose,

mu malimu ga mu mbago,

wacileka muciligati ye citi ciwaha.

20 Na lono cali ciwe caleka,

kukuhemba gwegwe go Mulungu wetu,

no kuyimanyhilila milungu yo wulambi,

21 agwe go Mulungu,

mhali wakondya kuyimanya nghani yiyo,

hambi 'gwe nha wuzimanyile zose ze cinyele,

zono zili mu zinhumbula.

22 Ninga nase ku soko yako,

ciyidomile ne lifwa misi ne cilo.

Cikutendelwa nhe zingholo zono zikuwulagwa.

23 Wusinhuce, alu nhawule wugonile go Muwaha Mulungu?

Winukaje, cikukunena wulece kucileka cibitilila.

24 Alu nhawule wukwihundika hali'se,

akuno wukusemwa ze magaziko getu,

hamonga ne magazo getu?

25 Cenama sunga ho hasi,

cahangahanga ne manghundi.

26 Holeka winuce wucitaze,

wuciponye ku soko yo wendo wako.


Zabuli 45

Lwimbo lo kumukumya Mutemi

1 Nhumbula yangu yimemile mahweso maswanu,

nane nghumutulila Mutemi lwimbo lwangu,

no lulimi lwangu lwihwanile,

nhe kalamu yo mwandici munghula.

2 Agwe wuli muswanu kuciza wanhu wose ne zinghani zako,

zimemile wulonji wuswanu, nayo Mulungu,

kakulugutiza cibitilila.

3 Gwe go citung'ati, wuwikanize mumaje wako,

agwe yo gwegwe mono wuli we nhojo,

kanyhi yo gwegwe munghalo wuwaha wose.

4 Wujendelele kumwande so lukumyo lwako ko ku wusume.

Wuwutetele wiling'ano ne mbeka.

Ne vilungo vyako vyokupela wusume wuwaha muno.

5 Ne zisonga zako nha nghali muno muno,

nazo zipuguzaga mu zinhumbula ze walugu wo mutemi,
