sunga ne makungugo gakugwa hasi yako.

6 Ne ligoda lyako lyo wutemi,

lye 'goda lyo Mulungu,

likalaga cibitilila ne cibitilila.

Nagwe wukuwatema wanhu wako ko wiling'ano.

7 Agwe mbeka wendile wiling'ano, ninga si wendile wubi,

na ku soko yiyo, holeka Mulungu,

u Mulungu wako yakakuhagula,

nayo yakakujidila nyemo mbaha muno kuciza wayago.

8 Ne vivwalo vyako vina mhunjile,

wudi ne manemane,

ne watowa masumbi nawo wakukwimbila,

mu zinyumba zono zihambigwe kwe zimbali ze zinhembo.

9 Ne wana we cicekulu we watemi, wo wamonga,

na wawo wono wakukuhinjiliza, no mugolece wo wutemi,

yemile ku muwoko wako wo kulume,

yavwalile wihambo we zahabu za Ofili.

10 Wuhulicize ta go mwana we cicekulu wihwese!

Wuteje likutu lyako,

wusemwele lukulu i wanhu wako,

hamonga na we mhuga yako wose.

11 Lanje ta wuswanu wako vyono wukumwendeza mutemi,

mwene yo mulume wako,

holeka yikunojeye wumuhulice.

12 Ne wanhu wanya Tulo,

nawo wokuletela vipegwa,

sunga ne wagoli wose wokwenda wavumilwe nagwe.

13 Umwana we cicekulu wo mutemi yakuza,

lanje vyono muswanu ta!

Civwalo cakwe cihonwe kwa mhuti za zahabu.

14 Yakutegulwa kuli mutemi,

yavwalile civwalo ce mawala menji,

akuno yakusindicizwa ne wahinza wayagwe,

nawo wakutegulwa kuli mutemi nhavivilya.

15 Nawo wakubita kuko wena nyemo no wusangalale,

no kwinjila mwi'kulu lyo Mutemi.

16 Gwe go mutemi, i wanago,

wohalila ligoda lyo wutemi lye walezi wako,

nagwe kowatemya mononze yose.

17 Ne lilumbo lyako lyomanyika,

ku zindelo zose vilo vyose,

ne makungugo gose gokukumya cibitilila.


Zabuli 46

U Mulungu yali hamonga nase

1 Mulungu wo wutizizo wetu ne vilungo vyetu,

nayo yewikanize nhondo zose,

kuleta wutazi mwidaha lyo wugaye.

2 Holeka nase hamba si cokogopa cinji,

hamba yinze yisinjisice,

sunga ne matunda gagwile mwi'lenga,

3 hamba lilenga libume no kugala,

sunga ne vitunda vicicime ku soko yo wubume walyo.

4 Holili likolongo lyono malenga galyo,

galisangalazaga likaya lyo Mulungu,

wikalo wono welile wa Mono Yalawiliciye Muli Vyose.

5 Nayo Mulungu yali mwi'kaya lilyo,

holeka si lyosuma kusinjiswa,

hambi mwene nha kaliletelaga,

wutazi hakali mitondo muno.

6 U Mulungu kenulaga lizi lyakwe,

sunga ne yinze yiwa kwenjeduka,

ne makungugo gakolwa yikuwo,

sunga no wutemi wose wuwa kusinjisika.

7 U Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu we mihanga yali hamonga nase,

u Mulungu wa Yakobo, wo wutizizo wetu.

8 Ale nzonyi mugawone metumo go Muwaha Mulungu,

lanjenyi ta mapoto gono yetumile mono'nze.

9 Mwene yakuwudumya wulugu mono'nze yose,

mwene kabenanjizaga wupinde ne migoha,

sunga ne matuka go wulugu gose kagaticimizaga.

10 Yakutya nhavi, “Ale munyamale!

Mumanye vyono ane yo nene Mulungu!

Ane nghukumywa mu makungugo gose,

ane yo nene muwaha we yinze yose!”

11 U Muwaha Mulungu, Mulungu we mihanga,

yali hamonga nase,

u Mulungu wa Yakobo, wo wutizizo wetu.


Zabuli 47

Mulungu, u Mutemi we yinze yose

1 Nye nyo wanhu wose, towenyi maganza,

mukumyenyi Mulungu ko wusangalale!

2 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye, mono yakogofya.

Yo Mutemi muwaha kono'nze kose.

3 Kacipela wusume wo kuwasuma wanhu we makungugo,

kagawika makungugo hasi yo wulajilize wetu.

4 Kacipela yisi yiyi yiwe wuhazi wetu,

yisi yono wo lukumyo lwa Yakobo mono yamwendile.

5 U Mulungu kabita kucanyha akuno yakusangalalilwa,

Muwaha Mulungu kabita kucanyha,

akuno yakutowelwa ne zindulele.

6 Ale, mwimbilenyi Mulungu zi nyimbo zo lukumyo,

heya mwimbilenyi!

Mwimbilenyi Mutemi zi nyimbo,

zo lukumyo heya mwimbilenyi!

7 Mulungu yo mutemi we yinze yose,

holeka mwimbilenyi zi nyimbo zo lukumyo.

8 Mulungu yatemile makungugo gose nayo yekaye,

mwi'goda lyakwe lyo wutemi lyono lyelile.

9 Sunga ne watemi we makungugo,

wakwikonghola hamonga ne wanhu wo Mulungu wa Abulahamu.

Mwene Mulungu, yali vilungo yacizize

mapuga gose ge wasilikale wo wulungu,

mwene yamanyice muno muno muli wose.


Zabuli 48

Sayuni likaya lyo Mulungu

1 Muwaha Mulungu, yo Muwaha,

mono yanojeye kulumbwa mu Nyumba yakwe,

2 Mucanyha ye litunda lyakwe,

lyono lyelile Sayuni.

Litunda lyo Mulungu,

yitali kanyhi yiswanu,

likaya lyo Mutemi muwaha,

lyo liletaga nyemo ye yinze.

3 Na mu zikaya ze likaya lilyo lya Sayuni,

mumo mo Mulungu,

yakwilajila, vyono mwene wo wihundiko wono wukamile.

4 Watemi wakekonghola hamonga,

wakabitila wose hamonga kwihoma.

5 Ninga wali weza kuliwona du likaya lya Sayuni,

wakezina muno muno,

wakebatwa no woga, hodu wakawa kutiza.

6 Woga wukawebata, wakanza kuhangawa muno,

wakawa nho mucekulu,

mono yali mu nghunga yo lusawo.

7 Wakawa nha wono waputigwe,

ne libeho liwaha lya kucilima,

lyono lizibenanjizaga zingalawa zimbaha.

8 Zose zono cazihulikaga vyono Mulungu yo yetumile,

alu nhondo sese co weneco caziwona,

mwi'kaya lyo Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

nayo Mulungu kolidima likaya lilyo sunga cibitilila.

9 Gwe go Mulungu, ase cikuzihwesa muno zo

lusungu lwako mu Nyumba yako.

10 Agwe yo gwegwe mono wukukumywa muno hanhu hose,

lukumyo lwako lumanyice hanhu hose mono'nze yose.

Nagwe wutemile kwe ling'ani.

11 Holeka wanhu wa ku Sayuni,

yiwanojeye wasangalale!

Agwe kuling'anizaga muling'ano,

holeka wanhu wa mwi'kaya lya Yuda, wasecelele!

12 Ale, mubite mukalipilime likaya lya Sayuni,

bitenyi mukaviwale vizuli vyalyo.

13 Mugalangaje majinji galyo,

musunjilile sunga ne magomawakali galyo,

ho mosuma kuziganulila zindelo,

zono zikuza muwe kutya nhavi,

14 “Ayu yo Mulungu, Mulungu wetu cibitilila!

Nayo kowa mutonji wetu sunga cibitilila!”


Zabuli 49

Wulele wo witumbila sawo

1 Hulicizenyi zizi, anye nyo wanhu wose!

Tejenyi makutu genyu,

anye wose mono mwikaye mono'nze,

2 anye wose nyo wawaha ne wadodo,

nanye wose nyo wagoli, sunga nanye nyo waciwa.

3 Izinghani zangu zowa za mahala,

nane nolonga zinghani zo wizuce.

4 Nane notega likutu lyangu hulice fumbo,

no kuciloloseza cidalicizo cangu mu wutowa lisumbi.

5 Ane si nghogopa mu vilo vye zimbali,

lono nowa mhilimwe ne walugu mbavu zose.

6 Wanhu wabi, wono wetumbilaga zisawo zawo,

no kwilumbila winji wo wugoli wawo.

7 Mbeka, munhu si kosuma kwikomboza mweneco,

hamba kulipa kuli Mulungu cifo co wumi wakwe,

8 vyono cifo co wumi ciwaha muno muno.

Si kosuma kulavya cinhu co cose,

cono cosuma kuhomela,

9 cono cosuma kumunoza ho yajendelele kuwa mumi,

yalece kucijeza citenjelo.

10 Hambi wose nha wamanyile vyono,

sunga na we mahala nha wafwizaga du,

i walele, sunga ne wawula mahala.

Wose wawano, wawalecelaga wanji zisawo zawo.

11 Ne vitenjelo, wo wikalo wawo cibitilila,

wo wikalo wawo sunga ku zindelo zose,

hamba wawe ha mwamulizo wali watemile yinze.

12 U munhu, si kosuma kwikala mwi'lumbo lyakwe,

langa kofwa du nhe zinimu.

13 Azino zo za mu wulele wawo, zono ziwaganaga,

na wuwo wo mudumo wa wose,

wono welumbilaga zisawo zawo.

14 Kuli wene, lifwa yo mudimi wawo,

lyono lyowategula sunga ko ku citenjelo,

nhe zingholo zono ziwiciywe kuwulagwa.

Nawo we ling'ani, wokwilumbila muli wabi,

i miwili yawo yilawa yeza kuwola kuko ku yisi ye wafu,

kutali muno ne zikaya zawo zono wali wekaye.

15 Ninga ane, Mulungu konhaza,

komhonya mu vilungo vye yisi ye wafu.

16 Holeka agwe wulece kuhangawa,

lono wukuwona munhu yunji yakugolipa,

hamba lono sawo yakwe yikonjelezeka muno,

17 hambi lono yakufwa,

nha sikosoleleza cinji co cose,

hamba si vyobitana ne sawo yakwe

kuko ku yisi ye wafu yaye.

18 Holeka hamba yedunhumule mu mikalile yiyi,

sunga ne wanhu wamukumye ku soko

yo winji we sawo yakwe.

19 Ninga hamba yatye, vyo kobita du

vikahanje ne walezi wakwe kuko ku yisi ye wafu,

kono wutitu wutemile cibitilila.

20 Gwe, munhu hamba yatye, si kosuma kwikala

mwi'lumbo lyakwe vilo vyose,

langa kofwa du nhe vilimu.


Zabuli 50

Wuhembi we mbeka

1 Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu Mudaha vyose yakutya nhavi,

yakuwacema wanhu wose we yinze yose,

ku wulawa kucilima sunga ko kumwezi.

2 Ku wulawa ku Sayuni,

likaya liswanu muno u Mulungu,

yakwilajila no kumulika.

3 U Mulungu wetu yakuza,

hamba si konyamala yaye,

no moto wono wutakamizaga, nawo wumulongoleye,

no mukukunzo we libeho likali, wumupilime.

4 Nayo yakuzicema wulanga ne yinze zize zihomelele,

ziwone vyono yakuwaling'aniza wanhu wakwe.

5 Yakutya, “Ale, munghongholele iwehuwilwa wangu,

wono wawika cilagane nane mu wukumbice!”

6 Wulanga wukwanza zo wiling'ano wakwe,

nayo Mulungu mweneco yo hakimu.

7 “Ale, hulicizenyi,

anye nyo wanhu wangu, nane nolonga!

Ane nokuhomelela vyono wuli musutyi agwe Isilayeli,

Ane nili Mulungu, u Mulungu wako!

8 Ane si nghukukalipila ku soko ye makumbiko gako,

hambi nha wukali kundavizyaga vilavywa vyako vinyanywe.

9 I mbeka, ane si nghusaka,

ho wumhele zinghongolo za mwibululu lyako,

hamba zimvulati za mwi'tewa lyako, a a ng'o,

10 hambi zinimu zose za mu mbago,

nha sawo yangu,

sunga ne maelufu ge zinimu zose za mu matunda,

zose nha zangu nene.

11 Sunga ne zindeje zose za mu mbago,

nha zangu, sunga ne viwumbwa vyumi vyose,

vyono vili mu mawimbi, vyangu nene.”

12 “Hamba na lono nali mbone nzala,

si nali nghulonjele gwegwe yaye,

vyono yinze yose nha yangu,

sunga na vyose vyono vili mumo, vyangu vyose.

13 Alu agwe wukwihwesa,

vyono ane ndizaga nyama ye zinghambaku lo,

hamba lunji nghung'wa i sakami ye zimhene?

14 Lilumbo lyako,

liweze lyo likumbiko lyako,

kuli Mulungu wumufisilizize wilahe wako,

u Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye Muli Vyose.

15 Ale wunyhemaje lono wuli mu wugaye,

nane nokuponya, nagwe konghumya.”

16 Ninga Mulungu yakumulonjela u munhu mubi yakutya,

“So cici lo wukuzitambula-tambula zinzila zangu?

Hamba socici wukuzilonga-longa ze cilagane cangu?

17 Agwe nha kulemaga hono yunji yakuhose,

sunga ne zinzila zangu,

agwe si wendile kuzityatya yaye.

18 Agwe hono wumuwone munhu muhizi,

hodu du, mwelumbaniza nayo,

sunga ne wasenyha mukuhanga nawo.”

19 “Agwe kwikalaga nhondo zose,

wiwikanize du ku wulonga zimbi,

na ku wulonga wulambi nako, agwe wo mulimo.

20 Agwe kwikalilaga mu wulonga ze wayago du,

heya, wukumusonjeza sunga no nduguyo go mweneco.

21 Agwe wetuma zizo zose, nane nyamaye du,

wukwihwesa ane ninha gwegwe lo?

Ninga alu nghukutacila,

kanyhi nghukukalipila calinze.”

22 “Ale ta mwihwesaje viswanu ze nghani yiyi,

anye wose mono mumbedegaye,

ale mwilanjililaje, ane nomongomezenyi wose,

hamba hasina mono kosuma kumuponyenyi yaye.

23 Nayo mono yakumhela lilumbo liwa lyo

likumbiko lyakwe,

yuyo langa yo yanyogopile, no munhu wo wose mono

yakuhulika ane, nane nomuponya.”


Zabuli 51

Kulomba wunenelwe

1 Wumbonele lusungu, 'gwe go Mulungu,

mu nghango yo wendo wako wuwaha,

wugapatile lukulu masutya gangu,

ku soko yo winji wo lusungu lwako.

2 Wunyezeze lukulu wavu wangu,

wunghweme wubi wangu.

3 Nghuvumila wubi wangu,

hambi nha nikaye nghuwuwona wubi wangu,

calinze vilo vyose.

4 Nane nghusutizya gwegwe du wuli wene,

nituma zimbi hali gwegwe.

Holeka lono wukuning'aniza, wukunoza viswanu,

hambi nha wina ling'ani lono wukundipiciza.

5 Ane nha nili musutyi ku wulawa vyono nalelwa,

nili mubi ku wulawilila mu'nda ya yaya.

6 Ninga agwe wendile winenenyhezi wa mugati,

holeka wunyijizaje mahala mu nhumbula.

7 Wumbusize wubi, niwe nyelile, wunghweme,

ne Hisopo niwe muzelu swee.

8 Wumemye nyemo no wusangalale.

Wusangalaze kanyhi, ane mono wamhondanga.

9 Ninga wulece kuwulanga wubi wangu,

wupate masutya gangu gose.

10 Gwe go Mulungu, wumumbile nhumbula swanu,

wumbicile muli'ne nhumbula mhya yono yikamile.

11 Wulece kunhaga kutali nagwe,

hamba kumbusiza Muhe wako Mono Yelile.

12 Wundekaje sangalale,

vyono wamhonya, wumhele nhumbula ye cihuliko.

13 Nane ho nowejiza i wasutyi ze nzila yako,

nawo wanyo wubi, wokuwuyila gwegwe.

14 Gwe go Mulungu, u Mulungu we mhonelo yangu,

wumhonye ne lifwa lyono nojeye nifwe.

Nane nokwanza ko lwimbo, vyono

agwe kwitumaga ling'ani.

15 Wundigulaje mulomo wangu, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ho sume kuzanza zo lukumyo lwako.

16 Agwe, i mbeka, si wukwendezwa ne

likumbiko, yali yiwe si nhavivyo,

ane nali nghulavizye.

Agwe si wendile vilavywa vinyanywe.

17 Langa cilavywa cangu kuli'gwe go Mulungu,

yo nhumbula du yono yidejepe,

hambi 'gwe go Mulungu,

nha si kulemaga nhumbula

yono yidejepe na ye cihuliko.

18 Gwe go Mulungu,

wulinozezaje ziswanu likaya lya Sayuni,

wugazengaje kanyhi wupya majinji ga Yelusalemi.

19 Ale, baho langa,

ho kokwendezwa ne vilavywa vinyanywe,

sunga ne vilavywa vinyanyilwe lukulu,

sunga ne zinghongolo, nhondo,

ho zolavizywa mu cikumbicilo cako.


Zabuli 52

Wuling'anizi wo Mulungu ne lihewa lyakwe

1 Gwe go munhu 'gwe,

socici wukwilumbila wubi wako,

kuli wanhu waswanu?

2 Agwe kwihwesaga zo wongomeza wayago du,

nhondo zose, no lulimi lwako,

lwihwanile liceto likali!

Agwe kubotaga zo wulambi du mapango gose.

3 Kanyhi agwe, wendile zimbi si nhe ziswanu.

Agwe wuwendile u wulambi si nhe mbeka.

4 Gwe go mukonji muwaha,

agwe wendile zinghani zo kuwatamisa wanji.

5 Holeka u Mulungu nayo kokongomeza cibitilila,

kokusola no kukuwusa mu nyumba yako,

kokukula wuwuce mono'nze, mu yisi yo womi.

6 Nawo we ling'ani woziwona zizo,

wokogopa, hanyuma wokuseka wawe kutya,

7 “Lanjenyi ta zono zimwagane munhu ayu!

Mwene si yenda Mulungu yawe wutizizo wakwe,

langa mwene yaketumbila winji we sawo yakwe,

no kuwulondola mumo wihundiko wakwe!”

8 Ninga ane, nihwanile muzeyituni,

wono wudedewaye mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.

Nane nilwitumbilaga lusungu lwakwe,

cibitilila ne cibitilila.

9 Gwe go Mulungu,

nokulumba vilo vyose,

ku soko ye metumo gono wetuma.

Nane nokwanza hali wanhu wako,

we ling'ani, zo wuswanu wako.


Zabuli 53

Wusocele wo munhu

(Zab 14)

1 I walele welonjelaga caka wakutya,

“Hasina Mulungu!”

Wose wahodanji,

wetuma zinghani ze cikomaza,

holeka hasina hamba monga,

mono yakwituma ziswanu.

2 Mulungu yakuwasunjilila wanhu,

ku wulawa ku wulanga,

ho yawone hono ho yuli yunji we mahala,

hono hawe ho yuli munhu wo wose,

mono yakumulondola Mulungu.

3 Wose wapatuka, wose watipiye,

hasina mono ketumaga ziswanu,

hasina hamba monga.

4 Alu Mulungu nayo yakutya,

“Mwene wawo wono wakwituma makani mabi,

alo wasina wizuce lo?

Wono wakuwahiziza wanhu wangu,

hamba si wakuhemba ane yaye!”

5 Baho 'lu langa, wocicima ko woga,

u woga wono wakali hamba si nawuwona yaye,

hambi Mulungu nha kogeyagaza i mafupa ge walugu,

nawo wokolwa cevu, hambi Mulungu nha yakondya kuwaleka.

6 Yali yiwe yili ciba,

hono mhonelo ya Isilayeli,

yali yize kulawa mu Sayuni!

Mulungu kolo yawagomola wanhu wakwe,

i wana wa Yakobo wosecelela, Waisilayeli wowona nyemo.


Zabuli 54

Wulombe wo kwicitila kuli walugu

1 Gwe go Mulungu wumhonezye mwi'tagwa lyako,

wumbice niwe nili mwelecele kwe vilungo vyako.

2 Wuhulice wulombe wangu,

'gwe go Mulungu wuzibocele zinghani zangu.

3 Hambi wanhu wedunhumuzi, nha wakunhowanga nawo,

i wawulaji nha wakuwulondola wumi wangu,

wanhu wono wasina cogopo hamba cidodo ca kuli Mulungu.

4 Ninga, ane Mulungu wo wutazi wangu,

Muwaha Mulungu, yo kawusikamyaga wumi wangu.

5 No wubi we walugu wangu,

wuwawuyile weneco, ko wihuwilwa wako,

'gwe go Mulungu wuwongomeze.

6 Nane nokulavizya likumbiko kwa nhumbula nzelu,

kanyhi nolikumya litagwa lyako so wuswanu wako.

7 Hambi nha wakondya kumhonya mu magayo gangu gose,

nane nakondya kuwona vyono walugu wangu wose,

wakondya kusumwa.


Zabuli 55

Wulombe wo munhu mono yakwiwonelwa

1 Gwe go Mulungu, wuhulicize wulombe wangu,

wulece kuwutumbila mugongo wulombe wangu.

2 Nghukunena wuhulicize kanyhi wumvumile,

nakondya kukatazwa no wugazice wangu.

3 Nane nogope muno so wulonje,

wokogofya we walugu wangu,

na ku soko yo wiwonelwa ne wanhu wabi.

Wawo wanhu wabi wakungaza muno,

wakumbonela likuwo kanyhi wasulile.

4 Ne nhumbula yangu yimemile woga,

no wogofye we lifwa wundomile nene du.

5 Nane nghucicima so woga wuwaha,

nenjililwa no wogofye wuwaha muno.

6 Yali yiwe yili ciba,

hono nali niwe ne mabawa nha nziwa!

Nali nguluce mbite kutali nghacehe kuko.

7 Heya, nali mbite kutali muno muno,

nghacehe mu nyika.

8 Nali nhizize hanhu hono hapolile,

niwe kutali muno ne libeho lyo mukukunzo.

9 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuwatemanulaje milonjele yawo,

hambi nha nghuwona kowuli,

wuwulaji no wugombe kuko kwi'kaya,

10 vilipilime likaya misi ne cilo,

no kuliletela likaya wuwulaji wuwaha,

11 Wuhodanji watandala mwi'kaya lyose,

hamonga no wisuzi no wiwonelwe hanhu hose.

12 Hono mulugu wangu yali yasimanje lunji,

nali sume kuzikangalila zizo,

na hono mono yasulile yali yambedegale,

nali sume kwihundika niwe kutali no mwene.

13 Kumbe lu, yo gwegwe go muyangu,

yo gwegwe mwiyendi muyangu mono cizoweye muno!

14 Ase cali ciyizoweye,

ciduwanaga nha munhu na mwiyendi muyagwe,

kanyhi cali cihembanaga,

hamonga mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.

15 Ale ciba walugu wangu wafwe i vilo lono vikali,

wabite ku yisi ye wafu lono wakali womi,

hambi wubi nha wuwamemile twaa,

sunga mo mugati mu zinhumbula zawo.

16 Ninga ane nghumulilila Mulungu,

nayo Muwaha Mulungu komhonya.

17 Ane nikalaga du nghulila ko wukalale,

cinyamihe, hakali mitondo, na mu misi mugati,

ninga mwene kolihulika lizi lyangu du.

18 Mwene komhonya no kumbika mholile,

mu wulugu wono cidomile nawo,

hambi walugu wangu nha wenji muno.

19 U Mulungu mono yatemile ku wulawa cibitilila,

mwene kotega likutu lyakwe, kowasuma,

hambi wene nha si wawukamisaga wulajilize,

hamba kumogopo Mulungu kose.

20 U muyangu yakondya kuwatowanga i weyendi

wayagwe, yakondya kubena ne vilagane vyakwe.

21 Ne zinghani zakwe zitelemeye,

si nhe tilazi, ninga nhumbula yakwe,

ayo yisulumilaga wulugu du.

Nghani zakwe zili telele zinha mafuta, ninga,

zitemaga kotya mumaje mukali.

22 Mulecelaje Muwaha Mulungu,

wugazice wako wose,

nayo kokucitila muli zose,

hambi mwene nha si kamulekaga munhu,

wakwe we ling'ani yasumwe.

23 Ninga i wanhu wawulaji,

hamonga ne wanhu wabi,

u Mulungu kowategula mu vitenjelo vyawo,

lono wakali hamba si wanaficila nusu,

ye vilo vyo wikale wawo mono'nze.

Ninga ane nokwitumbila gwegwe du go Mulungu.


Zabuli 56

Kumwitumbila Mulungu

1 Gwe go Mulungu, wumbonelaje lusungu,

hambi wanhu wa nha wakusaka wambonele,

madaha gose walugu wangu nha wamhilime.

2 Misi yose walugu wangu wakunhowanga,

nawo lu ho wenji muno muno,

wawo wono wakwihoma nane.

3 Gwe go Mulungu u Mudaha Vyose lono woga wunyibate,

ane nghwitumbiye gwegwe.

4 Nane nghumulumba Mulungu,

ku soko ye nghani yakwe,

holeka nimwitumbiye du Mulungu,

hamba si nghogopa yaye.

Hambi munhu nha musocele konhenda cici?

5 Misi yose wekalaga wakwijiliza zinghani zangu,

hambi mahweso gawo gose,

nha gali mu wutamisa nene du.

6 Wetang'anilaga ho waziwice zawo hamonga,

ho wasepe 'ne, wazisunjililaga zose zono nitumaga,

wakusepa ho wambulaje.

7 Gwe go Mulungu,

wuwalipaje wawo i wabi mu nghango yo wubi wawo,

wuwagwise hasi kwe likuwo lyako.

8 Agwe wuwumanyile wusukangwe wangu,

wuwicile cikumbucizo ce mahozi gangu.

Wule, zose si zili mu citabu cako?

9 Na lono nghukulomba wutazi wako,

baho ne walugu wangu wawuyaga ku nyuma.

Nane manyiye lukulu,

vyono Mulungu yali hamonga nane.

10 Nane nghumulumba Mulungu ku so nghani yakwe,

nghumulumba Muwaha Mulungu,

ku so wutazi we nghani yakwe.

11 Nimwitumbiye Mulungu,

hamba si nghogopa cinji yaye,

Hambi munhu konhenda cici?

12 Gwe go Mulungu, nowufisiliza wihuwizo wangu kuli'gwe,

nane nokulavizya vilavywa vye malumbo.

13 Hambi nha wamhonya mwi'fwa,

heya kunghamisa ho ndece kugwa hasi,

ho njendelele kuwa hali gwegwe go Mulungu,

nikale mu wuzelu we womi.


Zabuli 57

Kulomba wutazi

1 Wumbonele lusungu, 'gwe Mulungu wumbonele

lusungu, ane nghutiziza kuli'gwe.

Hambi mu mubeho we mabawa gako,

nha mo mowuli wihundiko,

sunga kolo magazo gaga mawaha gakupuluta.

2 Nane nghumulilila Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye,

u Mulungu mono kafisilizaga visakwa vyangu vyose.

3 Nayo kohuliciza ku wulawa ku wulanga no kumhonya,

kowakoza cevu wawo wono wakunyiwonela.

Mwene Mulungu kondajila zo lusungu lwakwe,

hamonga no wihuwilwa wakwe.

4 Nawo walugu wamhilime wenha masimba malya wanhu,

ne meno gawo, gehwanile migoha ne zisonga,

ne zinimi zawo zihwanile mumaje mukali.

5 Wunojeye wukumywe ko ku wulanga 'gwe go Mulungu,

ne nhojo yako yiweze mu yinze yose.

6 Hambi i walugu nha wanhejile no lwavu,

wangwize, nane nghuzizidika muno so wukalale.

Wahimbiye likombo mu nzila, ninga hamba nhavivyo,

weneco wo wakugwila mumo.

7 Gwe go Mulungu, nhumbula yangu 'ne yimile,

yikamile, heya,

nhumbula yangu yimile yikamile, holeka nane nokwimba,

no kukusangalalila gwegwe!

8 Holeka winuce 'gwe go nhumbula yangu!

Mwinukaje nanye nyo ndonondo ne lisumbi!

Nane nosinhuka hakali mitondo muno muno.

9 Nane nokulumbila muli makungugo,

go Muwaha Mulungu,

kanyhi nokwimbila ze malumbo muli wanhu.

10 Hambi lusungu lwako nha luficile ko ku wulanga,

no wihuwilwa wako wuficile ko kucanyha.

11 Wunojeye wukumywe,

ko ku wulanga 'gwe go Mulungu,

ne nhojo yako yiwe mu yinze yose.


Zabuli 58

Kumulomba Mulungu yawaling'anize i wabi

1 Nanye mono mutemile!

Alu, mulig'anizaga mu mbeka?

Wulenyi, mbeka muwaling'anizaga wanhu mu ling'ani?

2 Hamba hadodo!

Anye langa mwikalaga mukwihwesa,

zo wituma zimbi du nhondo zose,

kanyhi yo nyenye mono mukuleta,

nghani ze mewonezi mu'si.

3 Hambi i wabi, nha wahodanjice du,

ku wulawa vyono walelwa,

wekalaga wakulonga zo wulambi du,

kulawa vyono walelwa.

4 Wamemile sumu yose,

sumu yinha ye zinzoka kanyhi wali ciziwi,

wenha lizoka linyamwilu lyono lili ciziwi.

5 Lyono silihulikaga hamba lizi lyo muhawi,

hamba lwimbo lo muganga, u munghula ku wuhawi.

6 Gwe go Mulungu, wugabenaje meno gawo,

wugakule i meno ge masimba gago,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

7 Wajililile lukulu nhe malenga,

gono gang'welelaga hasi mu muhangha, wawe nhe mahanze,

wakanyanjilwe, wanyale.

8 Wajililile lukulu,

nha vyono nghola yinjilaga mu nhope yajilila,

wawe nho muda wono wahodanjika,

hamba kuliwona ne lizuwa yaye!

9 Holeka, lono wakali du,

hamba si wanamanya cinji agwe wuwakulaje nhe mamivya,

hamba mabeleje.

Na kwe likuwo lyako go Mulungu,

wapemhelusizwe kutali muno muno akuno ho wakali womi.

10 Nawo we ling'ani mbeka wosangalala,

lono wakuwona i wabi vyono wakulipicizwa.

Nawo wojenda wakugazika,

mucanyha ye sakami ye wanhu wabi.

11 Ne wanhu wose wolonga wotya,

“Heya mbeka wanhu we ling'ani wahumbilizwaga!

Na mbeka u Mulungu,

ho yuli mono yakuyiling'aniza yinze!”


Zabuli 59

Kulomba wudimwe

1 Gwe go Mulungu wangu,

wumhonye kuli walugu wangu,

wunyhitile kuli wawo wono wakwisumaniza nane.

2 Wumhonye kuli wawo i wanhu wabi,

wunghomboze kuli wawo wawulaji!

3 Alu, lanje, ta,

vyono wakusepa wambulaje, i wanhu,

wawulaji wakwilunza hamonga ku soko yangu.

4 Lanje vyono wakumangha, wakwiwikaniza ku soko yangu,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kumbe nane ho sina lisutya, hamba wuhoswe,

hamba wubi muli'ne,

holeka winuce 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wunhaze!

5 Ale winuce 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

Mulungu u Mudaha Vyose,

yo gwegwe go Mulungu wa Isilayeli.

Winuce wuwalipicize wawo wanhu,

wono si wakumanyile yaye,

wulece kuwalecela lukulu i wabi,

hamba yumonga, wose i wetuma zimbi.

6 Walugu wawa, wawuyaga nhondo zose cinyamihe,

wakufwema nhe zimbwa,

akuno wakulipilima-pilima likaya.

7 Wulanje ta,

wogofye wawo hamonga ne maligo gawo!

Zinimi zawo zihwanile mimaje mikali,

kanyhi wene wehwesaga vyono,

hasina mono yakuwahulika.

8 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe wukuwaseka du,

hamba si wukuwacelelela bwete,

wanhu wawo wono si wakumanyile.

9 Ane nokwimbila zinyimbo zo kukukumya gwegwe,

agwe mono yo wuli yigomawakali lyangu.

10 Nayo Mulungu wangu,

komhela zo wendo wakwe,

kondajila niwawone walugu wangu,

vyono wakondya kusumwa.

11 Wulece kuwawulaga musunho,

i wanhu wangu ho walece kusemwa, langa,

wuwayumvyaje du kwe vilungo vyako,

kanyhi wuwagwise hasi, hambi gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo gwegwe go ngula yetu!

12 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuwalekaje wongomele,

mu nghango yo wubi we zinghani zawo,

webatwe weneco mu widunhumuzi wawo!

Ku soko yo wulijitwe,

hamonga no wulambi wawo,

13 wuwongomezezaje mwi'kuwo lyako, nawo

hamba si wokwijela kanyhi yaye, ne wanhu wose

wamanye vyono Mulungu yo yawatemile,

mu yisi ya Yakobo,

sunga ko ku ndumo ye yinze.

14 Walugu wawa,

wawuyaga nhondo zose cinyamihe,

wakufwema nhe zimbwa, akuno,

wakulipilima-pilima likaya,

15 wakuyumba-yumba akuno, na akuno

wenha zimbwa, wakulondola wulye,

na hono walece kwiguta,

hodu, wawa kwikala wakukoloma du.

16 Ninga ane nokwimba zo lukumyo lwe vilungo vyako,

kanyhi, nosangalalila zo lusungu lwako hakali mitondo,

hambi nha yo gwegwe go wihundiko wangu,

no wutizizo wangu mu vilo vyo wugaye.

17 Nokulumba, agwe mono wuli vyo vilungo vyangu,

'gwe go Mulungu, gwegwe go wihundiko wangu,

go Mulungu mono kumbonelaga lusungu!


Zabuli 60

Kulomba wukombozwe

1 Gwe go Mulungu, wacitaga no kucipondajiza,

wakolwa ne likuwo,

ninga alu, cikukunena wucigomolele zo wuswanu wako.

2 Kuyicicimya yisi no kuyipandula,

alu wukande mazatu gayo,

hambi nha yeza kupanduka.

3 Kuwaleka wanhu wako wenjile mu magayo,

nalu ceza pepesuka nha wono wagalile divayi.

4 Kuwasugula awalya wono wakogopile,

ho wasume kuyityega songa.

5 Wuciponye kwe vilungo vyako,

wucipitule no wulombe wetu,

i wanhu wako wono wuwendile, ho wekale wapolile.

6 U Mulungu kalonga ku wulawa hono helile hakwe katya,

“Alu nobita sangalaye nghayigawe yisi ya Shekemu,

ne lilolo lya Sukoti nalyo,

noligawa kuli wanhu wangu.

7 Gileyadi yo yangu,

sunga na Manase nayo yangu nhavivyo,

ayi nayo Efulayimu, nha yo kofiya yangu ye cuma,

nayo Yuda, yo nghome yangu yo wutemi.

8 Ayi nayo Mowabu,

yo bakuli yangu yo kwizuguziza.

I cilatu cangu naco,

nocigumila ku Edomu, ho niyiteme.

Nane baho langa ho nozoza kwe lizi,

lyo wusume kuli Wafilisiti.”

9 Alo nani lu, mono konhegula,

kwi'kaya lye ligomawakali?

Hamba nani mono konhonga sunga fice ku Edomu?

10 Gwe go Mulungu,

alu wacilecela lukulu du hongo?

Hamba lu sikojendela lukulu,

kanyhi hamonga ne mapuga getu?

11 Wucipele wutazi kuli walugu wetu,

hambi wutazi wono wukulawa kuli wanhu,

nha si wukwiliye cinji yaye.

12 Ase cosuma du ko wutazi wo Mulungu wetu,

Mwene kowapondanjizanga i walugu wetu.


Zabuli 61

Kulomba wudimwe

1 Gwe go Mulungu, wuhulice cililo cangu,

wuhulice wulombe wangu.

2 Ku wulawa ku mhelo ye yinze,

nghukulilila ne nhumbula yangu yizilice.

Wunhegule mu canyha ye litalawe liwaha.

3 Hambi agwe, nha yo gwegwe go wutizizo wangu,

kanyhi yo gwegwe go ligomawakali lyangu,

kuli walugu wangu.

4 Alu wunghombole,

nikale mwi'hema lyako cibitilila,

ho nikale mholile hasi ye mabawa gako.

5 Hambi 'gwe go Mulungu,

nha kuhulika zo wihuwizo wangu,

kanyhi kumhela ne ziswanu,

zono wawehuwiza wono wakogopile gwegwe.

6 Wumonjeleze mutemi vilo vyo wikale wakwe,

i myaka yakwe yo wikale yiwe minji.

7 Yateme cibitilila hali'gwe go Mulungu,

u lusungu no wihuwilwa wako, vimudime.

8 Nane baho nhondo,

ho nokwimbila zinyimbo zo lukumyo,

akuno nghuwufisiliza no wihuwizo wangu nhondo zose.


Zabuli 62

U Mulungu yo mudimi wangu

1 Ane nimulindiye Mulungu du,

hambi i mhonelo yangu,

nha yilawaga kuli mwene.

2 Mwene, yo mudimi wangu mono yakumhonya.

Mwene wo wihundiko wangu,

muli mwene nikaye mholile.

3 Alu mojendelela kulewana no munhu sunga nili,

mono yenha lijinji lyono liname,

lyono lili behi no kumwaguka?

4 Heya, anye langa mukuhila mungwise du,

ku wulawa mwi'lumbo lyangu.

Ne nyemo yenyu 'nye, yili ku wulonga,

zo wulambi du.

Anye mulugutaga du ko wulonje,

ninga mu zinhumbula zenyu, mukulijitiza.

5 Ane nimulindiye Mulungu du,

no witumbilo wangu wuli muli mwene.

6 Nayo yo mweneco du, mono kandimaga,

no kumhonya, mwene yo mudimi wangu.

Holeka hamba sinosinjisika yaye.

7 I mhonelo no lukumyo lwangu,

vyose vikulawa kuli Mulungu,

hambi Mulungu nha wo wutizizo wangu,

kanyhi mwene yo mutazi wangu.

8 Nye nyo wanhu, mumwitumbile Mulungu

nhondo zose,

kanyhi mumulonjele ne visakwa vyenyu.

Hambi Mulungu nha wo wutizizo wetu.

9 Wanhu wose nha wehwanile muhe wo wehele,

wawaha ne wadodo, wose yo nghani ya bwete du,

na hono wuwapime, si waficile hamba kilo,

wose wapelupelu muno si nho muhe wo wehele.

10 Mulecele lukulu kwitumbila wuhizi,

hamba kwikumizya sawo yo wuhizi,

kanyhi mulece kuzitumbila zisawo

hamba ziwe zonjezece muno.

11 Mulungu kalonga nghanghulo monga,

nane hulika nghanghulo nyinji,

vyono wuwaha wose, yo sawo yo Mulungu,

12 heya, u lusungu nha lwakwe Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi agwe nha kumupelaga munhu wo wose,

mu nghango ye metumo gakwe.


Zabuli 63

Nyhilu yo kusaka kuwa hamonga no Mulungu

1 Gwe Mulungu, yo gwegwe Mulungu wangu,

nane nghukulondola kwe nhumbula yangu.

Ne nhumbula yangu yikukaluciye muno,

nho munhu mono yena nyhilu ye malenga,

nina nyhilu kotya yisi,

yono yinyalile, yono yisina malenga.

2 Nina nyhilu yo kukuwonela hono helile hako,

ho niwuwone wuwaha wako,

hamonga no lukumyo lwako.

3 Hambi lusungu lwako,

nha luswanu muno lucizize wumi.

Holeka nane nokulumba.

4 Nane nakulumba mu vilo vyose vyono nghali mumi,

nane nokwinulila gwegwe miwoko yangu,

lono nili mu wulombe.

5 Nhumbula yangu yosangalala,

nha sikukuu ya vye mafuta na vyono vijinile,

nane nokwimba sangalaye zo lukumyo lwako.

6 Hamba lono ngonile mu citanda,

nghukwihwesa gwegwe du cilo cose.

7 Hambi nha yo gwegwe du,

mono kunhazaga vilo vyose.

Nane nosangalalila mu mubeho we mabawa gako.

8 Ne nhumbula yangu vyokwityatizya lukulu nagwe,

no muwoko wako wo kulume wukusikamya 'ne.

9 Ninga wawo wono wahidimbaliye wambulaje,

wogwila weneco kusi, ku yisi ye wafu.

10 Nawo wowulagwa ko mumaje,

wowa ndigwa ye macewe.

11 Ninga mutemi kosecelela muno,

ku soko yo Mulungu.

Nawo wono wakwihuwiziza mwi'tagwa lyo Mulungu,

wawo wamulumba muno.

Ninga milomo ye walambi wose, yodindwa.


Zabuli 64

Kulomba wudimwe

1 Wuhulice go Mulungu wuhulicize cililo cangu.

Wumhonye wumi wangu,

hambi ne nha nghuwogopa walugu wangu.

2 Wunyhitile no wuhile wubi we wanhu wabi,

hamonga ne lizozo lye wanhu wavu.

3 Wakuzinola zinimi zawo kotya mumaje,

wakulonganjika makani ga wusungu kotya zisonga.

4 Wakulawilila mu wihundiko wawo,

wawa kumuhoma munhu mwinenenyhezi,

wakumuhungucila no kumuhoma wasina,

hamba cogopo bwete.

5 Wakwilumbaniza no kuhangana mumo,

mu wuhile wawo wubi,

wakwilumvya ne hanhu,

hono woyihundika mitego yawo.

Wakwihwesa vyono,

“Hasina mono kosuma kuyiwona yaye.”

6 Wakwilumbaniza mu zinghani zawo zo wuhile wubi,

akuno wakutya, “Cakondya kugawikaniza viswanu,

i mahweso getu!

Alu nani kanyhi 'mbi mono kosuma,

kucimanya zo wuhile wetu?”

Ilihweso lye cinyele lyo munhu,

mbeka lihundice mu nhumbula yo munhu!

7 Ninga Mulungu kowaguma ne zisonga,

wotuga nghomele nhinicizo.

8 Kowongomeza ku soko ye zinghani zawo,

no munhu wo wose mono kowawona,

kosinjisa mutwe wakwe.

9 Ne wanhu wose wokogopa no kwanza,

ze metumo gono yetumile Mulungu,

wokwihwesa ze metumo gakwe.

10 Nawo we ling'ani,

wozisecelelela zono Muwaha Mulungu yetumile,

nawo wotiziza kuli mwene.

Ne wanhu waswanu wose, wokwikumya.


Zabuli 65

Lwimbo lwe ne lilumbo

1 Gwe go Mulungu,

yicinojeye ase cikukumizye mu Sayuni,

no kuwufisiliza wihuwizo wetu wa kuli'gwe.

2 Hambi 'gwe nha wukuwuhulika wulombe wetu.

Ne wanhu wose wokuziza no kunena wubi wawo.

3 U wubi wetu kolo wacilemela,

agwe kucinenelaga.

4 Wamoti wono agwe wuwahaguye wekale,

hono helile hako!

Ase cokwiguta ziswanu za mu nyumba yako,

u wumoteswe wa mu Nyumba yako yono Yelile.

5 Na mu metumo gako mawaha,

mo wukucipitulila no kuciponya.

Agwe go Mulungu,

nha yo gwegwe go mhonelo yetu.

Sunga ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vili mono'nze,

sunga na vyono vili kusi mu malenga,

vyose vikwitumbiye gwegwe du.

6 Agwe yo gwegwe mono wuli vilungo,

wukusimya matunda hanhu hayo,

7 kunyamazaga wukolome we lilenga,

ne lizozo lye mawingo galyo,

agwe kunyamazaga sunga ne lizozo lye wanhu.

8 I yinze yose yikutuga wizine,

ku soko ye metumo gako.

I metumo gako gakuleta wusagahale,

wa nyemo ku wulawilila kucilima,

sunga ko kumwezi.

9 Agwe yo gwegwe mono kuyidimaga yisi,

ko kuyitonyezeza mvula,

kuyipelaga moso no wucindye muno,

ne makolongo gayo,

kugamemyaga ne malenga ga ku wulanga,

kuyicindyaga yisi no kuwapela wuhemba.

Nhavivi vyo kunozaga.

10 Kutonyezaga mvula mbaha mu migunda,

kuwa kuyiloweka ne malenga,

yisi nayo kuwa kuyidejefya ne mafuso,

no kuviluguta vilimwa visume kucindika.

11 No mwaka wose kuwupelaga zo wuswanu wako,

na ho hose hono ko kolela gwegwe,

hamemaga wumoteswe.

12 Ne zi nyika zono zina lihanze zose,

zimemile mitugo,

nago matunda gamemile nyemo.

13 Wudimilo nawo wumemile ngholo zose,

nago malolo, gagubicizwe no wuhemba.

Navyo vivi vyose, visangalaye so nyemo.


Zabuli 66

Lwimbo lwo lukumyo ne lilumbo

1 Anye nyo wanhu wose mono'nze,

mumusangalalilaje Mulungu!

2 Mwanze zo lukumyo lwakwe kwa nyimbo,

mulavizyenyi lilumbo lyo lukumyo!

3 Mumulonjele Mulungu mutye, “Imetumo gako,

mbeka go metumo go wizine muno muno!

Na ku soko ye vilungo vyako viwaha,

sunga ne walugu wako lu, weza kukufugamila gwegwe.

4 Ne yinze yose yikukuhemba gwegwe,

sunga ne wanhu lu, wakwimba zo lukumyo lwako!”

5 Ale, muze ta muziwone zimbaha,

zono yetumile Mulungu,

Kanoza nghani zo wizine muli wanhu.

6 Mwene yalinyaza lilenga hakawa yisi nghavu,

ne walezi wetu wakasuma kulowoka mumo,

wakujenda kwa migulu, ale nase baho,

cikawa kusecelela muno.

7 Kagadimaga cibitilila kwe vilungo vyakwe.

Mwene yo kagadimaga makungugo gose.

No mubedegazi wo wose yalece kumupinga-pinga ku mwande.

8 Nanye nyo makungugo gose, ale,

mumulumbe Mulungu wetu,

mwanze zo lukumyo lwakwe,

zihulikwe hanhu hose.

9 U mwene yo kacipelaga wumi,

hamba si yacileka cigwe.

10 Hambi 'gwe go Mulungu, nha wacipima,

wacijela nha vyono fweza yikuwa kujelwa mu moto.

11 Wakondya kucigwiza mu lwavu,

wacidinghuza mutegulo muzito muno.

12 Kuwaleka walugu wetu wacikanyanjile,

cikolela mu moto na mu malenga.

Ninga luno wakondya kucileta hanhu hono hapolile.

13 Nane nokuletela mu Nyumba yako,

vilavywa vinyanywe,

no kukufisiliziza wihuwizo wangu.

14 Nane nokulavizya nha vyono nehuwiza nene no mweneco,

mu cipango cono nali nili mu wugaye.

15 Nokulavizya vilavywa vinyanywe vyono vijinile muno,

vilavywa vinyanywe vye zingholo zinume,

nolavya zinghongolo hamonga ne zimhene.

16 Anye mono mumogopilo Mulungu,

alenyi muze wose, muhulicize,

nane nomulolosezenyi zono yanhendeye Mulungu.

17 Ane namulomba wutazi,

kanyhi nghamukumya no lulimi lwangu.

18 Na hono nali nihwese zimbi mu nhumbula yangu,

nayo Mulungu hamba si yali yahulicize ng'o.

19 Ninga alu mbeka Mulungu kahulika,

heya mbeka, kawuhulika wulombe wangu.

20 U Mulungu yalumbwe,

hambi nha si yawulemile wulombe wangu,

hamba kuluwusa lusungu lwakwe kuli'ne.


Zabuli 67

Lwimbo lwo wulumbe

1 Wuciwonele lusungu go Mulungu, wucilugute,

wucilanje ko wuswanu.

2 I yinze yose ho yimanye wutonji wako,

ne makungugo gose ho gamanye vilungo vye mhonelo yako.

3 Wanhu wose wakulumbe, 'gwe go Mulungu,

makungugo gose wakukumye.

4 Ne makungugo gose gasangalale no kusecelela,

hambi agwe nha wukuwaling'aniza wanhu we ling'ani.

No kugatonga makungugo ga mono'nze.

5 Wanhu wose wakulumbe 'gwe go Mulungu,

ne makungugo gose gakukumye.

6 Yisi yacindya vilimwa vyayo, u Mulungu,

Mulungu wetu kacilugutiza.

7 Mulungu kacipela vye lihewa lyakwe,

holeka wanhu wose,

wa mono'nze yiwanojeye wamogope.


Zabuli 68

Lwimbo lwo wusume

1 U Mulungu kenuka,

ne walugu wakwe weyagala,

na wono wamusulile, wose wakumutiza!

2 Nawo wakupemheluswa,

nha vyono lyosi lipemheluswaga ne mbeho,

ne wabi wose wokwajililila lukulu,

hali Mulungu, hamba kwijela yaye,

nha vyono lihula lyenjedukaga ha moto.

3 Ninga we ling'ani wasangalaye,

no kusecelela hali Mulungu,

wakusecelela akuno wakwimba so nyemo.

4 Mwimbilenyi Mulungu,

kanyhi mulikumye ne litagwa lyakwe,

nozelezenyi nzila yakwe,

mwene mono yakujendela mu mavunde,

litagwa lyakwe yakucemwa, Muwaha Mulungu,

ale, musangalale hali mwene.

5 Mulungu mono yekaye hono helile hakwe,

mwene yo Sogo ye wana wono wasina walezi,

kanyhi yo mudimi we wafwile.

6 Nayo Mulungu yo kawapelaga waciwa,

wikalo wono wikalaga,

kanyhi kawawopolelaga wawopwa,

no kuwasangalaza, ninga i wabedegazi,

wojendelela kwikala mu yisi yono yinyalile.

7 Gwe go Mulungu,

lono wawatonjela wanhu wako,

lono wali wali mu muhinzo wawo mu nyika,

8 Yinze yacicima,

sunga no wulanga nawo wukatonya mvula,

ku so wuze wako,

go Mulungu wa Sinayi, heya,

so wuze wako go Mulungu wa Isilayeli!

9 Gwe go Mulungu,

wayitonyeza yisi mvula mbaha,

wukayidejefya lono yali yeza kusakala.

10 Ne wanhu wako wakatuga mumo wikalo,

na so wuswanu wako,

wukawapela waciwa visakwa vyawo.

11 U Muwaha Mulungu kalavyaga wulajilize kamonga du,

nawo wacekulu yipuga yiwaha,

wawa kuzitandaza zinghani zizo,

12 “I watemi hamonga ne mapuga gawo,

wose wakutiza!”

Iwacekulu nawo ku zikaya,

wakugawana vyono vitecigwe.

13 Alu nhawule wanji wa muli nyenye wagonile mu mabululu,

mu wugono we zinziwa mwizuwa lyo wulugu?

Mono movili vihwani vye zinziwa vya fweza,

vyono vina mabawa gayo gakung'alala zahabu.

14 U Mulungu Mudaha Vyose, yali vilungo,

yali yeza kukondya kuweyagaza watemi,

mwi'tunda lya Salimoni,

ne seluji nayo yikawa kutonya mwi'tunda lilyo.

15 Matunda ge Bashani, go matunda matali muno,

matunda ge Bashani, gena vizuli vinji muno!

16 Nanye mo vizuli!

Nhawule lo mukuliwonela wivu litunda,

lyono Mulungu yalihagula,

ho yekale mucanyha yalyo?

Ninga Muwaha Mulungu,

kokwikala kuko cibitilila!

17 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakulawa ku Sinayi,

yakuza hono helile hakwe,

yali hamonga ne lipuga yiwaha,

lya maelufu na maelufu lye matuka go wulugu.

18 Yakubita kucanyha,

akuno yali hamonga ne mateka gakwe,

kabocela vipegwa kuli wanhu,

heya nhavivyo sunga na kuli wanhu i wabezi,

nayo Muwaha Mulungu kokwikala muli wene.

19 Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe,

mono kacidinghulilaga mitegulo yetu mwizuwa ne zuwa.

Mwene yo Mulungu mono kaciponyaga.

20 Mulungu wetu, yo Mulungu we mhonelo,

nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yo kaciponyaga mwi'fwa.

21 Nayo Mulungu,

koyipondanjiza mitwe ye walugu wakwe,

heya, i mitwe ya wose,

wono wakali wadindimiye zo wituma wubi.

22 Muwaha Mulungu yalonga yakatya,

“Ane nowagomola i walugu ku wulawa ku Bashani,

nowagomola ku wulawa mu visolo vye lilenga.

23 Agwe ho wusume kukola mu zisakami zawo,

sunga ne mabwa nago gayinamhe nha vyono gosuma.”

24 Gwe go Mulungu i mijendele yako,

yo wusume nha yijeye calinze,

i mijendele yo Mulungu wangu, u Mutemi wangu,

sunga ko ku hanhu hakwe hono helile!

25 I wembi walongoye ku mwande,

mu nyuma kowali watowa masumbi,

haciligati naho, wahinza watowa vigoma.

26 “Mukumizyenyi Mulungu haciligati,

ye lipuga yiwaha lye wanhu.

Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

anye nyo lulelo lwa Isilayeli!”

27 Yamule Benyamini, u mudodo wa wose,

ho watyatye i walongola wa Yuda ne lipuga lyawo,

ha nyuma ho waze wawaha wa Zabuloni,

na wa Nafutali.

28 Gwe go Mulungu,

ale wulajilaje wuwaha wako,

wuwaha wono wetumamila ku soko yetu,

29 mu wulawa mu Nyumba yako ku Yelusalemi.

Ne watemi wokuziza du,

wena ne vipegwa vyawo mu mawoko!

30 Wugakalipilaje le, malimu gago gono

gekalaga mwi'lamba,

litewa lye zindama hamonga ne zinghongolo,

sunga makungugo gago gakupele cogopo,

hamonga no kukulipa kodi.

Ale, wuweyagaze wanhu wawo i wenda wulugu!

31 Wawaha watambule, wokuza ku wulawilila Misili,

Waetiyopiya nawo,

womwinulila Mulungu miwoko yawo.

32 Nanye mo wutemi wa mono'nze,

mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu zinyimbo zo lukumyo,

33 mwimbilenyi mwene mono yekaye ku wulanga,

wulanga wono wali katali na katali.

Muhulicizenyi lono yakulavya lizi lyakwe kwe vilungo.

34 Mwanze zo wuwaha wo Mulungu!

Lukumyo lwakwe luli muli Isilayeli,

lukumyo lwakwe luli mu wulanga.

35 Mulungu yo we cogofyo muno,

i hanhu hakwe hono helile, heya,

mwene yo Mulungu wa Isilayeli!

Nayo kawapelaga wanhu wakwe vilungo no wuwaha.

Mulungu yalumbwe!


Zabuli 69

Kulilila wutazi

1 Gwe go Mulungu, wumhonye,

hambi malenga nha gaficila mu singo yangu.

2 Alu neza kudodomela mu nhope mbaha,

sunga na ha wusambo we magulu hasina.

Alu ngwiliye ha ciligati ye cisolo ce malenga,

neza kusolwa ne mawingo.

3 Alu natimba muno so cililo cangu,

sunga ne malaka gangu ganyala muno.

Ne meso gangu geza si gakuwona viswanu,

lono nghuwulondola wutazi wako.

4 Nawo wanhu ho wenji wono wasulile bwete,

wenji muno wacizize no winji we zimvwili zangu.

Ne walugu wangu wakunonga za wulambi,

wali vilungo, wakusaka wambulaje.

Wakusaka ati ngomole vinhu vyono'ne si solile.

5 Gwe go Mulungu,

agwe yo gwegwe mono wuwumanyile wulele wangu,

ne masutya gangu gose,

hasina hamba limonga lyono yihundikwe hali gwegwe.

6 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, u Mudaha Vyose,

wulece kuvumilila lukulu,

awalya wono wakwitumbiye wakolwe cevu ku soko yangu.

Wawo wono wakukulondola,

'gwe go Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

walece kupuwa ku soko yangu.

7 Hambi nha nghozwa cevu ku soko yako gwegwe,

soni zifunicize mu wusu wangu.

8 Sunga neza nha nili mujenzi kuli wandugu zangu,

i wawaha zangu hamonga ne walumbu zangu,

hamba si wakumanya yaye.

9 Uwendo wangu so Nyumba yako, wukumbulaga.

Ne matovulo gose gono wakutovulaga gwegwe,

gose gambuyila nene.

10 Na lono nenenenyhezeza mu wuleka kulya,

wanhu wakahosa muno.

11 Nali neza kuvwala ligunila,

ho ndajile wukalale wangu,

wene wakasekanga muno sunga wakambedegala.

12 Ne wanhu nhondo weza wakuhehela, mu zinzila du,

sunga ne wagala wujimbi wose,

wakutula nyimbo zo kunyimba nene.

13 Ninga'ne nghukulomba,

agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuvumile wulombe wangu,

nha vyono wendile gwegwe,

lono nghukulomba.

Wuhulicize, 'gwe go Mulungu,

hambi agwe nha wuli lusungu muno,

kanyhi yo gwegwe du,

mono yo mhonelo ye mbeka.

14 Nghukulomba wumbuse mu nhope yiyi,

ho ndece kudodomela,

wumhonye kuli wawo wono wasulile,

wumhonye mu visolo vye malenga.

15 Wulece kundeceleza solwe,

ne lihwazi lye malenga,

wulece kundeka ndodomele ha cisolo,

hamba kudodomela mu citenjelo.

16 Wuhulicize 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

so wuswanu we lihewa lyako,

wunanje ku so winji wo lusungu lwako.

17 Wulece kwihundika kutali,

nane no mwitumami wako, wuhulicizaje 'lu,

hambi nha nili mu wugaye.

18 Wunyizize, wumhonye, wumhonye kuli

walugu wangu wenji.

19 Hambi agwe nha wumanyile vyono nghutovulwa'ne,

wumanyile ne cevu,

hamonga ne zisoni zono zikunyagana,

sunga ne walugu wangu, agwe wuwamanyile wose.

20 Zinhovu nhondo zanhukuwika,

sunga no mutima wangu, nane natimba!

Nondola wutuzwe, hamba kuwuwona yaye,

hamba si yejeye no munhu mono konhuza yaye.

21 Lono nali ndabuce,

wene wakamhela sumu yo nilye,

nali nghaluce,

wene wakamhela siki yo ning'we.

22 Ne zindigwa zawo,

ze zi sikukuu ze makumbiko,

ziweze mutego kuli wene,

Ne zi sikukuu zawo ziwagwize weneco.

23 Meso gawo gatukuwikwe ho walece kuwona,

ne migongo yawo wuyitimvye.

24 Wuwajidile likuwo lyako,

no wukali wako wuwagane.

25 No wikalo wawo wikale bwete, no munhu wo wose,

yalece kwikala kanyhi mu mahema gawo.

26 Hambi nha wakuwagaza,

wono agwe wakondya kuwalipiciza,

kanyhi wakuwonjelezeza nghomele,

awalya wono agwe wakondya kuwapela zinghomele.

27 Ale, wuwalipicize mu nghango yo wubi wawo,

hamba wulece kuwanenelela lukulu.

28 Wuwapatilaje lukulu mu citabu ce womi,

walece kuwa mwi'walo lya we ling'ani.

29 Langa wunyinulaje 'ne 'gwe go Mulungu,

wumhonye, ane mono nili mu wusungu,

kanyhi mono nakondya kutukuwika mutima wangu.

30 Nane nolikumya litagwa lyo Mulungu kwa nyimbo,

kanyhi nomutogoza ko kumulavizya yilumbo.

31 Ne nghani yiyino,

yo yomwendeza muno Muwaha Mulungu,

si nha kumulavizya vilavywa vya ng'ombe,

hamba si nha cilavywa ce nghongolo mututu.

32 Nawo wono wakwiwonelwa,

kolo weza kuziwona zizino,

wosecelela muno muno.

Nawo wono wakumulondola Mulungu,

wotuga vilungo.

33 Muwaha Mulungu kawahulicizaga waciwa,

hamba si kosuma kuwasemwa yaye wanhu wakwe,

wono wawopigwe.

34 Nanye nyo wulanga ne yinze, mumukumye Mulungu,

lilenga na vyose vyono vili mumo, nanye mukumyenyi.

35 Hambi Mulungu nha koliponya likaya lya Yelusalemi,

no kulizenga kanyhi likaya lya Yuda.

Ne wanhu wakwe, wokwikala mumo no kutema,

36 no lulelo lwe wetumami wakwe,

wawo wo wolihala likaya lilyo,

na wose wono wamwendile,

wawo wo wokwikala mwi'kaya lilyo.


Zabuli 70

Kulomba wutazi

(Zab 40:13-17)

1 Wumhonye, go Mulungu,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wunhaze mbela!

2 Ho wawone soni, kanyhi wakozwe cevu,

wawo wono wakusaka kunyongomeza!

Wagomolwe kunyuma, wakozwe cevu,

wawo wono wakwendezwa no wutamiswe wangu!

3 Nawo wono wakwendezwa no wuzwajizwe wangu,

wapuwe no kukolwa cevu!

Wono watezaga, “Agwe! Agwe!”

4 Ninga awalya wose wono wakulondolaga,

wasangalale, no kusecelela ku soko yako.

Na walya wose wono wakwendezwa,

ne vilungo vyako vyo kuponya,

weze kulonga nhondo zose wakutya,

“Mbeka Mulungu yo we vilungo muno!”

5 Wunyizize mbela 'gwe go Mulungu!

Ane mono nili musocele kanyhi nili muciwa.

Hambi nha yo gwegwe go mutazi wangu,

kanyhi go mukombozi wangu,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kutinya kunhaza.


Zabuli 71

Wulombe wo mukombi

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane nghutiziza kwako,

holeka wulece kundeka ho ngholwe cevu.

2 Wunimule, wumhonye,

ku soko yo wiling'ano wako,

wuteje likutu lyako!

3 Weze wuli wutizizo, no wihundiko wangu,

ne vilungo vyono vikamile vyo kumhonya,

hambi 'gwe nha yo gwegwe go mudimi wangu,

kanyhi yo gwegwe go wihundiko wangu.

4 Wumhonye 'gwe go Mulungu wangu,

mu miwoko ye wabi.

Wumhonye mu wulawa mu wanhu wabi ne wawulaji.

5 Hambi 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo gwegwe du mono nghwitumbiye.

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane nghwitumbiye gwegwe ku wulawa wudodo wangu,

6 nghwitumbiye ku wulawa vyono nalelwa,

hambi nha yo gwegwe mono wandavya mu'nda ya yaya.

Yo gwegwe du mono nghukumyaga vilo vyose.

7 Nane naweza colecezo co wizine kuli wenji,

ninga agwe yo gwegwe go wutizizo wangu wono wukamile.

8 Mulomo wangu wumemile zo lukumyo lwako,

hamonga ne lilumbo lyako misi yose.

9 Kanyhi wulece kundeka kolo nakombipa,

na kolo vilungo vyangu vyamalika wulece kundeka.

10 Hambi i walugu wangu nha wakunonganga,

nawo wono wakusepasepa, wakwilumbaniza hamonga,

11 wakutya, “Ale, Mulungu yamuleka,

holeka mutyatyenyi mukamwibate,

hambi lu nha hasina kanyhi wo kumuponya!”

12 Holeka wulece kwikala kutali nane go Mulungu,

wuze mbela wunhaze, 'gwe go Mulungu wangu,

13 ho wakozwe cevu no kongomela,

wose wono wakwisumaniza nane,

nawo wose wono wakusacila zimbi,

wakozwe cevu no kuzwajizwa.

14 Ninga ane nolindila, akuno nitumbiye,

kanyhi nokukumya kali kenji muno muno.

15 Nane nosimula ze metumo gako ge ling'ani,

kanyhi nokwanza ze metumo gako,

ge mhonelo misi yose,

hamba gawe nha gapulute mahala gangu.

16 Nane, go Muwaha Mulungu,

nokwamuliziza mu metumo gako mawaha,

nokwanza zo wuswanu wako gwegwe du wuli wene.

17 Gwe go Mulungu, yo gwegwe du,

mono wanyijiza ku wulawa wudodo wangu,

holeka nane si nolecela lukulu,

ku wanze we metumo gako go wizine.

18 Wulece kundecela lukulu 'gwe go Mulungu,

hamba niwe nawa nili mukombi nina ne zimvwi,

sunga langa kolo nakondya kuwalonjela ze vilungo vyako,

wanhu wo lulelo lono lukuza.

19 Gwe go Mulungu i vilungo vyako,

hamonga no wiling'ano wako,

vificile ko ku mavunde.

Agwe yo gwegwe mono wanoza zimbaha muno.

Alu go Mulungu yo nani 'mbi,

kanyhi mono yehwanile gwegwe?

20 Kundetela magayo menji mabi,

ninga hamba yiwe nhavivyo,

agwe komhela kanyhi wumi no kunyinula.

21 Konyonjelezeza kanyhi ne lilumbo lyangu,

hamonga kanyhi no kusindamika.

22 Nokwimbila zo lukumyo kwe lisumbi,

ku soko yo wihuwilwa wako,

'gwe go Mulungu wangu,

nokwimbila nyimbo kwe lisumbi,

agwe mono welile wa Isilayeli.

23 Nane nowa no wusangalale,

lono nghwimba zo lukumyo lwako,

nokwimba kwe nhumbula yangu yose,

hambi nha wamhonya.

24 Nane nosimulila zo wiling'ano wako misi yose,

hambi nha wanyihwesezaga zimbi ninga lu,

wakozwa cevu hamonga ne zisoni.


Zabuli 72

Kumulombela mutemi

1 Gwe go Mulungu wumupele mutemi ning'aniza yako,

wumupele mwana wo mutemi wiling'ano wako,

2 ho yaliteme likungugo lyako kwe ling'ani,

ne waciwa wako nawo, ko wiling'ano.

3 Ho hawe no mulamu mono yisi,

ne yisi yose yimeme wiling'ano wako.

4 U Mutemi yawatetele,

i wasocele wa mu nghungugo, yawataze wana we waciwa,

langa yawongomezeze lukulu i wanhu wabi.

5 Mutemi yawe ne vilo vinji vyo wikale wakwe,

nhe lizuwa, na kanyhi nho mwezi, mu zindelo zose.

6 Mutemi yawe nhe mvula yo wunyunyute mu migunda,

yawe nhe mvula yono yiyitonyelaga yisi.

7 No wiling'ano wulawilicile,

mu vilo vyose vyo wikale wakwe,

no mulamu nawo wonjezece muno,

sunga mwezi kolo wukuduma kumulika.

8 Yateme ku wulawilila lilenga sunga lilenga,

kanyhi ku wulawa kwi kolongo lya Efulata,

sunga ko ku mhelo ye yinze.

9 Na ku mwande yayo,

i walugu wakwe womwinamila.

Nawo wono wakwisumaniza nayo,

wolokwigwisa weneco mu manghundi.

10 Ne watemi we Talishishi, hamonga ne vizingwa,

wose womulipa kodi, watemi we Sheba na Seba,

nawo womuletela vipegwa.

11 Ne watemi wose wa mono'nze, womupela cogopo,

sunga ne wanhu we makungugo gose, womwitumamila.

12 Nayo komuponya u muciwa mono yakumulomba,

sunga no muciwa mono yasina munhu wo kumutaza.

13 Mwene, kowawonela lusungu wanhu wasocele ne waciwa,

kowaponya wono wali mu mikalile yo wugaye.

14 Kozikomboza zinhumbula zawo,

mu wiwonelwe no wuwulaji, hambi wumi wawo,

nha wina cambuzi ciwaha muno muno kuli mwene.

15 Mutemi yawe ne vilo vinji vyo wikale wakwe,

yabocele vipegwa vye zahabu ku wulawa ku Sheba,

ne wanhu nawo wajendelele kumulombela kuli

Mulungu vilo vyose, ho yamoteswe misi yose.

16 Yisi nayo yiweze no wuhemba winji muno,

wumeme na mu matunda ga Lebanoni,

ne wanhu wameme mu makaya,

nawo womoteswa wawe nhe mahanze.

17 Litagwa lyo Mutemi, lyeze no lukumyo vilo vyose,

no lukumyo lwakwe lwikale sunga ha nghango,

yo wung'alale we lizuwa.

Ne makungugo gose, gamoteswe muli Mwene,

sunga ne wanhu wose wamutye yo mumoti.

18 Yalumbwe Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

mono kanozaga mapoto yali wene.

19 Ilitagwa lyakwe lye nhojo,

likumywe cibitilila.

No lukumyo lwakwe lumeme mu yinze yose.

Heya, heya!

20 Awu wo mudumilizo we malombo ga Dawudi,

u Mwana wa Yese.


Zabuli 73

CITABU CE KADATU

(Zabuli 73–89)

Wiling'ano wo Mulungu

1 Mbeka Mulungu yo muswanu kuli Isilayeli,

muswanu na kuli wono wena nhumbula nhelevu.

2 Nali neza nili behi,

no kwikobala, na hadodo du nali nhelele,

3 hambi nha nali kuwawonela wivu i wedunhumuzi,

lono nali neza kuwona i wabi wakumoteswa.

4 Hambi wawo nha si waganagwa no wugaye yaye,

i miwili yawo mitelevu,

kanyhi wena ne vilungo du, wakamile.

5 No wugaye we wanhu,

hamba si wuwaganaga wene,

kanyhi hamba si wagazikaga,

nha vyono wanji wagazikaga.

6 No widunhumuzi waweza kuli wene,

nha mukufu mu singo zawo,

no wukali nawo, wawa nha wo mwenda wawo wo kuvwala,

7 Ne meso gawo gavimbile so wujine,

zinhumbula zawo zilavyaga ze mahweso gawo mabi,

8 Wendile kuwezwajiza wanhu wanji, no kuwalonga makani mabi,

wedunhumulaga no kupanga zo wiwonela wanhu wanji.

9 Na kwe milomo yawo, wawulongaga vibi wulanga,

na kwe zinimi zawo, wetapaga mono'nze.

10 Sunga ne wanhu wo Mulungu nawo wakuwatyatya,

no kwihuwila zose zono wakuzilonga.

11 Akuno wakutya, “Mulungu si kozimanya yaye!

Hamba Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye,

si kosuma kuziwona ng'o!”

12 Nhavivi vyo wenha i wanhu wabi,

wakwizize vyose no kuciziliza.

13 Alu ne hongo, neyeza bwete nhumbula yangu,

no kwilanjilila ndece kwituma wubi?

14 Misi yose nhuga magazo,

nagwe kumhelaga wulipicizwe,

hakali mitondo nhondo zose.

15 Nane mhali nonje nha vyono wene wakulonga,

si nali niwe nili munhu we mbeka muli wanhu wako.

16 Nane nghajela kuyihwesa nghani yiyi,

ninga yikawa nghani nghamu muno muno kuli'ne,

17 sunga langa nali neza kuhelela hono helile hako,

baho langa ho namanyila zono zowagana i wabi.

18 Mbeka, agwe kuwawikaga i hanhu ha cinheleza,

wukuwanoza wagwe wajilile.

19 Wakongomezwa nhinicizo,

no kupatilwa kutali ko wogofye.

20 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, kolo wukusinhuka,

wene wakwajilila kamonga,

hamba kukumbucilwa kanyhi,

wawa kwihwana nha ndoto,

zono munhu kazisemwaga,

lono yakwinuka hakali mitondo.

21 Nali neza kuwuwona wusungu mu nhumbula yangu,

no kuhomeka mu ciselinji cangu.

22 Nghawa mhuwile hamba kumanya cinji,

nghehwana nhe lilimu hali'gwe.

23 Ninga hamba yiwe nhavivyo,

ane nili kuli 'gwe du vilo vyose,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Agwe, wunyibate muwoko no kumhela vilungo.

24 Agwe yo gwegwe du. Mono wukunhonga ko wulanjizi wako,

na ku mudumo, kombocela mu lukumyo lwako.

25 Alu nani 'mbi kanyhi ku wulanga,

mono kosuma kunhaza, langa hono si gwegwe du?

Na hono niwe nili hamonga nagwe mono'nze,

hamba sinosuma kwanjila cinji yaye.

26 Hamba niwe nyhepuciywe vilungo,

mu muwili na mu nhumbula,

agwe go Mulungu, yo gwegwe go vilungo vyangu,

ne ligawo lyangu yo gwegwe du cibitilila.

27 Na muli wose mono kokwitemanula nagwe, ale,

yuyo mbeka kofwa du.

Nayo mono si yakukuvumila,

yuyo nayo agwe komongomezeza lukulu.

28 Ninga viswanu kuli'ne,

niwe behi nagwe go Mulungu,

hambi agwe go Muwaha Mulungu nha yo mhonelo yangu.

Nane nozanza ze milimo yakwe yose yono witumile!


Zabuli 74

Kulomba likungugo liponywe

1 Go Mulungu, alu nhawule wacitajila lukulu?

Alu so cici likuwo lyako likwaka kuli zingholo zako!

2 Wukumbucile wihangulize wako,

wono wawahagula gwegwe ku wulawa na katali,

mbeyu yono wayikomboza yiwe sawo yako,

litunda lye Sayuni lyono walinoza liwe wo wikalo wako.

3 Ale winuce wukole mucanyha,

ye matongo gagano ge vilo vyose.

Nawo walugu wakondya kuhodanga vinhu vyose,

vya mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.

4 Walugu wako wakuzoza mu Nyumba yako,

ne lizozo lyo wusume!

Waziwicile mumo ne zibendela zo wusume wawo!

5 Wehwana no mutenya zingwagu,

mono katemaga mabici ne nhemo yakwe.

6 Wakawubenanjiza mulango we Nyumba yo Mulungu,

kwe zinhemo zawo hamonga ne zinyondo zawo.

7 Wakayiticimiza no moto Nyumba yako yono yelile.

Wakahatovula i hanhu hono agwe wukogopwa.

8 Vyono wali welahize sunga wacimale ase wose,

holeka wakahaticimiza mu yisi yose,

hanhu hose hono cekongholelaga kukuhemba.

9 Vitanjilo vyetu vyose vyono vyelile,

sicikuviwona lu, alu ceza cisina hamba muwizi.

Hamba si cimanyile zizi,

zojendelela kuwa nhavivi sunga nili!

10 Alu 'gwe go Mulungu,

u mulugu ho kojendelela kukusekanga sunga nili?

Alu mwene ho wolitovula litagwa lyako sunga cibitilila?

11 So cici lo wukulema kucitaza lu?

Nhawule wuyiwicile ku mugongo miwoko yako?

12 Agwe go Mulungu, yo gwegwe mono wuli

mutemi wetu ku wulawa katali na katali,

mono kunozaga mhonelo mono'nze yose.

13 So vilungo vyako, watemanula lilenga,

wukayitijinha mitwe ye mazoka ga mwi'lenga.

14 Wayitijinha mitwe ye lilimu,

lyono likucemwa Lewiyatani,

wukawa kuzipela zinimu za mu mbago muvimba walyo.

15 Agwe yo gwegwe mono wanoza mivwilo ne vikolongo,

wukaganyaza i makolongo mawaha.

16 Misi yo yako, sunga cilo naco co cako,

waviwika mwezi ne lizuwa hanhu hayo.

17 Agwe yo gwegwe,

mono wayiwika mipaka yose ye yinze,

yo gwegwe mono wawika vipango vye cibahu,

ne vipango vye mbeho.

18 Ninga wukumbucile 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wubedegazi wono wukulawa kuli walugu wako,

likungugo lyono lipuwile,

likulitovula litagwa lyako.

19 Wulecele lukulu 'gwe go Mulungu,

kugagumilila malimu, wumi we wendece wako,

kanyhi wulece kuwasemwa wanhu wako,

wono wali mu wugaye.

20 Wucikumbucilaje i cilagane cako kuli'se!

Yisi nayo yimemile wuwulaji wose,

hanhu hose ha citi,

21 Holeka wulece kuwaleceleza wali wene,

ho wakozwe cevu, wose wono wakwiwonelwa.

Nawo waciwa hamonga ne wanjile,

wakukumye gwegwe.

22 Ale winuce go Mulungu, witetele,

wukumbucile wubedegazi we vilo vyose,

wono wukulawa kuli wono si wakumanyile.

23 Wulece kugasemwa mazozo ge likuwo,

gono gakulawa kuli walugu wako,

hamonga ne liyowo lye vilo vyose,

lya wono wakwisumaniza nagwe.


Zabuli 75

Mulungu yo muling'anizi

1 Cikukulumba 'gwe go Mulungu, cikukulumba!

Nase cikuziganula zo wuwaha wako,

no kuzisimulila ze metumo gako mawaha gono witumile.

2 U Mulungu yakutya,

“Ane nakondya kuliwika lidaha lyono likwiliye!

Na mwidaha lilyo langa, ho noling'aniza mugoloka.

3 Nayo yisi hamba yisinjisice, sunga na vyose,

vyono vili mugati yayo,

ane yo nene mono niyibatilize misinji yayo.

4 Nane nghuwalonjela wedunhumuzi nghutya nhavi,

‘Anye nyo wedunhumuzi,

mulecele lukulu widunhumuzi wenyu?

Ne wabi, nawo nghuwatya nhavi,

nanye nyo wabi, mulecele lukulu widunhumuzi wenyu!’

5 Mulece kwiwona nha muli cinji yaye,

kanyhi mulece kulonga zinghani zo wikumyi.”

6 Nawo wuling'anizi,

si wulawaga kucilima hamba kumwezi yaye,

hamba si wulawaga ku nyika hamba mu matunda yaye.

7 Langa u Mulungu mweneco yo muling'anizi,

mwene kamunyetyaga ayuno,

no kumwinula ayuno.

8 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yebate cikombe mu muwoko wakwe,

cikombe cimemile divayi nghali ye likuwo lyakwe,

yakuyijida, ne wanhu wabi wose wakuying'wa,

heya, wakuying'wa sunga libanhi lye ndumilizo.

9 Ninga ane nosangalala cibitilila,

nowa mwimbila zo lukumyo u Mulungu wa Yakobo.

10 Nayo kovinyenyanga vilungo vyose vye wanhu wabi,

langa vilungo vye weling'ani avyo vyokangazwa.


Zabuli 76

U Mulungu yo yali cisumisumi

1 U Mulungu yo yamanyice mu yisi ya Yuda,

ne litagwa lyakwe lye 'tambule mu Isilayeli.

2 Ne lihema lyakwe lili ku Salemi,

no wikalo wakwe wuli ku Sayuni

3 Kuko ko yabenanga zisonga ze walugu,

yakabena ne ngula, no mumaje, heya,

sunga ne vitwalo vyose.

4 Agwe wina nhojo, wulawiliciye,

si nhe matunda ge cibitilila.

5 Na wono wena nhumbula ye vilungo,

wabokwa mateka gawo,

nalu wagonile zinhongo zawo zo mudumilizo,

i vilungo hamonga no wunghula wawo wose,

wuwa nghani ya bwete du.

6 Nagwe go Mulungu wa Yakobo,

wali weza kuwakalipila du, hodu du, zifwalasi,

hamonga na wono wali wali mucanyha ye zifwalasi,

wose wakafwa yitenene.

7 Agwe go Mulungu, mbeka wuli wa kogofya muno,

alu nani kanyhi 'mbi,

mono kosuma kukangalila hono wuwe wakolwa likuwo?

8 Nagwe nha kuyilajila ning'aniza yako,

vyono yinha, ku wulawa ku wulanga,

nayo yinze yiwa kogopa no kunyamala twii,

9 lono wali weza kwinuka no kuling'aniza,

kuwaponya wose wono wakwiwonelwa mono'nze.

10 Ne likuwo lye wanhu,

likuletelaga lukumyo lono lucizize muno;

nawo wono waponile ku wulugu,

wowa kuzikumbucila zi sikukuu zako.

11 Alenyi mumupelaje Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wenyu, vyono mwehuwiza,

nanye mono muli behi, mupelenyi vipegwa Mulungu,

mono yanojeye wanhu wamogope.

12 Mwene kawawusiza wumi i wawaha wedunhumuzi,

kawogofyaga muno muno watemi we yinze.


Zabuli 77

Wutuzwe mwidaha lyo wugaye

1 Nghumulilila Mulungu kwe'zi yiwaha,

nghumulilila nayo kohulika.

2 Nane nimulombaga Muwaha Mulungu, lono nili mu wugaye,

nghumwinulila miwoko yangu ne cilo,

hamba kukatala yaye,

ninga si nhugaga wutuzwe wo wose.

3 Nane lono nghumukumbucila Mulungu,

nghutuga wuzinguce, na lono nghwihwesa muno,

nhumbula yangu yikuzilika.

4 Yakunyimila ndece kutuga nhongo hamba hadodo,

'nye, ane nili mu wugaye mbeka,

no kulonga kose hamba si nghusuma yaye.

5 Niwa kwihwesa sunga ne vilo vye katali,

nghumbucila sunga ne myaka yono yapuluta.

6 Na mu cilo cose, nili mu wihwesehwese,

nghwihwesa no kwiwuza nghutya nhavi,

7 “Alu mwene, Muwaha Mulungu, hongo yacilecela lukulu?

Hamba si kokwendezwa nase kanyhi lo, hamba hadodo?

8 No lusungu lwakwe luwaha ma, alu hodu lwamalika?

Alu hodu, hamba si kowufisiliza,

kanyhi wihuwizo wakwe?

9 Hamba Mulungu yasemwa lo vyono yali lusungu?

Hamba wule, alo likuwo lyakwe,

lyowa lyo lyawuwusa wupolo wakwe lo?”

10 Hanyuma kanyhi nghatya,

“Alu cono cikunhamisa muno,

mu nhumbula yangu cocino, wo wutya,

u Mulungu lu mono yali vilungo,

yaweza yasina vilungo kanyhi!”

11 Ninga 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nozikumbucila ze metumo gako,

nozihwesa zinghani zako ze katali zo wizine.

12 Nokwihweseleza muno muli zo mulimo wako,

hamonga ne metumo gako mawaha.

13 Gwe go Mulungu,

vyose vyono kwitumaga 'gwe, vyelile.

Hasina mulungu muwaha kanyhi mono yehwanile gwegwe.

14 Agwe yo gwegwe Mulungu mono kwitumaga mapoto,

walajila kuli makungugo wuwaha wako.

15 Wawakomboza wanhu wako ko muwoko wako, heya,

u lulelo lwa Yakobo na lwa Yusufu.

16 Ne malenga nago gali geza kukuwona go Mulungu,

heya, gali geza kukuwona,

mbeka gakogopa muno muno,

lilenga likacicima sunga ko ku visolo vyayo.

17 Ne mavunde gaketa malenga,

mabalalusi gakabalaluka muno muno,

ne zisonga zako, zikamulika ako na ako,

18 Lizi lye libalalusi lyako, likaza,

limuli lyayo, likayimulika yinze nayo,

yinze yikasinjisika, yikacicima.

19 Wayiwika nzila yako mucanyha ye lilenga,

wukajenda mwi'lenga liwaha,

ninga nzayo zako hamba kwijela yaye.

20 Wukawatonga wanhu wako nhe zingholo,

ko wutonji wa Musa na Haluni.


Zabuli 78

Mulungu ne wanhu wakwe

1 Ale hulicizenyi wijize wangu,

anye nyo wanhu wangu,

muteje likutu muhulicize zinghani zangu.

2 Ane nolonga nanye mu nzila ye vihwanicizo,

nomulolosezenyi ze zifumbo ze katali,

3 ze zinghani zono cazihulika,

kanyhi no kuzimanya,

zinghani zono walezi wetu waciloloseza.

4 Nase hamba si coleka kuwasimuliziza,

i wana wetu, cowasimuliziza lulelo,

lono lukuza kuli zo wuwaha wo Muwaha Mulungu,

kuli ze mapoto gakwe gono yagetuma.

5 Hambi nha yawika wulajilize kuli Waisilayeli,

na kuli lulelo lwa Yakobo,

yakasimya wuhomelezi.

Yakawalajiliza walezi wetu,

vyono wawejize ne wana wawo malajilizo gakwe.

6 Ho wanhu we ndelo yono yikuza wagamanye,

heya, i wanhu wono wolelwa hanyuma, wagamanye,

nawo ho wawejize i wana wawo.

7 Nawo ho wasume kumwitumbila Mulungu,

ho walece kugasemwa i metumo go Mulungu,

langa ho wagebatilile lukulu i malajilizo gakwe.

8 Walece kuwa nha vyono wali i walezi wawo,

wono wali wabedegazi kanyhi wafyuzi,

lulelo lono lusina wulunji we nyikala, wono wukamile,

u lulelo lono lusina wihuwilwa kuli Mulungu.

9 I wanhu wa Efulayimu, mwizuwa lyo wulugu,

wakatiza wakawuya ne nyuma,

akuno ho webate no wupinde hamonga ne zisonga mu mawoko.

10 Si wasaka kwibata cilagane co Mulungu,

kanyhi walema no kugatyatya malajilizo gakwe.

11 Wakasemwa zono Mulungu yali yetumile,

ze mapoto gono yali yawalajila.

12 Yanoza mapoto hali walezi wawo,

mwilolo lya Sowani mu yisi ya Misili.

13 Yalitemanula lilenga, yakawalowosa wene,

yakaganoza malenga geme wima nhe lijinji.

14 Mu mapango ge misi,

yawatonga ne livunde ninga mu cilo cose,

yawatonga no wuzelu wo moto.

15 Yagapandula matalawe mu nyika,

yakawang'wesa malenga, mu wulawa mu visolo.

16 Kanyhi yalavya malenga wulawa mwi'talawe,

yakagahulumya nha makolongo.

17 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

vyo wakacila du kumusutizya Mulungu,

sunga na mu nyika,

wakamufyulila u Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye.

18 Wakamujela Mulungu mu zinhumbula zawo,

wakusaka wapegwe ndigwa yono weneco wasakaga.

19 Wamulonga Mulungu wakutya,

“Alu mwene mbeka,

Mulungu kosuma kucipela ndigwa mu muno mono nyika?

20 Hamba yiwe, heya mbeka yatowa litalawe,

malenga gakahuluma nhe kolongo,

ninga aluno ma, mbeka kosuma kucipela ndigwa,

hamonga no kuwapela wanhu wakwe nyama?”

21 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yali yeza kuzihulika zizo,

yakawa kukolwa ne likuwo,

likuwo lyakwe likawakolelwa i wana wa Yakobo,

no kumwinucila Isilayeli,

22 vyono si wamwihuwila Mulungu,

hamba kwihuwila kose vyono kosuma kuwaponya.

23 Ninga hamba yiwe nhavivyo,

yagalajiliza mavunde ga kucanyha,

yakayidigula milango ya ku wulanga,

24 yakawagwisiza Mana ho walye,

yakawapela wuhemba wa ku wulanga.

25 Hodu wakalya ndigwa ye wamalayika,

nayo Mulungu, yakawapela ndigwa,

mu nghango nha vyono wasaka.

26 Yakayinoza mbeho ya kucilima yipute muno muno,

na kwe vilungo vyakwe, yakayitonga mbeho ya kutakama,

27 yakawaletela zindeje nyinji muno wanhu wakwe,

zikawa nyinji kotya musawawa wa mwi'lenga,

28 ne zindeje zizo zikagwa haciligati ye nghambi yawo,

zikapilima mahema gawo gose.

29 Ne wanhu wakalya wakeguta.

Mulungu yawapela cono wasakaga.

30 Ninga na lono wali wakali wena,

no wuhile wo kulya,

ne ndigwa lono yali yikali mu milomo yawo,

31 lo lulo baho likuwo lyo Mulungu,

likawacila, Mulungu,

yakawawulaga i wanhu,

wono wali wali vilungo muli wene,

yakawagwisa ne wazelelo wa Isilayeli.

32 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

vyo wakacila du kumusutizya Mulungu.

Si wehuwila yaye,

hamba yawe yali yetuma mapoto mawaha.

33 Holeka Mulungu nayo,

yakavidumulila i vilo vyawo nho muhe,

ne myaka yo wikale wawo,

mu wugaye wo kogofya.

34 Na mu nghango yono yajendelelaga kuwawulaga,

awalya wono wasigalaga,

wakajendelela kumupitucila,

wakanena wubi wawo wakamupitucila Mulungu,

kwe zinhumbula zawo zose.

35 Wakumbucila vyono Mulungu, yo mudimi wawo

na vyono u Mulungu Mudaha Vyose,

yo mukombozi wawo.

36 Ninga wamukonga Mulungu, na muli zose zono

wamulonjelaga, zali za wulambi du.

37 Hamba si wali wehuwilwa kuli mwene,

hamba si wasuma no kwibata cilagane cakwe.

38 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, Mulungu yakawa yali

lusungu, yakawanenela wubi wawo,

hamba si yawongomeza yaye.

Yeyimila likuwo lyakwe kali kenji, hamba si

yalileceleza likuwo lyakwe limukole muno.

39 Mwene yakumbucila vyono wene wawa wanhu du,

wehwanile mbeho yono yipulutaga yiwa kwajilila.

40 Wihwesaje ta,

kali kalinga wamufyulilaga mu nyika!

No kumukalaza mumo mu nyika!

41 Wakamujela Mulungu kali kenji muno muno,

wakamukalaza muno muno yuyo,

Mono Yelile wa Isilayeli.

42 Hamba kukumbucila i vilungo vyakwe,

hamba za mwizuwa alilya lono yawaponya,

mu miwoko ye walugu wawo,

43 lono yetumila mezino mawaha mu yisi ya Misili,

hamonga ne mapoto ga mwilolo lya Sowani!

44 Yagahundusa malenga ga mu makolongo,

gakawa sakami,

Wamisili ho walece kutuga malenga go kung'wa.

45 Yawagumilila mawumbi ge mahazi gono gawagaza,

hamonga ne mabula gono,

gawambuza wakatuga wambuce wuwaha.

46 Yakazikombola zi nzije,

zikalya vilimwa vyawo,

hamonga no kuyihodanga migunda yawo.

47 Yayihodanga migunda yawo ya mizabibu,

ne mvula ya mabwe,

hamonga ne mikuyu yawo ne mbeho nghali muno.

48 Yawulaga zing'ombe zawo,

ne mvula ye mabwe zingholo zawo,

yakaziwulaga ne limuli.

49 Yalileka likuwo lyakwe liwazize,

hamonga no wukali, ne simbili no wugaye,

hamonga ne litewa lye wamalayika wawulaji.

50 Hamba si yalimila likuwo lyakwe,

hamba si yenda kuwatetela yaye,

langa yawawulaga no wutamwa we lihuhuji.

51 Kanyhi yawawulaga i wana wose wa Misili,

i walongozi kulelwa, heya,

ndawililo ye vilungo vyawo mu mahema ga Hamu.

52 Hanyuma yawahamya wanhu wakwe,

nhe zingholo, yakawalongoza ku nyika,

nhe litewa lye zimitugo.

53 Yawajendya wapolile, hamba si wogopa yaye,

langa lilenga likawagubiciza walugu wawo.

54 Nayo yakawahamizya ku yisi yakwe,

yono yelile, mu matunda gono yagateka mweneco,

kwe vilungo vyakwe.

55 Yagawinga makungugo ku mwande yawo,

yakawapela Isilayeli yisi zawo ziwe zawo,

yawapela no wikalo we zimbeyu zakwe.

56 Hamba nhavivyo, wakacila kumujela,

no kumufyulila Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye,

hamba si webata zo wuhile wakwe yaye.

57 Wakawa wasina cihuliko, hamba kuwa wehuwilwa,

wakawa nha vyono wali wenha walezi wawo,

wakayumba-yumba nhe songa yono si nghangale.

58 Wamukalaza Mulungu,

mu wuhemba milungu yinji mu wuhembi wawo,

holeka wamunoza Mulungu yawe,

no wivu ku soko ye makumbicilo ge milungu yawo,

ye vihwani.

59 Nayo Mulungu yali yeza kuziwona zizo,

hodu yakakolwa ne likuwo,

no kumulemela lukulu Isilayeli.

60 Yakawuleka no wikalo wakwe wa ku Shilo,

wikalo wono yali yekaye muli wanhu.

61 Yakalileka ne Lisanduku lye Cilagane,

lisolwe ne walugu,

Lisanduku lyono lina vitanjilo vye vilungo vyakwe,

hamonga no lukumyo lwakwe.

62 Yawasimbila wanhu wakwe mweneco,

yakawakombola ho wawulagwe ne walugu wawo.

63 Ne wazelelo wawo wose, wakamalicila ku wulugu,

i wahinza nawo, wakekala si wawatujile,

wo kuwatola yaye.

64 I wakohani wawo nawo, wakawulagwa wose,

ne i wacekulu zawo, si wakombolwa ho wawalilile yaye.

65 Ninga ku ndumilizo yayo,

u Muwaha Mulungu yakenuka nha yakulawa mu nhongo,

nha citung'ati mono yasangalazwe ne divayi.

66 Yakawatowanga walugu wakwe,

no kuwagomola ne nyuma,

yakawakozeza cevu lukulu cibitilila.

67 Hamba nhavivyo ninga, yayilema mhuga ya Yusufu,

hamba si yawahagula wo mulango wa Efulayimu.

68 Langa yawahagula wo mulango wa Yuda,

kanyhi yakalyenda muno ne litunda lya Sayuni.

69 Nayo yakayizenga Nyumba yakwe kuko,

yikawa yinha nho wikalo wakwe ku wulanga,

yikawa yihwanile ne yinze,

yono yayisimya yikamile cibitilila.

70 Yamuhagula Dawudi mwitumami wakwe,

mono yamusola ku wulawa kwi dimo,

71 yamucema lono yali yali mu wudima litewa

lyakwe, yakamunoza ho yamudime Yakobo,

mono lye kungugo lyakwe,

yawadime Isilayeli wanhu wakwe.

72 Nayo Dawudi yakawadima,

mbeka kwe nhumbula yakwe yose,

hamonga no kuwatonga ko wunghula,

wuwaha muno muno.


Zabuli 79

Kulomba likungugo liponywe

1 Gwe go Mulungu,

i wanhu wono si wakumanyile, wenjila mu yisi yako.

Wayitipiza Nyumba yako yono Yelile.

Wayileka Yelusalemi yeza matongo.

2 Wayileka miwili ye wetumami,

wono wafwiye, yiligwe caka ne zindeje,

miwili ye wanhu wako,

yiwe ndigwa ye malimu ga mu mbago.

3 Sunga ne sakami yawo,

yimwajikanjice caka caka kotya malenga,

mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi,

ne miwili yawo mitagwe mumo,

hamba hasina munhu wo kuwazika.

4 Caweza wanhu mono cikutovulwa ne wekalani wayetu,

ne wekalani wayetu weza kucisekanga no kucizwajiza.

5 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu kosimba cibitilila?

Alu likuwo lyako lyokwaka nha moto sunga nili?

6 Wuwajidile likuwo lyako

i wanhu wono si wakumanyile,

sunga no wutemi wose,

wono siwukulyogopa litagwa lyako.

7 Hambi nha wawawulaga i wanhu wako,

wayihodanga ne yisi yako.

8 Wulece kucilipiciziza wubi we walezi wetu.

Cikulomba lusungu lwako lucizize mbela,

hambi nha cenamywa muno muno.

9 Gwe go Mulungu go muponyi wetu,

wucitaze ku soko ye lilumbo lyako.

Wuciponye no kucinenela no wubi wetu,

ku soko ye litagwa lyako.

10 Alo socici we makungugo wacilonje, wawe kutya,

“Alu Mulungu wenyu yali hayi lu?”

Wucitaze sunga ciwone ne meso getu co weneco,

vyono wukuwalipiciza wanhu we makungugo,

ku soko yo wita sakami ye wetumami wako.

11 Cililo ca wono wafunjigwe cikuficile,

na kwe vilungo vyako,

wuwaponye wono waling'anizwe wawulagwe.

12 Wuwalipaje kali mupunghati wawo,

wekalani wayetu, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko ye zinhovu zono wakutovuye gwegwe!

13 Nase co wanhu wako,

mono cili ngholo ze litewa lyako,

cokulumba cibitilila,

hamonga no kukukumya vilo vyose.


Zabuli 80

Wulombe wo kuligomola likungugo

1 Wucihulicize 'gwe go mudimi wa Isilayeli,

mono kuwatongaga wana wa Yusufu, nhe zingholo.

Agwe mono wikaye mwi'goda lyako lyo wuwaha,

mucanyha ye makelubi,

2 Wuwamulicilaje ku mwande wa Efulayimu,

Benyamini, na Manase.

Wulajile vilungo vyako, wuze wuciponye!

3 Gwe go Mulungu,

cikukunena wucilanje ko wuswanu wako,

nase coponywa.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go Mulungu we mihanga,

alo kojendelela sunga nili kuwusimbila,

wulombe we wanhu wako?

5 Alu wawunoza wukalale waweza yo ndigwa yetu,

kanyhi wacing'wesa mahozi ko winji muno.

6 Wagaleceleza makungugo gono gacipilime,

geza kuyihoma yisi yetu,

ne walugu wetu wakucitovulanga.

7 Gwe go Mulungu we mihanga,

wucilanje ko lusungu lwako, nase coponywa.

8 Waleta muzabibu wu monga ku wulawa Misili,

nagwe wukawawinga wanhu we makungugo ganji,

hanyuma yayo, wukawuhadika mu yisi yawo.

9 Wawuzuwila ho wuwiluce viswanu,

nawo wukota milela no kutandala mu yisi yose.

10 Wagagubika matunda no mubeho wayo,

ne manhambi gayo gakawa nha mihagata miwaha.

11 Ne manhambi gayo,

gakatandala sunga ko kwi'lenga lya Meditelaneya,

ne mahapilwa gayo,

nago, gakatandala gakafika ku nhumbi,

ye likolongo lya Efulata.

12 Alu socici, wukagakumula majinji,

gono gali gawupilime?

Alu yeza wo wose mono yakupuluta mu nzila yiyo,

yahepe zabibu zayo,

13 sunga ne zingubi za mu mbago,

nazo zawa kuwuhodanga,

ne vilimu vya mu mbago navyo, vyeza kuwulya.

14 Holeka go Mulungu we mihanga,

cikukunena wucilanje kanyhi!

Wucilanje ku wulawa ku wulanga,

wuze wuwudime wuwuno muzabibu!

15 Wono wawuhadika ko muwoko wako wo kulume,

na cico cinhambi cidodo, cono wacinozeza vilungo.

16 Na wawo walugu wetu,

wono wawudumulanga, no kuwuticimiza mu moto,

wuwalangaje ko wukali, no kuwongomezeza lukulu.

17 Muwoko wako wumucitile no kumudima munhu wako,

mono wamuhagula, ho yawe yakamile ku soko yako.

18 Nase hamba si cokulecela lukulu,

no kuwuya ne nyuma kanyhi,

Wuciwumifye du, nase cokukumya gwegwe.

19 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu we mihanga, wucigomole,

wucilanje ko lusungu lwako, nase coponywa.


Zabuli 81

Lwimbo lwe sikukuu

1 Mwimbilenyi zo lukumyo, u Mulungu wa Yakobo,

mumusangalalile Mulungu vyo vilungo vyetu.

2 Mwimbilenyi lwimbo lwe nyemo,

towenyi ne zing'oma,

towenyi masumbi ne zindonondo,

ze mazi maswanu.

3 Towenyi ndulele mu ndawo yo mwezi

na lono mwezi wukutula,

mu sikukuu yetu.

4 Ali lyo lilajilizo kuli Isilayeli,

na yiyo yo nghong'onheza yo Mulungu wa Yakobo.

5 Mulungu yakamupela sunga na Yusufu,

lilajilizo lilyo, lono yayihomela yisi ya Misili.

Nghuhulika lizi lyono si nghulimanya yaye, likutya,

6 “Ane nghayinenula mitegulo yako mu mawega gako,

nghakuwusiza kanyhi sunga ne mitegulo,

yono wali wudinghuye mu miwoko yako.

7 Na lono wanyhemeleza,

lono wali wuli mu wugaye, ane nakuponya.

Nane nghakupitula lono nali nihundice mwididimo.

Nghakujela ha malenga ga Meliba.”

8 Ale muhulicize zono nghumusugulenyi,

anye nyo wanhu wangu.

Yali yiwe ciba, hono wali wuhulice,

'gwe Isilayeli!

9 Wulecele lukulu kuwa no

mulungu wo wose kuli'gwe.

Wulecele lukulu kuhemba mulungu yunji.

10 Hambi ane, nha yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wako,

mono nakuwusa kulawa mu yisi ya Misili.

Holeka wahame mulomo wako, nane nokulisa.

11 “Ninga woyi hee!

Wanhu wangu hamba si wasaka kuhulika 'ne,

Isilayeli wakambedegala.

12 Hodu nane nghawa kuwaleka wajendelele,

no wukamu we zinhumbula zawo,

wetume zono wakuhila weneco.

13 Ninga yali yiwe ciba, wanhu wangu wali wahulice!

Yali yiwe ciba hono Isilayeli wali watyatye u wutonji wangu!

14 Nane nali niwawulaje lwa kamonga walugu wawo,

nali niwasumile lukulu, wono wakuwewonela.

15 Nawo wono wasulile, wali wafugamile ko woga wuwaha,

no wulipicizwe wawo, wali wuwe wa cibitilila.

16 Ninga nali nghulise agwe, ko wuhemba wuswanu,

kanyhi nali nghwigusye no wuci wa mwi'talawe.”


Zabuli 82

Mulungu yo mutemi we lilumbo

1 Mulungu yo muwaha we libalaza lya ku wulanga,

mwene kalamulaga hagati ye milungu.

2 “Lecelenyi lukulu ning'aniza,

yono si yili ling'ani!

Kanyhi mulecele lukulu kuwahijila mbejele,

no kuwendeza iwabi!

3 Watetelenyi wono wasoceye,

hamonga ne wana wono wasina walezi,

muwanozeze ling'ani waciwa na wono wasina cinji.

4 Waponyenyi wono wasoceye hamonga ne waciwa,

muwakomboze mu miwoko ya wono wakuwewonela.”

5 “Ninga 'nye hamba si mukutanga,

hamba kwizuka yaye!

Anye mwikalilaga mu wubocela mafupo du,

mwayihodanga sunga ne ling'ani yose mononze!

6 Ane nghatya, ‘Yo nyenye milungu,

anye wose muli wana wo Mulungu,

Mono Yalawiliciye.’

7 Ninga hamba yiwe nhavivyo, nanye mofwa du,

nhe wanhu wanji wose, no wumi wenyu woduma,

nha vyono wudumaga wumi we watemi wose.”

8 Gwe go Mulungu, wuze wuyiling'anize yinze,

hambi i makungugo gose, nha sawo yako gwegwe.


Zabuli 83

Wulombe ku soko ye walugu wa Isilayeli

1 Gwe go Mulungu, wulece kunyamala, wulece kutula,

hamba kunyamala twii 'gwe go Mulungu!

2 Lanje ta walugu wako vyono wasagahaye,

na vyono wakukubeza wono wakusulile.

3 Wakuhila wawasepe wanhu wako no kuwatamisa,

wakusaka wawatendele vibi wawo wono kuwadimaga.

4 Wakutya nhavi,

“Alenyi muze cilyongomezeze lukulu likungugo lyawo,

ne litagwa lya Isilayeli lyajililile lukulu.”

5 Nawo welumbanizize kumonga welunze ho walewane nagwe,

6 wanya Edomu, ne Waisimayeli,

Wamowabi, ne wanhu wa Hagali,

7 wanya Gebali, Amoni na Amaleki,

ne wanhu wa Filisiti,

sunga ne wekala ku Tulo wose.

8 Sunga ne Ashuli nayo yelunza nawo,

wakuwataza wana wa Loti!

9 Wuwanozeze nha vyono wawanozeza wanhu wa Midiyani.

Nha vyono wawanozeza wa Sisela,

na Yabini he kolongo lya Kishoni,

10 wono wawongomeza ku Endoli,

sunga ne miwili yawo yikawa mboleya ya yisi.

11 Wuwanozeze wawaha wawo,

nha vyono wawanozeza wa Olebu na Zebu,

ne watemi wawo wose, wawe nha Zeba na Zalumuna,

12 wono watya,

“Cokwisolela sese yisi yo Mulungu.”

13 Gwe go Mulungu wangu,

wuwanoze wawe nha manghundi,

wawe nhe mahanze gono gakupemheluswa ne mbeho,

14 nha vyono moto wuticimizaga mbago,

nha vyono malambi-lambi go moto,

gatakamizaga matunda.

15 Wuwawingaje wawo kwe cihumbi cako,

wuwogofye ne libeho lyako liwaha lyo mukukunzo,

16 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuwanozaje wawone cevu ciwaha,

ho wawulondole wuwaha wako.

17 Wawone cevu no kugazika cibitilila,

wakozwe soni no kwajililila lukulu.

18 Ho wamanye vyono yo gwegwe du wuli wene,

go Muwaha Mulungu,

Mulungu Mono Wulawiliciye mu yinze yose.


Zabuli 84

Nyhilu yo wusaka kuwa mu Nyumba yo Mulungu

1 Ane niyendile muno muno Nyumba yako,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga.

2 Nghusulumila niwe mu Nyumba yo Muwaha Mulungu.

Nane nghumusangalalila Mulungu,

mumi kwe nhumbula yangu,

na ko muwili wangu wose.

3 Sunga ne vikobobole vizenjile manhuli gavyo,

nazo zisamamba zina wikalo,

hanhu hono zikuwika zinyana zayo,

ha vikumbicilo vyako, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu we mihanga,

go Mutemi wangu no Mulungu wangu!

4 Wamoti wono wekalaga mu Nyumba yako,

mono wosuma kukukumya cibitilila.

5 Wo wamoti wono wakupegwa vilungo kulawa kwako,

wono wakusulumila kubita kwi'tunda lya Sayuni.

6 Nawo lono wakukolela mwilolo,

lyono linyalile lya Baka,

walinozaga liwa mivwilo,

ne mvula ye mhinga,

yilimemyaga makombo-makombo.

7 Nawo wajendelelaga du kutuga vilungo,

mu nghango vyono wakucila kujendelela ku mwande,

nawo womuwona Mulungu we milungu mu Sayuni.

8 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go Mulungu we mihanga,

wuhulice wulombe wangu, wuhulice

'gwe go Mulungu wa Yakobo.

9 Gwe go Mulungu wetu,

wumulanjilile mutemi wetu ko wuswanu wako,

heya, wumulanjilile viswanu mutemi mono wamuhagula.

10 Hambi lizuwa limonga,

hono litumamilwe mu Nyumba yako,

nha yili ciba muno muno,

si nha witumamila vilo elufu monga hanhu hanji,

hamba no wima ha mulango we Nyumba yako, du,

nha kuswanu muno kuciza wikalo,

wo kwikala hamonga ne wanhu wabi.

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yo mudimi wetu,

kanyhi yo mutemi wetu wo lukumyo,

mwene kacimotyaga vye lihewa lyakwe,

kanyhi yakucipela no lukumyo.

Muwaha Mulungu si yakuwa kuwema cinji co cose

ciswanu, wono wetumaga ze ling'ani.

12 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu we mihanga,

wo wamoti wono wakwitumbiye gwegwe!


Zabuli 85

Kulilombela likungugo wumoteswe

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, kwendezwa ne yisi yako,

kuwamotya kanyhi Isilayeli.

2 Kuwanenela wanhu wako wubi wawo,

wawusemwa wubi wawo wose.

3 Kuwimila wukali wako wose,

hamba si wukuwakolelwa likuwo kanyhi.

4 Wucigomole, 'gwe go Mulungu,

go Mukombozi wetu,

wulece kuciwonela likuwo kanyhi.

5 Alu 'mbi kojendelela kucisimbila cibitilila?

Alu mwene, wukali wako,

wojendelela sunga ku zindelo zose lo?

6 Alu hongo, si kocipela kanyhi mikalile mipya,

i wanhu wako ho wakusangalalile?

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wucilajile wuswanu wako,

wucipele ne mhonelo yako.

8 Nghuhulika zono yakutya Mulungu, u Muwaha Mulungu,

yakwihuwiza kuwapela mulamu wanhu wakwe wehuwilwa,

hono walece kuwuwuyila kanyhi wulele wawo.

9 Mbeka, mwene yewikanize kuwaponya wose,

wono wamogopile, no lukumyo lwakwe,

lokwikala mu yisi yetu.

10 Lusungu lwakwe ne mbeka yakwe vyokwitang'anila,

nayo ling'ani no mulamu vyokwikumbatila.

11 I mbeka yocindika mono yisi, iling'ani nayo,

yotima kulawa ku wulanga.

12 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, kociletela wumoteswe,

ne yisi yetu yocitujila ndigwa nyinji.

13 I ling'ani nayo yolongolela hali Muwaha Mulungu,

no kumunozelezeza nzila yakwe.


Zabuli 86

Kulomba wutazi

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuteje likutu lyako wuhulicize,

hambi ne nha sina cinji, kanyhi nili musocele.

2 Holeka wumhonyaje mwi'fwa,

hambi 'ne nha nghogopile gwegwe.

Wumhonye ane no mwitumami wako,

mono nghwitumbiye.

3 Agwe yo gwegwe go Mulungu wangu,

holeka wumbonele lusungu,

ane nghukulilila misi yose.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,
