wusangalaze ane no mwitumami wako,

hambi wulombe wangu nha wuli kwako du gwegwe.

5 Hambi agwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nha wuli muswanu,

kanyhi wuli munenezi nhondo zose,

kanyhi wuli lusungu kuli wose wono wakukulomba.

6 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuhulice wulombe wangu,

kanyhi wuhulice ne cililo co wulombe wangu.

7 Nane nghucemelezaga mu vilo vyo wugaye,

hambi 'gwe nha kuhulicizaga.

8 Gwe Muwaha Mulungu,

hasina mulungu mono yehwanile gwegwe,

hamba hasina mono kosuma kuzinoza,

zono agwe kuzinozaga.

9 I makungugo gose gono wagawumba,

gokuza no kukuhemba gwegwe,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nago gokwanza zo wuwaha we litagwa lyako.

10 Hambi nha yo gwegwe mukala wuwaha,

mono kunozaga mapoto mawaha,

agwe yo gwegwe Mulungu we ciwene.

11 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wunyijize ze nzila yako,

nane noyityatya ko wihuwilwa,

wuyitongaje nhumbula yangu yikogope gwegwe.

12 Nane nokulumba kwe nhumbula yangu yose 'gwe,

go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wangu,

nane nokwanza zo wuwaha we litagwa lyako cibitilila.

13 Hambi lusungu lwako kuli'ne nha lwinji muno!

Nagwe kumhonya nhumbula yangu mu citenjelo.

14 Gwe go Mulungu,

wanhu wedunhumuzi wose wamhinjiye nene,

lipuga lye wanhu wawulaji,

wono si wakogopile gwegwe wakusaka wambulaje.

15 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuli Mulungu wo lusungu, kanyhi wina lihewa,

agwe wuli mukangalizi muno,

kanyhi wumemile lusungu ne mbeka.

16 Wumhitucile kanyhi wumbonele lusungu,

wumhele vilungo vyako wumhonye,

hambi ane nha nghwitumamilaga,

nha vyono yaya yanozaga.

17 Wundajile citanjilo co wuswanu wako,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, awalya wono wasulile,

kolo weza kuwona vyono wakondya kunhaza,

no kusindamika, wene ho wapuwe.


Zabuli 87

Lukumyo kuli Yelusalemi

1 Mulungu yazenga likaya lya Sayuni,

yalizenga mucanyha ye litunda lyono lyelile.

2 Muwaha Mulungu, yalyendile likaya lya Sayuni,

kuciza wikalo winji wo wose wa Yakobo.

3 Ale ta wuhulicize zinghani zo lukumyo,

zono zikulongwa kuli'gwe, agwe gwe 'kaya lyo Mulungu.

4 “Mwi'walo lya wono wamanyile ane no Mulungu,

wali baho wanhu wa ku Misili na ku Babiloni.

Nhavivilya kanyhi na ku Filisiti na ku, Tulo na ku Etiyopiya,

wono wadahile kutegwa vyono,

‘Awa wo walelelwa ku Sayuni.’ ”

5 Ninga kuli za Sayuni yotegwa,

“Sayuni yo nyina yawo wose,

Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye yo kolikamya.”

6 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yo kowika liwalo lye makungugo,

nayo yo kowandika munhu na lwakwe yatye,

“Ayuno mulelelwa ku Sayuni!”

7 Nawo wose wakwidawala, akuno wakwimba wakutya,

“Agwe Sayuni yo gwegwe mwamulizo wetu.”


Zabuli 88

Kulomba wutazi

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go mhonelo yangu,

ane nikalaga du nghulila misi yose,

na ku cilo, nghuzizaga gwegwe.

2 Nghunena wulombe wangu wukuficile,

wuhulice ne cililo cangu, wunhaze,

3 Magayo menji gono gakondya kunyagana,

sunga neza nili behi no kufwa.

4 Ne vilungo vyangu vyose vyakondya kumalika,

nehwana na wose wono wakubita kwi'fwa.

5 Nakondya kulekwa muli wono wafwiye, nehwana,

na wono wawulagwe, wono wali mu vitenjelo,

wono agwe si kuwakumbucilaga kanyhi,

wono wali kutali no wudimi wako.

6 Wanhaga mu cisolo ce citenjelo,

mono mowuli wutitu, ne cisolo citali.

7 Likuwo lyako limvunile muno muno,

nagwe wunhyatiliye ne mawingo gayo.

8 Wanhemanula ne wendece wangu,

kunoza niwa nha cinhu cavu hali wene.

Alu mbopigwe, hamba si nosuma kwiwopolela.

9 Sunga ne meso gangu gatunduwaye,

ku soko yo wukalale.

Nane nghukulilila nhondo zose,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

akuno nghukwalila ne maganza gangu.

10 Wule, kosuma 'mbi kuwanozeza mapoto wono wafwa?

Ma, wono wafwiye wosuma kwinuka, ho wakukumye?

11 No lusungu lwako 'mbi lotambulwa mu citenjelo?

Hamba ne mbeka yako ma,

hambi yotambulwa kono kowali wafu?

12 Ma, i mapoto gako,

gowa gamanyice 'mbi mumo mu wutitu?

Hamba wuswanu wako nawo,

wowa wumanyice kuli wawo wono agwe wawasemwa?

13 Ninga ane nghulililaga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nane nghuletelaga wulombe wangu,

hakali mitondo nhondo zose.

14 Alu so cici wukunhaga,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wangu,

alo socici wukundeka?

15 Hambi ku wulawa wudodo wangu nha ngazagwa,

mbikanizizwe kufwa,

sunga nalu natimba so wugazwe wo wulipicize wako.

16 Likuwo lyako likali,

alu lyanyagana no wutowe wo wogofye wako,

alu wukunyongomeza.

17 Vyanhejela nha malenga misi yose,

vimhilime vyose hamonga.

18 Kunhemanula ne wendece wangu,

sunga ne wayangu wose,

alu'ne nekala mu citi du, sina yunji kanyhi.


Zabuli 89

Lwimbo lwa mwidaha lyo wugazice we likungugo

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane nokwimba zo lusungu lwako cibitilila,

nane nozanziza zindelo zose zo wihuwilwa wako.

2 Manyile nha vyono lusungu lwako,

lwikalaga cibitilila,

no wihuwilwa wako, wukamile kotya wulanga.

3 Wukawa kutya,

“Ane noza cilagane no muhagulwa wangu,

nimwilahila mwitumami wangu Dawudi.

4 Nane nomuwika wa mu lulelo lwako yawe mutemi vilo vyose,

kanyhi nowukangaza wutemi wako cibitilila.”

5 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulanga wokumya ze mapoto gako,

hamonga no wihuwilwa wako mu mhuga ya wono welile.

6 Alo nani 'mbi ku wulanga,

mono kokwihwananizwa no Muwaha Mulungu?

Hasina hamba monga mu viwumbwa vyose,

vya ku wulanga, mono kokwihwananizwa nagwe!

7 Agwe wogopwe mu wihangulize wa wono welile,

na wose wono wakupilime, wakukupela lilumbo,

kanyhi wakogopile.

8 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu go

Mulungu we mihanga,

Alo nani mbi mono yena vilungo,

nha gwegwe go Muwaha Mulungu?

Agwe mono wuli mwihuwilwa muli zose.

9 Agwe wutemile vilungo vye lilenga,

no kugatuza mawingo galyo kolo gakwinuka.

10 Agwe walipondanga lizoka Lahabu no kuliwulaga.

Wukawasumila walugu wako mu vilungo vyako.

11 Wulanga wo wako, kanyhi yisi yo yako,

Agwe wayiwumba yinze,

hamonga na vyose vyono vili mumo.

12 Wawumba kusumuka na kutakama,

ne matunda ge Taboli na Helimoni,

gakusangalalila litagwa lyako.

13 U muwoko wako wuli vilungo,

agwe wuli vilungo muno muno kuciza!

14 Hambi ze ling'ani no wuling'anizi,

nha wo musinji wo wutemi wako,

hambi lusungu no wihuwilwa wako,

nha vijeye mu witume wako.

15 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wo wamoti wanhu,

wono wakukuhemba gwegwe no kukwimbila,

wono wekaye mu wuzelu wo lusungu lwako.

16 Wena nyemo misi yose ku soko yako,

kanyhi wakukukumya ku soko ye ling'ani yako.

17 Hambi nha yo gwegwe nhojo ne vilungo vyawo,

na ko lusungu lwako wukucipela wusume.

18 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi u mudimi wetu nha muhagulwe na gwegwe,

agwe mono welile wa Isilayeli,

yo gwegwe mono wucipelile mutemi wetu.

Wihuwizo wo Mulungu kuli Dawudi

19 Katali wawalonjelela mu wugubulilwe,

wono welile wako, wukatya,

“Nimupela wutazi yunji mono yali vilungo,

nimutemya yunji mono nimuhaguye muli wanhu.

20 Nimuhagula Dawudi u mwitumami wangu,

niwa kumwitila ne mafuta gangu gono gelile.

21 U muwoko wangu we vilungo,

wowa wuli hamonga nayo vilo vyose.

Ne vilungo vyangu no mweneco, vyomusikamya.

22 Hamba ne walugu wakwe si womusumila lukulu,

ne wabi nawo, hamba si womugaza yaye.

23 Nane nowatijinha walugu wakwe,

nowongomezeza lukulu wose wono wamusulile.

24 Nowa mwihuwilwa kuli mwene,

kanyhi nomuwonela lusungu,

nayo kotujila wusume mwi'tagwa lyangu.

25 Nane nowutandaza wutemi wakwe,

wufice sunga ko kwi'lenga lya Meditelaneya,

nayo kotema sunga ko kwi'kolongo,

lya Efulata na Tigili.

26 Nayo kononjela yawe kutya,

‘Agwe yo gwegwe Baba,

kanyhi yo gwegwe go Mulungu wangu,

kanyhi yo gwegwe mono wuli mudimi wangu,

ne mhonelo yangu.’

27 Nomunoza yawe mwanangu mulelwa wangu mulongozi,

kanyhi kowa muwaha yacizize watemi wose wa mono'nze.

28 No lusungu lwangu lwa kuli mwene,

nolukamisa cibitilila,

ne cilagane cangu ca kuli mwene,

cokwikala cibitilila.

29 No lulelo lwakwe lwe citemi-temi,

lojendelela kutema cibitilila,

ne ligoda lyakwe lyo wutemi lyojendelela du,

nha vyono winha wulanga.”

30 “Ninga langa u lulelo lwakwe,

hono lulece kugebata i malajilizo gangu,

hono walece kuwa ne mikalile,

yono yiling'aniye ne malajilizo gangu,

31 na hono wawubene wulajilize wangu,

walece kuwa ne cihuliko kuli zo wutonji wangu,

32 baho langa ho nowatowela,

ku soko ye masutya gawo,

kanyhi nowalipiciza ku soko yo wubi wawo.

33 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

nojendelela du kumwenda Dawudi,

hamba si nowulecela lukulu,

u wihuwizo wangu kuli mwene.

34 Sinocibena cilagane cangu ca kuli mwene,

hamba sinoyipitulilila lukulu,

nghani yono namwihuwiza.”

35 “Nane ko wele wangu, nilahiza kamonga du,

na mbeka, hamba sinomukonga Dawudi yaye.

36 Lulelo lwakwe lokwikala cibitilila,

no wutemi wakwe wowa nhe lizuwa.

37 Kokwikala cibitilila nha vyono winha mwezi,

nha muhomelezi mwihuwilwa mu mwanya.”

Wukalale ku so wusumwe wo mutemi

38 Ninga lu 'gwe go Mulungu,

wumukoleywe likuwo muno,

u mutemi muhagulwa wako,

kanyhi wakondya kumutaga.

39 Waciwusa cilagane cono wacinoza,

no mwitumami wako,

wukawa kuwutaga mu nhope mukumbo wakwe wo wutemi.

40 Wagakumulanga majinji ge likaya lyakwe,

wukagabenanjiza ne magomawakali gakwe.

41 Alu wose wono wakukolela baho,

wakuyihiza sawo yakwe.

Ne wekalani wayagwe nawo, wose wakumusekanga.

42 Wawapela walugu wakwe cisumisumi,

kuwasangalaza walugu wakwe wose.

43 Kuvidujiza vitwalo vyakwe,

kumuleka yasumwe ne walugu wakwe mu wulugu.

44 Kumuwusiza wulajilize wakwe,

wo wutemi, kuwugwisa wutemi, wakwe.

45 Kumucepulila vilo vyakwe vyo wuzelelo,

wamupela cevu ciwaha.

Kulomba wukombozwe

46 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

alo kokwihundika sunga cibitilila?

Alo likuwo lyako lyokwaka sunga nili?

47 Ninga weze kukumbucila,

vyono nina vilo vicefu vyo wikale wangu,

go Muwaha Mulungu!

Nha yo gwegwe mono waciwumba,

kanyhi ho cifwe mwizuwa linji!

48 Alo nani 'mbi mono kokwikala yalece kufwa?

Hamba nani mwene mono kosuma kumuponya munhu ne lifwa?

49 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu lusungu lwako luli hayi?

Hamba wihuwizo wako wono wamwihuwiziza Dawudi,

ko wihuwilwa wako, alu luli hayi lo?

50 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wukumbucile ta,

vyono wanhovulanga ane no mwitumami wako,

na vyono nghugakangalila maligo,

ga wono si wakumanyile gwegwe.

51 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

walugu wako wakumusekanga mutemi wako,

muhagulwa wako.

Wakumuliganga ko kose kono yakubita.

52 Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe cibitilila!

Heya! Heya!


Zabuli 90

CITABU CE KANE

(Zabuli 90–106)

Mulungu we cibitilila no munhu

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe yo gwegwe kuwezaga wikalo wetu,

vilo vyose mu zindelo zose.

2 Agwe yo gwegwe go Mulungu,

we cibitilila ne cibitilila,

mono wali wuli baho du,

sunga na lono matunda gali gakali si nawa,

hamba yinze kuwumbwa.

3 Agwe kumulonjelaga munhu,

yawuye kono yalawilila,

kumupitulaga kawa manghundi.

4 Myaka elufu monga kuli'gwe,

yiwezaga nhe zuwa limonga,

yinha yigolo yono yapuluta,

yinha lidaha lidodo du lye cilo.

5 Kuwakwenghwelezezaga kutali nhe lihwazi.

Na hakali mitondo wawa wenha,

mahanze gono gakuwiluka.

6 Hakali mitondo gakusipuka no kudedewala,

ninga cinyamihe gakuwa gakondya kunyala.

7 Likuwo lyako likucongomeza,

cikogofywa no wukali wako.

8 Hambi'gwe nha wuwumanyile wubi wetu,

nagwe kuwanzalikaga wubi wetu ko wuzelu wako.

9 Na kwe likuwo lyako, wumi wetu wihemaga,

wuwa kumalika, nha vyono muhe wumalikaga.

10 Lunji cidahile kufisa myaka makumi mupunghati,

mu wikale wetu, na lunji makumi munana,

hono ciwe cina vilungo, ninga hamba nhavivyo,

mu myaka yose, cili mu magayo du no wukalale.

Navyo vilo, vikumalika mbela mbela,

nase cikupuluta musunho!

11 Alo nani 'mbi,

mono kosuma kumanya zo wukali we likuwo lyako?

Hamba nani lu mono yaziceleleye zo wukali wako?

12 Wucijize zo wucefu we vilo vyo wikale wetu,

ho cisume kuwa ne nhumbula ye mahala.

13 Ale wuwuye go Muwaha Mulungu!

Alo kojendelela kusimba sunga nili lo?

Wuciwonele lusungu ase co wetumami wako.

14 Wucigushe zo lusungu lwako hakali mitondo,

nase ho cisecelele,

no kusangalala vilo vyetu vyose.

15 Wucipele lu myaka minji yo wusangalale,

mu nghango ye myaka minji ye magayo

no wugazice, yono wacipela.

16 Wucilajile ze metumo gako ge vilungo,

ase co wetumami wako.

Wuwalajile wana wetu vilungo vye nhojo yako.

17 Gwe Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wetu,

wuciwonele lusungu wucipele cambuzi muli zose,

zono cikwituma.


Zabuli 91

U Mulungu yo Mudimi wetu

1 Hono wuwe wuli mu wudimi wo Mulungu,

Mono Yalawiliciye,

na hono wiwicile lukulu muli mwene,

mono yali vilungo,

2 kosuma kumulonjela Muwaha Mulungu wutye,

“Agwe yo gwegwe go wutizizo wangu,

kanyhi go wihundiko wangu, go Mulungu wangu,

mono nghwitumbiye!”

3 Mbeka u Mulungu kokuponya mu mutego,

kanyhi kokucitila na muli matamwa mabi.

4 Mwene kokuhundiciza ne mabawa gakwe,

nagwe kodimwa no mwene viswanu,

no muwoko wakwe wokudima no kukucitila.

5 Nagwe kowa kanyhi wusina soko yo kogopa,

wogofye wo wose ku cilo,

hamba lunji wuhungucilwe we nhinicizo ku misi.

6 Wusina soko yo kogopa lihuhuji,

lyono lidahile kwijela nhinicizo ne cilo,

hamba mabi ge liwala lyolyose lilya,

gono gadahile kukuziza mu madaha ge misi.

7 Hamba wawe wanhu elufu monga,

wono wafwiye wagwe behi nagwe,

hamba lunji wanhu elufu yikumi,

wagwe hali'gwe mbavu zose,

agwe hamba si kotamiswa yaye.

8 Nagwe kowawona ne meso gako gwegwe go mweneco,

i wanhu wabi vyono wakulipicizwa.

9 Agwe kumunoza Muwaha Mulungu wutizizo wako,

kanyhi kumusaka Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye yawe,

wo witumbilo wako.

10 Holeka nghani yo yose 'mbi, si yokwagana yaye,

hamba nghani yo yose ye mawulaji,

siyosejelela ha kaya yako.

11 Hambi Mulungu,

nha kowalajiliza wamalayika wakwe,

wakudime hanhu ho hose hono kowa kubita.

12 Nawo wokudinghulila mu miwoko yawo,

ho wulece hamba kwikobala mwi'bwe.

13 Nagwe kogakanyanjila masimba ne mazoka,

kozitijinha zinyana ze masimba ne mazoka.

14 Mulungu yakutya,

“Ane nomuponya mono yanyendile.

Kanyhi nomudima mono yakumanya vyono nili Mulungu.

15 Nane nomupitula lono yakunyhemeleza,

na lono yali mu wugaye,

ane nowa nili hamonga nayo.

Nomuponya no kumupela cogopo.

16 Nomupela vilo vinji vyo wikale wakwe mono'nze,

kanyhi nomuponya.”


Zabuli 92

Lwimbo lwo kumukumya Mulungu

1 Yo nghani swanu muno kukulumba agwe,

go Muwaha Mulungu, kwimba so cogopo cako,

'gwe go Mulungu Mono Wulawiliciye.

2 Yo nghani swanu kwanza zo lusungu lwako,

hakali mitondo, no wihuwilwa wako vilo vyose,

3 kuzanza ko wutowa masumbi,

hamonga no mulizo we ndonondo.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, i metumo gako mawaha,

nha gakusangalaza muno, nane nokwimba na nyemo mbaha,

ku soko ye metumo gako.

5 Hambi metumo gako nha mawaha

muno muno 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Sunga ne mahweso gako,

nha ga mahala menji muno muno!

6 U munhu mulele, hamba si kosuma kwizuka,

nayo muwula mahala,

hamba si kosuma kuyimanya nghani yiyi yaye,

7 nha vyono nawo i wabi,

wosuma kuwiluka nhe mahanze,

nawo wono wetumaga zo wubi,

vyono wosuma kumoteswa,

ninga hamba yiwe nhavivyo,

ndumilizo yawo wawo wose,

yowa wongomele we cibitilila,

8 Nagwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

yo gwegwe du Mono Wulawiliciye cibitilila.

9 Wawo i walugu wako go Muwaha Mulungu,

heya i walugu wako wose mbeka wokongomezwa,

na wose wono wetumaga zo wubi, wokwiyagala!

10 Agwe kumhela vilungo vinji,

vinha vilungo vye mbogo,

kunjidila mafuta go wusangalale.

11 Nane niwawona nene no mweneco,

i walugu wangu vyono wakondya kusumwa,

hulika ne cililo cawo i wetuma zimbi.

12 We ling'ani langa,

awo wakuwa kumoteswa nho mutende,

kanyhi wawilukaga wawa,

nho muhagata wa ku Lebanoni.

13 Wakuwa nhe mibici yono mihadikwe,

mu nyumba yo Muwaha Mulungu,

yikumota mu Nyumba yo Mulungu wetu.

14 Hamba wawe wakombipe,

ninga wojendelela kulela mana sunga ku wukombi,

womema moso, nawo wodedewala, no kuwa ne vilungo

ho wasume kulwanza u wiling'ano,

lo Muwaha Mulungu,

15 na vyono kanyhi, musina wubi wo wose kuli mwene,

mono wo wihundiko wangu.


Zabuli 93

Mulungu mutemi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yatemile yena ne nhojo yakwe!

Muwaha Mulungu yevwicile nhojo ne vilungo!

Nayo yayisimya yinze hanhu hayo,

hamba si yosinjisicila lukulu yaye.

2 Ne ligoda lyako lyo wutemi,

lyawikwa ku wulawa katali.

Nagwe wali wuli baho,

ku wulawa cibitilila ne cibitilila.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

visolo vye lilenga vinuye mazi gayo, heya,

vinuye mazi hamonga no mukukunzo wayo.

4 Muwaha Mulungu, yena wutemi wuwaha ku wulanga,

yali vilungo yacizize mawingo ge lilenga.

5 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulajilize wako wo we cibitilila,

ne Nyumba yako, yelile cibitilila ne cibitilila.


Zabuli 94

Mulungu yo muling'anizi wa wose

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go Mulungu mulipicizi, ale,

wulajile likuwo lyako!

2 Ale, winukaje wime wima agwe,

go muling'anizi we wanhu wose,

wuwalipicize wose,

i wedunhumuzi wono wasina cihuliko,

nha vyono yiwanojeye!

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu alu i wabi,

wojendelela kwikumya sunga nili lo?

Alu wajendelele kwiyikumya sunga nili?

4 Alu i wabi wojendelela kwikumya so wubi?

Hamba wokwidunhumula no kwikumya sunga nili?

5 Wakuwongomeza wanhu wako 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

no kuwewonela wawo wono yo sawo yako.

6 Wakuwawulaga i wacekulu wafwile,

hamonga ne wajenzi,

wakuwawulaga ne wana wono wasina walezi!

7 Wakutya ti, “Muwaha Mulungu si yakuwona yaye,

u Mulungu wa Yakobo siyamanyile yaye!”

8 Anye mo walele, mwihwesaje ta hadodo!

Anye nanye mono musina mahala,

alu ho motuga nili wizuce?

9 Alu mukwihwesa mono yalinoza likutu,

hongo si kosuma kuhulika yaye?

Hamba mono yaliwumba liso,

alu hongo si kosuma kuwona ng'o?

10 Mono yagatemile makungugo,

alu hongo si kosuma kulipiciza?

Hamba mono kafundaga wanhu,

wukwihwesa yasina wizuce lo?

11 U Muwaha Mulungu,

yagamanyile mahweso ge wanhu,

yamanyile vyono si gakwiliye hamba nhavi bwete.

12 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

yo mumoti munhu mono kumufundaga cogopo,

munhu mono kumwijizaga wulajilize wako.

13 Hambi nha komuceheza mu vilo vyo wugaye,

ninga i wanhu wabi wakuhimbilwa yikweme.

14 Muwaha Mulungu,

si kowalecela lukulu wanhu wakwe,

hamba si kowasemwa yaye wono yo sawo yakwe.

15 Holeka sunga ne ning'aniza,

yowa ye ling'ani,

nawo we ling'ani woyityatya.

16 Hamba nani 'mbi mono yema,

ho yanhaze kuli wetuma zimbi?

Hamba nani mono yema ku lubavu lwangu,

yanhaze kuli wetuma zo wubi?

17 Na hono Muwaha Mulungu yali yalece kunhaza,

mhali nakondya kubita ku yisi ye wafu.

18 Nali neza kutya, “Neza kugwa,”

lusungu lwako 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

lukasimya'ne wima.

19 No wuhangawe wa mu nhumbula yangu,

lono wukucila,

agwe kunhuzaga kanyhi kusangalazaga.

20 Agwe si kokwilumbaniza ne mahakimu,

wono si watyatyaga ze ling'ani,

wono wawuwikaga wulajilize mu nzila,

yo kuwutetela wubi.

21 Wene wawo welumbanizizaga mu zinzila,

zo kuwongomezeza wanhu we ling'ani,

no kuwaling'aniza wufwe,

wanhu wono wasina wusutye.

22 Ninga u Muwaha Mulungu wangu,

yo kanyhitilaga muli zose,

kanyhi wo wihundiko wangu.

23 Nayo kowagomolezeza weneco wubi wawo,

kanyhi kowapatila lukulu ku soko yo wubi wawo.

Heya, Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wetu, kowongomeza!


Zabuli 95

Lwimbo lwe malumbo

1 Muze cimwimbile Muwaha Mulungu,

cimusangalalile Mulungu we mhonelo yetu!

2 Cinjile kuli mwene ne zinyimbo zo wulumbe,

cimusangalalile ne zinyimbo zo lukumyo.

3 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo Mulungu Mono yalawiliciye.

Mutemi mono yali vilungo mu milungu yose.

4 Mwene yayitemile yinze yose,

sunga ne malolo hamonga ne vizuli vye matunda.

5 Lilenga lyakwe, yo mweneco, mono yalinoza.

Ne yisi nghavu, yo mweneco mono yayiwumba.

6 Nzonyi cifugame no kumuhemba,

cimufugamile Muwaha Mulungu,

Muwumbi wetu!

7 Hambi mwene nha yo Mulungu wetu,

nase cili wanhu mono mwene yakucidima,

ase cili ngholo zakwe zono yakuzilisa.

Holeka izuwali mulihulice lizi lyakwe,

8 “Mulece kuwa musina cihuliko,

nha vyono wali wenha i walezi wenyu ku Meliba,

nha vyono wali mwizuwa lilya ha Masa ku nyika.

9 Kuko kono walezi wenyu wanjela no kumhima,

hamba wawe wali wakondya kugawona metumo gangu,

gono nali nawatendela.

10 Na mu myaka makumi mane,

hamba si nendezwa nawo,

nghawa kutya, ‘Mbeka wanhu awa,

wo wanhu wono wajiliye!

Hamba si wakuwusacila lukulu no wutonji wangu!’

11 Hodu nane nghasimba,

nghawa kwilahiza nghatya,

‘Hamba si wokwinjilila lukulu kanyhi,

mu yisi yono nali niwapele wuceho!’ ”


Zabuli 96

Mulungu yo mutemi we lilumbo

(1Zin 16:23-33)

1 Mumwimbile Muwaha Mulungu lwimbo lupya!

Mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu mo we yinze wose!

2 Mumwimbile Muwaha Mulungu,

no kulikumya litagwa lyakwe.

Mwanze nhondo zose ze nghani yakwe swanu,

vyono mwene yo mhonelo yetu.

3 Mugalonjele makungugo zo lukumyo lwakwe,

hamonga na ze metumo gakwe,

mawaha kuli wanhu wose.

4 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo yalawiliciye nayo,

yo yakukumywa muno muno,

kanyhi yo yanojeye kogopwa muno,

kuciza milungu yose.

5 Milungu ye makungugo ganji si cinji yaye,

ninga Muwaha Mulungu yo yawumba wulanga.

6 Lilumbo no wuwaha vimupilime vilungo no wuswanu,

vimemile mu Nyumba yakwe.

7 Mumupelaje cogopo Muwaha Mulungu,

anye wose we zimhuga zose ze wanhu,

mupelenyi Muwaha Mulungu lukumyo no wuwaha wakwe.

8 Likumyenyi litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

mulete cilavywa co kwinjilila mu Nyumba yakwe.

9 Mumuhembe Muwaha Mulungu lono yakwilajila,

wucicime hali mwene, 'gwe go yinze yose!

10 Mugalonjele makungugo mutye nhavi,

“Muwaha Mulungu yo Mutemi!

Yayisimya yinze hanhu hayo,

hamba si yosinjisicila lukulu yaye.

Nayo kowaling'aniziza wanhu mu ling'ani.”

11 Sangalalenyi anye mo wulanga ne yinze!

Nalyo lilenga litowe cihumbi,

hamonga na vyose vyono vili mumo!

12 Nanye mo migunda musangalale,

hamonga na vyose vyono vili mumo!

Holeka sunga ne mibici yose ya mu mbago,

nayo yimbe ko wusangalale!

13 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kokuza no kuyitema yinze.

Heya, mwene kowatema wanhu,

mu ling'ani hamonga no wiling'ano.


Zabuli 97

Mulungu yo mutemi we lilumbo

1 Muwaha Mulungu yo yatemile!

Yinze yisangalale, nanye,

mo vizingwa sangalalenyi!

2 Mavunde ne citi ciwaha vimupilime, nayo,

yatemile ko wuswanu ne mbeka.

3 No moto wumulongoleye ku mwande yakwe,

wukuwaticimiza i walugu wakwe mbavu zose.

4 Ne mamuli gakwe gakuyimulicila yinze,

nayo yinze yikugawona yiwa kucicima.

5 Nago matunda gakwenjeduka kotya yihula,

ha mwande yo Muwaha Mulungu.

Heya, ha mwande yo Muwaha we yinze yose.

6 Wulanga wukwanza ze ling'ani yakwe,

ne makungugo gose gakuluwona lukumyo lwakwe.

7 Na wose wono wakuhemba vihwani,

wakukozwa cevu, heya,

sunga na wose wono wakwikumizya milungu,

yono yisina cambuzi,

milungu yose nha yimuhembaga Muwaha Mulungu du.

8 Wanhu wa ku Sayuni waseceleye,

ne makaya gose ga Yuda nago gakusangalala,

ku soko yo wuling'anizi wako go Muwaha Mulungu.

9 Hambi 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu

Mono wulawiliciye nha wutemile mu yinze yose,

kanyhi yo gwegwe du,

mono wuli vilungo muno muno wucizize milungu yose.

10 U Muwaha Mulungu, yawendile wono wawusulile wubi,

kanyhi mwene kawudimaga wumi wa wehuwilwa wakwe,

no kuwaponya mu vilungo vye wanhu wabi.

11 No wuzelu, wumumulicilaga munhu,

we ling'ani ne nyemo nayo,

kuli wanhu waswanu.

12 Nanye mo we ling'ani,

muzisangalalilaje zono Muwaha Mulungu yetuma!

Mukumbucile zo lukumyo lwakwe, mumulumbe!


Zabuli 98

Mulungu yo mutemi we yinze yose

1 Mumwimbile Muwaha Mulungu lwimbo lupya,

hambi nha ketuma nghani zo wizine!

Nayo ketujila wusume,

so vilungo no wuwaha wakwe vyono vyelile.

2 Muwaha Mulungu kalajila zo wusume wakwe,

kawanzalicila we makungugo,

ze vilungo vyakwe vye mhonelo.

3 Kawukumbucila wuswanu wakwe,

no wihuwilwa wakwe kuli wanhu wa Isilayeli.

Nazo zimbavu zose ze yinze zakondya,

kuwuwona wusume ne vilungo vyo Mulungu wetu.

4 Yinze yose yimusangalalile Muwaha Mulungu,

yimukumye kwe zinyimbo ne zinyhenze.

5 Mumukumye Muwaha Mulungu kwa nyemo,

kwe lizi liswanu hamonga ne masumbi.

6 Mutowelenyi zinyheze Muwaha Mulungu mutemi wetu,

musangalalilenyi kwa ndulele,

hamonga ne mazi ge zifilimbi.

7 Nalyo lilenga litowe cihumbi,

hamonga na vyose vyono vili mumo,

yinze hamonga na wose wono wekaye mugati yayo.

8 Nanye mo makolongo, towenyi maganza,

nanye mo matunda, mwimbe hamonga ne nyemo.

9 Sangalalenyi hali Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha yakuza kuyitema yinze.

Nayo koyiling'aniza yinze mu ling'ani,

i wanhu nawo kowatemela mu ling'ani.


Zabuli 99

Mulungu yo mutemi we lilumbo

1 Muwaha Mulungu yo yatemile,

nago makungugo gakucicima!

Mwene yekaye mu makelubi,

nayo yinze yikusinjisika!

2 Muwaha Mulungu yo yalawiliciye mu Sayuni,

nayo yali mucanyha ye makungugo gose.

3 Wanhu wose walikumye litagwa lyakwe,

lyono lilawiliciye kanyhi lyo wogofye.

Hambi Mono Yelile, nha yo mweneco du!

4 Gwe go mutemi mono wulawiliciye,

go mwenda wiling'ano!

Kuwukangaza wiling'ano muli Isilayeli,

kuleta wiling'ano ne ling'ani.

5 Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

u Mulungu wetu, mugwe funama hali mwene.

Hambi Mono Yelile, nha yo mweneco du!

6 Nawo Musa na Haluni wo wali wakohani wakwe,

nayo Samwili yali yali mu mhuga,

ya wono wamulililaga Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo yakacihulika cililo cawo.

7 Nayo Mulungu yakalongana nawo,

ku wulawa mwi'vunde,

nawo wakawukamisa muno wuhile wakwe,

hamonga ne malajilizo gono yawapela.

8 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wetu,

agwe wawahuliciza. Ninga kuli wene,

agwe wukawa Mulungu munenezi,

hamba yiwe nha wawalipiciza,

ku soko ye masutya gawo.

9 Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu,

muhembe mwi'tunda lyakwe lyono lyelile!

Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu mwene,

yo Mulungu mono yelile.


Zabuli 100

Lwimbo lwo lukumyo

1 Mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

'nye nyo we yinze wose!

2 Mumuhembe Muwaha Mulungu ne nyemo,

mumubitile akuno mukwimba no wusangalale!

3 Mumanye vyono Muwaha Mulungu, yo Mulungu du.

Yo mweneco mono yaciwumba, nase cili sawo yakwe,

cili wanhu wakwe,

kanyhi cili ngholo ze madimo gakwe.

4 Mwinjilaje mu milango ye Nyumba yakwe,

akuno mukulumba,

mwinjilaje mu wikalo wakwe no wulumbe.

Mumupelaje lilumbo, no kulikumya litagwa lyakwe.

5 Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha muswanu,

no lusungu lwakwe lwikalaga cibitilila,

ne mbeka yakwe yikalaga mu zindelo zose.


Zabuli 101

Wihuwizo wo mutemi

1 Lwimbo lwangu ku soko yo wihuwilwa ne ning'aniza,

nane nolwimba so lilumbo lyako 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu.

2 Ne mikalile yangu yowa yisina yilema lyo lyose.

Alu 'gwe ma, konyiziza nili?

Mu kaya yangu, nokwikala mu mikalile,

yono yikwilaniciye,

3 hamba sinoyikangalilila nghani yo yose yimbi.

I mijendele ye wanhu wabi, ane si niyendile yaye.

Sunga ne mikalile yawo, ane si noyityatya ng'o.

4 Ne nzila yo yose yo wajilile, yeze yili kutali nane,

nane nokwilega no wubi we mawala gose.

5 Nowapezezya lukulu,

wose wono wendile kuwaheha wayawo,

hamba si nosuma kuwakangalila,

i wedunhumuzi, ne wepelelezi.

6 Wanhu wehuwilwa, awo langa wo wayangu,

ne wetumami wangu,

wowa wo walya wono wasina wubi.

7 No munhu wo wose mono mulambi,

ayo si kosuma kwikala mu kaya yangu,

hamba mukoci wo wose si cokwikalana nayo yaye.

8 Nane nhondo zose, nowongomezeza lukulu,

i wabi wose mu yisi yetu,

nowawusiza lukulu i wabi wose,

mwi'kaya lyo Muwaha Mulungu.


Zabuli 102

Lilombo lyo muzelelo mono yali mu wugaye

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuhulice wulombe wangu,

ne cililo cangu cikuficile.

2 Wulece kuwa kutali nane lono nili mu wugaye!

Wuteje likutu lyako, wuhulice mbela lono nghulomba!

3 I vilo vyangu nha vikupuluta mbela mbela nha lyosi,

ne mafupa gangu gakwaka nha moto.

4 Natemangwa, nawa nhe mahanze gono ganyalile.

Nawa sina hamba nzala ya ndigwa.

5 Ku soko ye lizi lyo wugule wangu,

mafupa gangu ganamatiye ne nghuli yangu.

6 Nehwana nhe zindeje za mu nyika,

kanyhi nhe zinhuwi mu matongo.

7 Nghugona nuguye du, nihwanile,

nhe ndeje nyhiwa,

yono yili mwi'tembe mucanyha.

8 Nawo walugu wangu,

wekalaga wakusimanga misi yose.

Nawo wono wakunzwajiza,

wakwitumamila litagwa lyangu ko kulilijitila.

9 Na ku soko yo wukali we likuwo lyako,

mavu gaweza nha yo ndigwa yangu,

10 Nane nagahanza-hanza mahozi gangu,

na co kung'wa cangu.

Nagwe kunhondola no kunhaga kutali.

11 No wumi wangu, wihwanile,

nho mubeho we cinyamihe,

nghunyala nhe mahanze.

12 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wutemile cibitilila,

ne litagwa lyako lyokumbucilwa mu zindelo zose.

13 Agwe kokwinuka,

koliwonela lusungu likaya lya Sayuni,

hambi cipango nha cafika cono cinojeye,

co lusungu lwako,

naco aci co cipango cono cinojeye muno.

14 Ninga wetumami wako,

walyendile likaya lilyo lya Sayuni,

hamba liwe lyawa matongo gose,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

wakuliwonela lusungu du,

hamba liwe lyakondya kuweyanjika muno.

15 Nago makungugo, gomogopa Muwaha Mulungu,

ne watemi nawo, wose wolwogopa lukumyo lwakwe.

16 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

kolo yakondya kulizenga,

kanyhi likaya lya Sayuni,

baho langa ho kokwijelela,

vyono mwene yo wo lukumyo.

17 Nayo kowuhuliciza wulombe wo muciwa,

no wulombe wawo hamba si kowulema yaye.

18 Ale, wuzandikaje zizino,

ku soko yo lulelo lono lukuza,

i wanhu wono wolelwa hanyuma,

nawo wamukumye Muwaha Mulungu.

19 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

ku wulawilila hono helile hakwe, yakalanga hasi,

ku wulawilila ku wulanga, yakayilanga yinze.

20 Yakacihuliciza cililo ca wono wafunjigwe,

yakawawopolela wono wali waling'anizwe kufwa.

21 Ne litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

lyokwanzwa ku Sayuni, heya,

sunga no lukumyo lwakwe,

nalo lokwanzwa ku Yelusalemi,

22 mwipango lono makungugo gose,

gokwikongholelela hamonga,

ne matemi gamuhembe Muwaha Mulungu.

23 Mulungu kanyhepulila vilungo vyangu,

lono nghali muzelelo,

kacepula vilo vyo wikale wangu.

24 Gwe go Mulungu wangu,

wulece kusola lulu lono nghali muzelelo.

Gwe go Mulungu, yo gwegwe,

mono kwikalaga cibitilila,

25 wayiwumba yinze katali na katali,

wulanga wo mulimo we miwoko yako.

26 Azo zokwajilila no kusakala nha mwenda,

nazo zopuluta no kwajililila lukulu,

ninga agwe kosigala baho du vyo winha.

27 Hambi agwe, nha yo gwegwe du cibitilila,

no wikale wako wusina ndumo.

28 Ne wana wetu wokwikala wapolile,

ko wudimi wako, ne wana wawo nawo,

wowa wakamile hali'gwe.


Zabuli 103

Wendo wo Mulungu

1 Gwe go nhumbula yangu,

wumukumye Muwaha Mulungu.

Na vyose vyono vili muli'ne,

vilikumye litagwa lyakwe lyono lyelile!

2 Gwe go nhumbula yangu,

wumukumye Muwaha Mulungu!

Wulece kuwusemwela lukulu wuswanu wakwe.

3 Mwene kanenela wubi wangu wose,

kanyhi kamhonya ne matamwa gose.

4 Kamhonya mu wulawa mwifwa,

yakamhela ze lihewa no lusungu lwakwe.

5 Ne mikalile yangu yose kayimemya wumoteswe,

no wuzelelo wangu wukusipulwa,

ho njendelele kwikala nili muzelelo du,

kanyhi nili vilungo nha'ndi.

6 Muwaha Mulungu kaling'anizaga mu ling'ani,

kawanozaga wasocele, ho watuje ling'ani yawo.

7 Yamumanyiza Musa zinzila zakwe,

yawalajila wanhu wa Isilayeli ze metumo gakwe.

8 Muwaha Mulungu yo munghala lihewa no lusungu,

mwene si kakolwaga ne likuwo mbela,

kanyhi yena lusungu lwinji muno.

9 Mwene si kakalipilaga-kalipilaga nhondo zose,

hamba ne likuwo lyakwe si likalaga cibitilila yaye.

10 Kanyhi si yakuwa kucilipiciza,

nha vyono yicinojeye a a ng'o!

Hamba si kacilipaga mu nghango ye masutya getu.

11 Nha vyono winha wulanga,

wuli kutali ne yinze, ale,

no wuswanu wakwe vyo winha kuli wono wamogopile.

12 Nha vyono yinha kucilima,

na kumwezi vili kutali kutali, ale,

nayo Mulungu,

vyo kawuwikaga kutali nase wubi wetu.

13 Nha vyono sogo yo munhu,

kawawonelaga lusungu wanagwe, ale,

nha vivyo no Muwaha Mulungu nayo,

vyo kawawonelaga lusungu wose wono wamogopile.

14 Hambi Mulungu nha yacimanyile wimo wetu,

yamanyile vyono ase cili manghundi du.

15 Munhu nha yehwanile mahanze du,

kasanulaga nhe liluwa lya mu mugunda,

16 ninga kolo mbeho yikuliputa, hodu,

liwa kwajilila du, hamba kwijela,

kanyhi na hono lyali holili.

17 Ninga lusungu lo Muwaha Mulungu,

lwikalaga cibitilila,

kuli wose wono wamogopile,

no wuswanu wakwe wikalaga mu zindelo zose,

18 kuli walya wose wono webate cilagane cakwe,

no kugetuma malajilizo gakwe ko wihuwilwa.

19 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yaliwicile ku wulanga,

ligoda lyakwe lyo wuwaha, nayo yo yatemile muli vyose.

20 Nanye nyo wamalayika mono muli vilungo,

mumukumye Muwaha Mulungu,

mukumyenyi anye mono mwibate ze malajilizo gakwe,

mono mwibate ne nghani yakwe!

21 Nanye nyo we vilungo ku wulanga,

mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

mukumyenyi anye nyo wetumami wakwe,

anye mono mwibate zo wuhile wakwe!

22 Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

nanye nyo viwumbwa vyakwe vyose,

mukumyenyi anye wose mono muli mu wutemi wakwe.

Gwe go nhumbula yangu, mukumyaje Muwaha Mulungu!


Zabuli 104

Kumukumya muwumbi

1 Gwe go nhumbula yangu, wumukumye Muwaha Mulungu!

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wangu,

agwe yo gwegwe Mono wulawiliciye muno muno!

Kanyhi wivwicile nhojo ne lilumbo.

2 Wipilimye wuzelu nhe bulanjeti,

kanyhi wuwutandaze wulanga nhe hema,

3 wuzenjile wikalo wako,

mucanyha ye malenga mu wulanga,

wunozize mavunde gawa wo mutuka wako,

kugulucilaga mucanyha ye zimbawa ze libeho.

4 Kuyinozaga mbeho yiwa yo munhu wo kutuma,

u moto nawo hamonga ne malambi gayo,

kawa yo mwitumami wako.

5 Wuyisimizye yinze mu musinji wayo,

ho yilece kusinjisika cibitilila.

6 Nagwe wukayigubiciza yinze,

ne lilenga nha civwalo,

ne malenga nago, gakagagubiciza,

sunga ne matunda matali.

7 Nagwe wali weza kugakalipila malenga,

hodu gakawa kutiza lumangho,

gali geza kulihulika lizi lyako du,

gakakulumhuka lumangho.

8 Gakawa kuhuluma ku wulawilila,

mu matunda sunga mu malolo,

gakawa kubita sunga,

i hanhu hono wagawikaniziza.

9 Wagawicila mupaka, malenga gago,

ho galece kulapuka no kuyigubika kanyhi yinze.

10 Walavya mivwilo mu malolo,

yikolele muciligati ye matunda.

11 Ne mivwilo yiyo,

yizing'wesaga, zinimu za mu mbago.

Ne zinyhenje nazo,

mumo mo zidumulilaga nyhilu yazo.

12 Sunga ne zindeje nazo, zizengaga manhuli gazo,

mu mibici yono yili baho,

kanyhi zitimaga mu manhambi gazo ziwa kwimba.

13 Kanyhi kugatonyezezaga mvula,

i matunda ku wulawa kucanyha,

kuwa kuyimemya yinze no wumoteswe wako.

14 Kucindyaga mahanze, ndigwa ye vilimu,

ne vilimwa viwe ndigwa ye wanhu,

nayo munhu ho yetujile ndigwa yakwe,

ku wulawa mu muhangha,

15 nha cinji, divayi yono yomusangalaza,

ne mafuta gono gomupela nyemo,

sunga ne ndigwa yono yomupela vilungo.

16 Sunga ne mibici miwaha yo Muwaha Mulungu,

yose yikutuga malenga gono gakwiliye muno muno, heya,

sunga ne mibici ye mihagata ya ku Lebanoni,

yono yayicindya mweneco.

17 Ne zindeje nazo, zizengaga mumo manhuli gazo,

ziyobwa nazo, zinozaga wikalo wazo mu misonobali.

18 Na mu matunda matali,

mumo wo wikalo we zimhene za mu mbago.

Na mu matalawe namo, wo wutizizo we zimhimbi.

19 Agwe go Mulungu,

wawuwumba mwezi wucipimile mapango,

ne lizuwa nalyo, limanyile lyeneco,

lipango lyayo lyo wugwile.

20 Agwe kuletaga citi, ne cilo ciwa kuza,

ne zinyana ze zisimba nazo zikolomaga,

zituje mapelembo gazo.

21 Nazo zose zimulindilaga du,

Mulungu yazipele ndigwa yazo.

22 Nalyo lizuwa kolo likusosomoka du,

hodu nazo ziwa kuwuya kuceha,

mu zimhango zayo.

23 Ale, baho nayo munhu ho kawa kwinuka,

no kubita kwanza milimo yakwe,

nayo yakwituma sunga lizuwa ligwila.

24 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, milimo yako,

nha minji muno kanyhi yo wizine muno muno!

Wavinozeza vinhu vyose mu mahala mawaha!

Ne yinze yimemile viwumbwa vyako du!

25 Na kuko ko lili lilenga yiwaha,

kanyhi yigazi muno, na mugati yalyo,

vikalaga viwumbwa vinji muno,

vyono hamba ne liwalo lyavyo,

si limanyice yaye,

i viwumbwa vyumi, viwaha ne vidodo.

26 Sunga ne zingalawa zikolaga mumo mucanyha yalyo,

sunga ne lilimu lyono likucemwa Lewiyatani,

lyono waliwumba, nalyo lidawalaga mumo.

27 Ne viwumbwa vyose nha vikwitumbiye gwegwe du,

ho wuvipele ndigwa mu madaha gayo.

28 Navyo vilizaga co cose du cono kovipela gwegwe,

na lono wukufumbatula muwoko wako,

vyene vikwiguta vyono vijinile.

29 Na hono wihundice hadodo vivi du,

hodu vyene vyagaya,

na hono wuviwusize muhe wavyo wo wumi,

hodu vyofwa lwa kamonga,

no kuwuya mu manghundi mono vyalawilila.

30 Ninga hono wuviputile muhe wako,

hodu vikuwumipa kanyhi.

Agwe yo gwegwe mono kuyisipulaga yinze,

yiwa kutuga futa mhya.

31 Lukumyo lo Muwaha Mulungu lwikale cibitilila,

Muwaha Mulungu, yendezwe ne metumo gakwe mweneco.

32 Mwene kayilangaga du,

yinze nayo yiwa kucicima caka,

kanyhi kagapamizaga du matunda,

nago gawa kulavya lyosi!

33 Nane nowa kumwimbila du Muwaha Mulungu,

mu mikalile yangu yose,

na mu wikale wangu wose, wa mononze,

nowa kumukumya Mulungu wangu.

34 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

u lwimbo lwangu luluno lukwendezaje,

hambi i nyemo yangu yose nha yilawaga kwako.

35 Wanyo wubi wose, wali wawuswe ta mono'nze si,

ho halece kuwa no wavu wo wose kanyhi!

Gwe go nhumbula yangu, wumukumye Muwaha Mulungu!

Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!


Zabuli 105

Mulungu ne wanhu wakwe

(1Zin 16:8-22)

1 Mulumbenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

mwanze zo wuwaha wakwe,

mugalonjele makungugo zono yetumile!

2 Mumusangalalile,

mumwimbile Mulungu zo lukumyo,

musimulile ze mezino gose gono yetuma!

3 Mwilumbilaje litagwa lyakwe lyono lyelile,

nawo wono wamogopilo Muwaha Mulungu,

yiwanojeye wasangalale.

4 Mubitilenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha yo yali vilungo.

Mulondolenyi mweneco du vilo vyose.

5 Nanye mo lulelo lwa Abulahamu mwitumami wakwe,

sunga nanye mo lulelo lwa Yakobo muhagulwa wakwe,

6 mukumbucile ze metumo gakwe mawaha gono yetuma,

sunga ne mapoto hamonga no wuling'anizi

vyono yawulavya.

7 Muwaha Mulungu, yo Mulungu wetu,

ne malajilizo gakwe gali vilungo mono'nze mose.

8 Nayo kacikamisaga cilagane cakwe cibitilila,

kanyhi kawufisilizaga wihuwizo wakwe,

kuli zindelo zose.

9 Mwene kacikamisaga cilagane,

cono yalagana na Abulahamu,

sunga no wihuwizo wono yamwihuwiziza Isaka.

10 Yamukangaziza Yakobo wihuwizo wakwe,

kanyhi yakamwilahila vyono cilagane cico,

cokwikala cibitilila.

11 Mulungu yakatya, “Nomupelenyi yisi ya Kanani,

nayo yowa yisi yenyu nyenye nyo weneco.”

12 Wanhu wo Mulungu, wali wacefu du,

kanyhi wali wajenzi mu yisi ya Kanani.

13 Wapilima-pilima ku wulawa likungugo ali na ali,

na kanyhi ku wulawa yisi ayi na ayi.

14 Ninga hamba wawe wapilima-pilima nhavivyo,

Mulungu si yawaleka watamiswe,

na cikali co cose yaye, Mulungu

yawapela suguzi watemi ku soko yawo, yakutya,

15 “Mulecele lukulu kuwapamiza,

wawo wetumami wangu wahagulwa,

hamba mulece kuwatamisa na cikali co cose,

wawo wawizi wangu!”

16 Mulungu yakayiletela nzala yisi yawo,

yakayihodanga ndigwa yawo yose.

17 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

yakatuma munhu ku mwande yawo,

mono yacemwaga Yusufu,

mono yaguzwa ku wuwanda.

18 Wakamuwopa Yusufu ne manyolo mu migulu yakwe,

na mu singo, wakamuvwika lubano lwa cuma,

19 sunga cali ceza kufisilizwa cono yaciwila.

Ne nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu,

yikamuhomelela vyono yo nhavivyo.

20 Nayo mutemi we Misili,

yakalajiliza vyono Yusufu yawopolelwe,

sunga muwaha we wanhu, yakamukombola.

21 Yakawa kumunoza yawe,

muwaha mu wutemi wakwe wose,

kanyhi yawe yo mulanjilizi,

muwaha we sawo yakwe yose,

22 ho yawatonje wetumami wakwe,

nha vyono yendile mweneco,

kanyhi yawejize wanyamhala wakwe ze mahala.

23 Hanyuma yayo, i wanhu wa Isilayeli nawo,

ho wakaza mu yisi ya Misili,

Yakobo yakawa mujenzi mu yisi yiyo ya Misili.

24 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yakawanoza wanhu wakwe,

welele muno muno kanyhi yakawapela wuwe,

wo kuwa wali vilungo,

muno kuciza vilungo vye walugu wawo.

25 Yakazipitula zinhumbula ze Wamisili,

ho wawasule wanhu wakwe,

wakawatendela ko wungwele wanhu wakwe.

26 Hanyuma yayo, yakamutuma Musa mwitumami wakwe,

yakamutuma na Haluni mono yamuhagula mweneco.

27 Wakanoza vitanjilo vyo Mulungu hali Wamisili,

sunga ne mapoto mu yisi yiyo ya Misili.

28 Nayo Mulungu yakaleta citi mu yisi yose,

ninga Wamisili wakabeza ze malajilizo gakwe.

29 Yakagahundusa malenga gawo gakawa sakami,

yakazikopola zisomba zawo zose.

30 Yisi yawo yikamema mabula gose,

sunga na mukaya yo mutemi namo gakamema nhavivyo.

31 Mulungu yakalonga,

gakawa kuza mawumbi menji muno ga mahazi,

hamonga ne makutupa, mu yisi yawo yose.

32 Hanhu ha mvula,

Mulungu yakawatonyezeza mvula ye mabwe,

hamonga ne mamuli gono gayimulicila yisi yawo yose.

33 Yakayihodanga mizabibu yawo hamonga ne mikuyu yawo,

yakayibenanga sunga ne mibici yawo yose.

34 Mulungu yakalonga, nzije zikaza,

hamonga ne mang'ungu menji muno gono gasina yiwalo,

35 Nago gakawa kulya vilimwa vyawo vyose,

vya mu yisi yawo, gakalya sunga ne mikalo yawo yose.

36 Mulungu, yakawawulaga i wana we cilume wose,

i walelwa walongozi wose wa mu wana we Wamisili.

37 Hanyuma yayo,

yakawa kuwawusa wanhu wa Isilayeli,

mu yisi ya Misili,

nawo wakawuka wena ne zipesa,

hamonga ne zizahabu,

kanyhi wose wawuka wali vilungo du,

hali hasina hamba yumonga mono yali yatimbile.

38 Nawo Wamisili wakasangalala muno so wuwuce wawo,

hambi nha wali webatwa no woga ku soko yawo.

39 Mulungu yakatandika livunde,

hacanyha ye wanhu wakwe,

no moto wo kuwamulicila ku cilo.

40 Wene wakamulomba Mulungu,

nayo Mulungu yakawaletela nghwale,

kanyhi yakawegusya ne ndigwa ya ku wulanga.

41 Yakalidigula litalawe,

ne malenga gakapulinhika,

nago gakahuluma nhe kolongo mu nyika.

42 Mulungu yakakumbucila,

cilagane cakwe cono celile,

cono yalagana no mwitumami wakwe Abulahamu.

43 Holeka yakawawusa wanhu wakwe,

mu yisi ya Misili, nawo i wahagulwa wakwe,

wakemba no kusangalala.

44 Mulungu yakawapela,

ne zisi za we makungugo ganji,

yakawapela ne migunda ye wanyawusi,

45 wanhu wakwe ho wagebate malajilizo gakwe,

watyatye ze mejizo gakwe.

Yalumbwe Muwaha Mulungu! Haleluya!


Zabuli 106

U wuswanu wo Mulungu kuli wanhu wakwe

1 Yalumbwe Muwaha Mulungu!

Mulumbenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha muswanu,

no lusungu lwakwe nha lwikalaga cibitilila!

2 Hambi nani mwene mono kosuma,

kuzilonga zimbaha zose zono yetuma?

Hamba nani lo mono kosuma,

kuzilolosa zo lukumyo lwakwe?

3 Wo wamoti wono webate malajilizo gakwe,

wono wakwituma nhondo zose zo mugoloka.

4 Ale, wunghumbucilaje na nene 'gwe,

go Muwaha Mulungu, lono wukuwataza wanhu wako,

wuhanzilizaje nawo, lono wukuwaponya,

5 nane ho sume kuwuwona,

wumoteswe we wahagulwa wako,

nane ho niyisangalalile nyemo,

ye nghungugo yako,

kanyhi ho niyilumbile lukumyo,

hamonga ne wanhu wako.

6 Cinoza wubi nhe walezi wetu,

cituma zo wavu, cituma zimbi.

7 Walezi wetu lono wali wali ku Misili,

hamba si wazicelelela ze mapoto go Mulungu,

hamba kukumbucila zo lwinji lo lusungu lwakwe,

lono yawalajilaga, wakamubeza Mulungu

ha nhumbi ye lilenga lya Shamu.

8 Ninga hamba yiwe nhavivyo,

mwene yawakomboza du,

nha vyono yali yehuwize,

ho yalajile zo wuwaha wakwe.

9 Yakalikalipila lilamba lya Shamu, likanyala,

yakawa kuwakoza mumo nha mu yisi nghavu.

10 Yakawaponya mu wulawa,

mu wanhu wono wali wawasulile,

yakawaponya mu wulawa,

mu vilungo vye walugu wawo.

11 Malenga ge lilamba,

gakawagubiciza walugu wawo,

si yasigala hamba yumonga bwete.

12 Baho nhondo, nawo wanhu wakwe,

ho wehuwilila ze vilagane vyakwe,

wakawa kumwimbila ne zinyimbo zo lukumyo.

13 Ninga hanyuma hadodo,

wakawa kuzisemwa ze metumo gakwe mawaha,

wakawa kuleka kumuhuliciza.

14 Wakakwegwa ne masulumizi ku nyika,

wakamujela Mulungu ku nyika.

15 Hodu nayo Mulungu,

yakawapela cono wasakaga,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

yakawagwisiza nhamwa 'mbi muno muno.

16 Wakamuwonela wivu Musa ku nghambi yawo,

hamonga na Haluni,

u mwitumami mono yelile wo Muwaha Mulungu.

17 Nayo yisi yikahama mulomo wayo,

yikamumela Datani,

yikamuzika Abilamu,

hamonga ne mhuga yakwe yose,

18 no moto wukawaziza watyatyi wawo,

nawo wukawaticimiza wawo wanhu wabi.

19 Wakenozeza weneco nyehe ya zahabu ku Sinayi,

wakacihemba cihwani cico,

20 wakalukawaniziza lukumyo lo Mulungu,

ku cihwani ce nimu 'ndya mahanze.

21 Wakamusemwa Mulungu mono yawakomboza,

mono yetuma mezino mu yisi ya Misili,

22 ne mapoto mu yisi yiyo ya Misili,

wakazisemwa sunga ne zinghani zose,

zo wogofye zono yetuma mwi'lamba lya Shamu.

23 Mulungu nayo yakasaka yawongomeze,

ninga Musa, muhagulwa wakwe,

yakema hagati yakalimila likuwo lyo Mulungu,

ho yalece kuwongomeza.

24 Nawo hanyuma wakayibedegala yisi swanu yiyilya,

ku soko vyono si wehuwila wihuwizo wo Mulungu.

25 Wakang'ung'ulicila mu mahema gawo mugati,

hamba si wasaka kanyhi kumuhulika Mulungu.

26 Holeka nayo Mulungu yakawasugula,

vyono kowongomezeza wose mu nyika,

27 kanyhi na vyono zindelo zawo,

koziyagaziza muli wanhu,

wono si wamumanyile Mulungu,

koweyagaza mu yinze yose.

28 Nawo wakawa kwilunza,

mu wuhemba Baali ku Peyoli,

sunga wakalya ne makumbiko ge wafu.

29 Na mu nzila yiyo,

wakacila muno kumukoza likuwo Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko ye metumo gawo, na ku soko yiyo,

matamwa mabi muno gakawagwila.

30 Ninga yakenuka Finehasi yakanoza wuling'anizi,

ne matamwa gago hodu gakawa kuwuka.

31 Ne nghani yiyo yikali yikumbuciywe so cogopo cayo,

ku wulawa mwidaha lililya sunga cibitilila.

32 Wamukoza likuwo Mulungu, ha malenga ga Meliba,

nayo Musa yakagazika muno,

mu nhumbula yakwe ku soko yawo.

33 Wakamukalaza muno muno Musa mu nhumbula yakwe,

sunga nhondo yakalonga zinghani zono,

si zinojeye, wula kwihwesa.

34 Hamba si wawawulaga wanhu we makungugo ganji,

nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yali yawalajilize.

35 Ninga wene vikelunza hamonga ne wanhu we makungugo,

sunga wakeyijiza no kwituma metumo gawo.

36 Wahemba vihwani vye milungu yawo,

navyo vihwani vivyo vikawongomeza.

37 Wakawa kuwawulaga wana wawo we cilume,

sunga na we cicekulu,

no kuwalavya so makumbiko kuli masetani.

38 Wakawawulaga wana wono wasina,

hamba masutya bwete,

no kuwalavya makumbiko kuli vihwani vya Kanani,

sunga ne yisi yikatipizwa so wuwulaji wuwo.

39 Holeka wakehodanga ku soko ye metumo gawo,

wakawa wasina wihuwilwa kanyhi kuli Mulungu,

wakawa nhe wasenyha.

40 Holeka likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

likawacila wanhu wakwe,

hamba si yendezwa kanyhi na wawo wanhu wakwe.

41 Yakawatajila mu miwoko ye wanhu we makungugo,

nawo wawo wono wali wawasulile, wakawatema.

42 Wawo nawo walugu wawo,

wakawewonela wakawetumya kwe vilungo.

43 Mulungu yawaponya wanhu wakwe kali kenji,

ninga wene wakamubeza,

na ku soko yiyo, holeka Mulungu nayo yawaleka,

ku soko yo wubi wawo.

44 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

yali yeza kucihulika cililo cawo,

no kugawona magazo gawo, gono wagazagwa.

45 Mulungu yakacikumbucila cilagane cakwe,

na ku soko yo lusungu lwakwe lwinji,

hodu yakawa kulituza likuwo lyakwe.

46 Kanyhi yakawanoza wawonelwe lusungu,

ne wanhu wose wono wawetumyaga.

47 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuciponye,

go Mulungu wetu.

Wucikongholezaje wose,

ciwuce muli we makungugo, nase ho cikulumbe,

no kulikumya litagwa lyako lyono lyelile!

48 Yalumbwe Muwaha Mulungu,

Mulungu wa Isilayeli,

ku wulawa cibitilila, sunga cibitilila!

Ne wanhu wose walonje watye, “Heya!”

Yalumbwe Muwaha Mulungu!


Zabuli 107

CITABU CE HANO

(Zabuli 107–150)

Lukumyo so wuswanu wo Mulungu

1 “Mulumbenyi Muwaha Mulungu vyono muswanu,

hambi lusungu lwakwe nha lwikalaga cibitilila!”

2 Ale mweze kulonga nhavivyo,

anye wose mono mwakombozwa no Muwaha Mulungu.

Yakamuponyenyi mu vilungo vye walugu wenyu,

3 kanyhi, yakamukongholezenyi,

ku wulawa mu zisi zo wujenzi,

ku wulawilila kucilima na kumwezi,

na ku wulawilila kusumuka na kutakama.

4 Wanji wakapilima-pilima,

caka mwi'hala na mu nyika,

hamba kufika ku kaya yo wikalo.

5 Wakawona nzala ne nyhilu,

sunga ne zinhumbula zawo zikazilika.

6 Na mumo mu wugaye wawo,

wakamulilila Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo Mulungu, yakawaponya mu magazo gawo.

7 Yakawatonga mu nzila yono yigolocile,

sunga wakayificila kaya yo wikalo.

8 Holeka yiwanojeye wamulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko yo lusungu lwakwe lwinji,

ku soko ye mapoto gono yawatendela.

9 Mwene kawacesaga wono wena nyhilu wawa kuca,

nawo wono wena nzala, kawegutyaga ziswanu.

10 Wanji wakekala mu cinyhiwa na mu wutitu,

akuno wawopwe ne magazo hamonga ne manyolo,

11 ku soko vyono wabeza malajilizo,

go Mulungu no kubedegala zo wutonji,

wo Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye.

12 Wakakatala muno muno,

so milimo mizito yono wetumywagwa,

sunga wakagwa ho hasi,

ninga hasina hamba yunji mono yasuma kuwataza.

13 Holeka wakawa kumulilila,

Muwaha Mulungu mumo mu magazo gawo,

nayo yakawaponya mu magazo gawo.

14 Yakawawusa mu cinyhiwa no wutitu,

yakagabenanjiza ne manyolo gawo.

15 Holeka yiwanojeye wamulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko yo lusungu lwakwe lwinji,

na ku soko ye mapoto gono yawatendela.

16 Mwene kayibenanjizaga,

no kuyidigula milango ye shaba,

no kugadumulanga madindilo ge vyuma.

17 Wanji wakalelepa ku soko yo wubi wawo,

wakagazika ku soko yo wavu wawo,

18 sunga ne ndigwa yose yikawipa mu milomo yawo,

wakawa wali behi no kufwa.

19 Holeka wakawa kumulilila,

Muwaha Mulungu mumo mu magazo gawo,

nayo yakawaponya mu magazo gawo.

20 Yawaponyezya mu nghani yakwe,

yakawaponya ho walece kongomela.

21 Holeka yiwanojeye wamulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko yo lusungu lwakwe lwinji,

na ku soko ye mapoto gono yawatendela.

22 Wamulavizye vilavya hamonga ne lilumbo,

wasimulile ze metumo gakwe ko wimbe we nyemo.

23 Wanji wakenjila mu zi ngalawa,

wakawa wakwilondolela ndigwa zawo mumo mwi'lenga,

24 sunga na wawo nawo wagawona,

metumo mawaha go Muwaha Mulungu,

i metumo gakwe go wizine mwi'lenga.

25 Yakalajiliza,

yakaleta libeho yiwaha lyo mukukunzo,

likagenula kucanyha mawingo ge lilenga.

26 Wakagumilwa mu mwanya mucanyha,

hanyuma sunga ko ku visolo kusi,

na ku so nghani yiyo,

wanhu wakazilika mu nhumbula zawo.

27 Wakayumba-yumba no kupebesuka,

nha wono wagalile wujimbi,

no wusugu wawo wose wa mu malenga wukamalika.

28 Holeka wakawa kumulilila Muwaha Mulungu,

mumo mu magazo gawo,

nayo yakawaponya mu magazo gawo.

29 Yakawutuza mukukunzo wunyamale twii,

nago mawingo gakanyamala.

30 Nawo wakasangalala muno vyono hanyamala twii,

nayo yakawafisa i hanhu hono wabitaga.

31 Holeka yiwanojeye wamulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko yo lusungu lwakwe lwinji,

ku soko ye mapoto gono yawatendela.

32 Kanyhi wamukumizye mu wihangulize we wanhu,

no kumulumba mwi balaza lye wanyamhala.

33 Mulungu yakaganyaza makolongo hakawa hakavu,

sunga ne mivwilo nayo,

yose yakayinyaziza lukulu,

34 ne yisi yono yilelaga,

yakayinoza yikawa yihala lya mwino,

ku soko yo wubi we wanhu wono wekalaga mumo.

35 Yakayipitula nyika, yikawa malamba ga malenga,

ne yisi nghavu, hakawa mivwilo ya malenga.

36 Yakawahamizya kuko wono wali nzala,

nawo wakazenga wikalo wawo kuko.

37 Nawo wakalima migunda,

no kuhadika mizabibu yawo kuko,

wakatuga mikalo minji kuciza.

38 Nayo yakawalugutiza wanhu wakwe,

nawo wakonjezeka muno,

sunga ne liwalo lye mitugo yawo,

nalyo Mulungu yakalyonjeleza muno muno.

39 Nawo wali weza kwanza kucepuka,

no kuwula vilungo,

ku soko yo wiwonelwe no wugazwe wawo,

40 Hodu, Mulungu yakawabedegala,

wono wawagazaga,

holeka yakawa kuwakoza mu nyika,

kono kusina nzila.

41 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

yawaponya waciwa mu wugazwe wawo,

yakawa kuyonjeleza ne mhuga yawo,

wakawa nhe'tewa lya ngholo.

42 Nawo we ling'ani, wakuziwona zizo,

kanyhi wakusangalala, ninga i wabi wose,

milomo yawo yodindwa.

43 We mahala, ale wazihwesaje zizo,

kanyhi walumanye no lusungu lo Muwaha Mulungu.


Zabuli 108

Lilombo ku so wucitilwe kuli walugu

(Zab 57:7-11; 60:5-12)

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

inhumbula yangu yikamile,

heya nghamile mu nhumbula yangu,

holeka nokwimba, kanyhi nokusangalalila!

Gwe go nhumbula yangu, alu wusinhuce!

2 Musinhuce nanye nyo ndonondo ne masumbi,

nane no mweneco nokwilawa kwinuka hakali mitondo!

3 Nane nokulumbila haciligati ye makungugo,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nokwimbila zo lukumyo haciligati ye wanhu.

4 Lusungu lwako lwinji lucizize wulanga,

no wihuwilwa wako,

wuficile sunga ko ku mwanya.

5 Gwe go Mulungu,

wulawiliciye ku wulanga kucanyha,

no lukumyo lwako luficile mono'nze yose.

6 Holeka wucikombolaje,

co wanhu wako mono wucendile,

wuciponye ko muwoko wako we vilungo,

kanyhi wucihulicize no wulombe wetu.

7 Mulungu kalonga ku wulawa,

hono helile hakwe katya,

“Alu langa nosangalala,

hono niyigawanule yisi ya Shekemu,

ne lilolo lya Sukoti nalyo,

noligawa kuli wanhu wangu.

8 Hambi Gileyadi nha yangu,

sunga na Manase nayo yangu,

Nayo Efulayimu nha yo kofiya yangu ye cuma,

na Yuda nayo, yo nghome yangu yo wutemi.

9 Nayo Mowabu lye bakuli lyangu lyo kwizuguziza,

nane noyigumilila Edomu cilatu cangu,

ciwe co citanjilo vyono nayo yangu,

kanyhi nozoza lizozo lyo wusume kuli Filisiti!”

10 Alu nani lo Gwe go Mulungu mono kosuma,

kunhegula sunga kwi'kaya lye ligomawakali?

Hamba nani mono konhonga sunga ko ku Edomu?

11 Hamba lu wo wutya,

wakondya kucilecela lukulu 'gwe go Mulungu?

Alu yo hodu du, hamba sikowa wuli hamonga,

kanyhi ne mapuga getu?

12 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

cikukunena wucitaze kuli walugu wetu,

hambi wutazi wono wukulawa kuli wanhu,

nha si wokwila cinji yaye.

13 Nase mbeka cosuma du,

hono Mulungu yawe yali ku lubavu lwetu.

Mwene kowapondanjiza i walugu wetu.


Zabuli 109

Wung'ung'ulice wo munhu mono yali mu wugaye

1 Ane nghukukumya 'gwe go Mulungu,

holeka wulece kunyamala!

2 I wanhu wabi ne wakonji, nha wakunzumhila,

wakumbalalila zinghani zo wulambi.

3 Wakumbalalila makani ge cevu,

kanyhi wakunzumhila bwete,

nane ho si hodanjile cinji yaye,

4 hamba yiwe ane niwendile,

ninga wene hamba si wanyhelelela yaye,

hamba nhavivyo, ane nghawalombela du.

5 Wene wakundipa zimbi,

ku soko yo wuswanu wangu,

kanyhi wakusula ku soko yo wendo wangu.

6 Wumuhagulile hakimu mono si yanojeye,

wumulece yasitakwe na yumonga wa muli walugu wakwe.

7 Na ha wuling'anizwe wakwe ho yejele yena masutya,

no wulombe wakwe nawo, wuwe yisutya linji nalyo.

8 Ne vilo vyo wikale wakwe, navyo viwe vicefu,

na munhu yunji yasole witumami wakwe!

9 Ne wanagwe, wawe wana wono wasina walezi,

no mugolece wakwe nayo, yawe mufwile!

10 I wanagwe nawo wangalime,

kanyhi wawe welombelezi,

kanyhi wawinjilizwe,

mu matongo ge zikaya zawo!

11 Nayo mono yakumudaya cinji,

yamuboce sawo yakwe yose,

no mujenzi nayo yetujile vinhu,

vya mu nhuguti yakwe!

12 Kanyhi halece kuwa na munhu wo wose,

mono komuwonela lusungu,

hamba mono kowalanjilila,

i wanagwe wono weza wasina walezi!

13 No lulelo lwakwe lose longomele,

sunga ne litagwa lyakwe lilece kukumbucilwa,

kanyhi mu ndelo zono zikuza!

14 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yawukumbucile wubi,

we walezi wakwe, hamba no wubi wo nyina,

wulece kupatilwa lukulu!

15 Muwaha Mulungu yawukumbucile wubi wawo,

nhondo zose, langa wene,

wo walece kukumbucilwa kanyhi mono'nze.

16 Hambi u mubi yuyo, nha si yacelelela,

hamba hadodo kuwa no lusungu,

langa mwene yakawewonela no kuwawulaga,

i waciwa, no mutoci hamonga no musocele.

17 Yakawa yendile muno kuwalijita wanhu,

ninga 'lu, 'lece wulijite wakwe wumuwuyile,

kanyhi mweneco.

Hambi nha si yenda wanji ho walugutwe holeka,

lece wulugutwe wuwo wumuwucile mweneco.

18 Hambi wulijite kuli mwene,

nha winha wuvwala mwenda, holeka,

lece ta, wulijitwe wuwo wumulowece,

nha malenga, kanyhi wulijitwe wuwo,

wumwinjile sunga mo mu mafupa gakwe kotya mafuta.

19 Leka wulijitwe wumugubice nha civwalo,

wumupilime nhondo zose nha musipi.

20 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

'lece zizo ziwagane wawo wono wakusitaka.

Wanhu wono wakunonga zono si zinojeye.

21 Gwe go Mulungu,

go Muwaha Mulungu wangu,

wunhendele nha vyono wendile gwegwe,

wumhonye ku soko yo wuswanu wo lusungu lwako!

22 Ane nha nili muciwa kanyhi nili mutoci,

nhamiswe mo mu nhumbula yangu mugati.

23 Nghwajilila nho mubeho we cinyamihe,

kanyhi nghupemheluswa nhe zinzije.

24 Sunga ne mafugamilo gangu nhondo gatimba,

ku soko yo wuwula kulya ndigwa,

alu nhondo nasigala nghuli ne mafupa.

25 Nawa nili munhu wa kusekwa hali wanhu,

na lono wakumbona, wakumbedegala,

no kusinjisa mitwe yawo.

26 Holeka wunhaze 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go Mulungu wangu, wumhonye,

mu nghango yo lusungu lwako.

27 Wuwamanyize walugu wangu,

vyono yo gwegwe du mono kumhonyaga,

na vyono yo gwegwe du,

go Muwaha Mulungu mono witumile zizo.

28 Ale, walekaje du wene walijite,

ninga agwe wundugutize,

wawo wono wakunzumhila bwete ho wakozwe cevu,

ninga ane langa no mwitumami wako, ho sangalale.

29 Walugu wangu wavwikwe cevu,

wefunice cevu cawo weneco nhe bulanjeti.

30 Nane nomulumba Muwaha Mulungu,

kwe 'zi yiwaha, kanyhi nomukumizya,

mwipuga yiwaha lye wanhu,

31 hambi mwene nha kawatetelaga waciwa,

kanyhi kamuponyaga kuli walya,

wono wakumuling'aniza yafwe.


Zabuli 110

Muwaha Mulungu no mutemi wakwe muhagulwa

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakamutya muwaha wangu mutemi,

“Wikale ku muwoko wangu wo kulume,

sunga kolo nawanoza i walugu wako,

wawe cibawo co wusambo we migulu yako.”

2 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha kotandaza,

vilungo vyako wulawilila ku Sayuni,

yakutya, “Ale wuwatemaje walugu wako!”

3 Nawo wanhu wako wokwilavya weneco,

ko mutima muzelu,

mwizuwa lyo kuwahoma no kuwasuma walugu wako.

Nha vyono yinha buhe ya hakali mitondo, ale,

nhavivyo ne wazelelo wako,

nawo, vyo wolokuziza gwegwe, mu matunda gono gelile.

4 Muwaha Mulungu yelahiza,

holeka hamba si kowukawaniziza,

lukulu wuhile wakwe,

“Agwe kowa wuli mukohani cibitilila,

ku luto lo wukohani wa Melikizedeki.”

5 U Muwaha Mulungu yali lubavu lwako lo kulume,

nayo kolo yakukolwa likuwo, kowapondanjiza watemi.

6 Kogaling'aniza makungugo,

no kuwawulaga wanhu wenji muno,

kowapondanjiza i walongola hanhu,

hose mono'nze yose.

7 Na mu nzila, u mutemi kong'wa malenga,

ga mu cikolongo, holeka kokwilumba so wusume.


Zabuli 111

Kumukumya Muwaha Mulungu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!

Nomulumba Muwaha Mulungu,

kwe nhumbula yangu yose,

mu wihangulize we wanhu wakwe we ling'ani.

2 Metumo go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha ga wizine muno muno!

Na wose wono wakugasangalalila,

wakusaka wagamanye viswanu.

3 Hambi co cose cono ketumaga,

nha cimemile lukumyo no wutemi.

Ne ling'ani yakwe, yikalaga cibitilila.

4 Muwaha Mulungu nha kacikumbucizaga,

ze metumo gakwe go wizine,

Muwaha Mulungu muswanu, kanyhi yali lusungu.

5 Mwene kawalisaga wono wamogopile mwene,

hamba si kacisemwaga cilagane cakwe yaye.

6 Kawalajila wanhu wakwe vilungo vyakwe,

ko kuwapela zi'si ze makungugo ganji.

7 Metumo gakwe ga ling'ani, kanyhi ga mbeka.

Ne mejizo gakwe gose nha mehuwilwa nhondo zose.

8 Ne malajilizo gakwe,

gekalaga cibitilila ne cibitilila.

Nago galavizwa mu mbeka na mu wiling'ano.

9 Mwene yawakomboza wanhu wakwe,

kanyhi yakanoza cilagane nawo ce cibitilila.

Mwene yelile, kanyhi wa kogofya muno muno.

10 Holeka mu wumogopa Muwaha Mulungu,

wo musinji we mahala gose,

nawo wose wono wakugebata malajilizo gakwe,

wo wawo wono wakupegwa wizuce.

No lukumyo lwakwe lwikalaga cibitilila.


Zabuli 112

I nyemo yo munhu muswanu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!

Yo mumoti munhu mono yamogopile Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yakusangalalila kugebata malajilizo gakwe.

2 No lulelo lwakwe lowa ne vilungo kononze,

ne wanagwe nawo wolugutizwa.

3 Ne mhuga yakwe wowa wagoli nayo,

kowa yamotile nhondo zose.

4 Wuzelu wuwamulicilaga mu wutitu,

i wanhu waswanu, heya,

wanhu wono wali lusungu,

hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yali lusungu kanyhi mupolo,

kanyhi yali ling'ani.

5 Yo mumoti u munhu mucemezi,

mono kakopeshaga hamba kusaka cambuzi,

mono ketumilaga milimo yakwe mu ling'ani.

6 Nayo we ling'ani hamba si kosumwa yaye,

langa kokumbucilwa nhondo zose.

7 Na hono yaganwe na nghani 'mbi,

hamba si kokogopa yaye,

hambi nhumbula yakwe nha yikamile,

kanyhi yimwitumbilaga Muwaha Mulungu.

8 Yakuwa yasina mahwele,

hamba si yakogopa yaye,

hambi nha yamanyiye lukulu,

vyono walugu wakwe wosumwa.

9 Kalavyaga ko wuswanu, kawapelaga bwete waciwa,

no wuswanu wakwe, wikalaga cibitilila.

No munhu we liwala lilyo, kolumbwa cibitilila.

10 Ninga i wabi waziwonaga zizo wawa kusimba,

wawa kugwezula meno gawo, ko lusulo no kwajilila,

witumbilo wawo, hodu wahodanjika.


Zabuli 113

Kumukumya Mulungu so waswanu wakwe

1 Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!

Anye nyo wetumami wo Muwaha Mulungu,

likumyenyi litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu!

2 Litagwa lyakwe lyeze kukumywa,

ku wulawa lulu, sunga ko ku cibitilila.

3 Litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

lijendelele kukumywa,

ku wulawa kucilima, sunga ko kumwezi!

4 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha yo yatemile makungugo gose,

no lukumyo lwakwe luli kucanyha yo wulanga wose.

5 Hambi nani kanyhi, mono kowa yehwanile,

no Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wetu?

Mwene nha yekaye mwigoda lyo wutemi kucanyha,

6 hamba nhavivyo,

kekalaga yalanjile za hono hasi, heya,

kazilangaga wulanga ne yinze.

7 Mwene kamwinulizaga muciwa,

ku wulawa mu manghundi.

Kanyhi kamunyasulizaga muciwa,

ku wulawa mu wosocele wakwe,

8 hanyuma kawa kumwikaza,

hamonga ne wawaha, heya,

hamonga ne wawaha we wanhu wakwe.

9 Mwene kanyhi kamupelaga,

lilumbo mucekulu mono si kalelaga,

kamusangalazaga ko kumupela wana.

Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!


Zabuli 114

Lwimbo lwe Pasaka

1 Lono Isilayeli yawucila ku Misili,

i wana wa Yakobo,

lono wawucila mu yisi yo wujenzi,

2 Yuda wukawa wikalo wono

welile wo Mulungu,

Isilayeli yakawa sawo yakwe mweneco Mulungu.

3 Lilenga lya Shamu lyaziwona zizo,

likawa kutiza,

likolongo lya Yoludani nalyo,

likaleka kuhuluma!

4 Ne matunda nago,

gakazumhazumha nha nduwilu,

vigongo navyo,

vikazumhazumha nhe zinyana ze zingholo.

5 Alu 'gwe go lilenga,

ho yikawa wule, nagwe wukatiza?

Nagwe, go likolongo lya Yoludani,

nhawule lo nagwe wukaleka kuhuluma?

6 Nanye mo matunda, nhawulenyi

mukawa kuzumhazumha nha nduwilu?

Nanye ma mo vigongo,

nhawulenyi mukawa kuzumhazumha,

nhe zinyana ze zingholo?

7 Holeka 'lu agwe go yinze,

wucicime hali Muwaha Mulungu,

heya, hali Mulungu wa Yakobo,

8 mono kalipamizaga du litalawe,

nalyo liwa yilamba lya malenga,

ne mabwe makavu gawa mivwilo ya malenga.


Zabuli 115

Mulungu yumonga du we mbeka

1 Kuli'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuli wene,

yo si sese,

langa yo gwegwe du wuli wene,

mono wunojeye lukumyo,

ku soko yo lusungu lwako,

no wihuwilwa wako.

2 Ku sokoci makungugo gakuciwuza gakutya,

“Alu Mulungu wenyu yali hayi?”

3 Mulungu wetu'se, yali ku wulanga,

nayo mwene ketumaga zose,

nha vyono yendile mweneco.

4 I milungu yawo nha ya fweza na zahabu du,

yono yinozigwe ne miwoko ye wanhu.

5 Yina milomo, ninga hamba si yikulonga bwete,

yina meso, ninga hamba si yikuwona yaye,

6 sunga ne makutu yina nago,

ninga hamba si yikuhulika ng'o,

ne zimhula zose yina nazo,

ninga hamba si yihevyaga,

munungho wa cinji co cose yaye,

7 'gwe, sunga ne miwoko yose yina nayo,

ninga si yisumaga hamba no kwalabata bwete du,

ati sunga ne magulu gose yina nago,

ninga hamba si yikusuma kujenda yaye.

Si yikusumila lukulu hamba kulavya yizi du kose.

8 Holeka na wose wono wayitumbilaga milungu yiyo,

nawo wehwanile nha yeneco, heya,

sunga na wose wono wayihuwiye milungu yiyo.

9 Nye nyo Isilayeli,

mwitumbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

mwene yo mutazi wenyu,

kanyhi yo ngula yenyu.

10 Nanye mo wana wa Haluni,

mwitumbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

mwene yo mutazi wenyu,

kanyhi yo ngula yenyu.

11 Anye nanye mono mumogopile Muwaha Mulungu,

mwitumbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu mwene,

yo mutazi wenyu, kanyhi yo ngula yenyu.

12 Mwene Muwaha Mulungu, yacikumbuciye du,

kanyhi kocilugutiza, kowalugutiza Waisilayeli,

kowalugutiza wana wa Haluni.

13 Mulungu kowalugutiza wose wono wamogopile,

kowalugutiza wawaha sunga ne wadodo.

14 Muwaha Mulungu yacile kumonjelezenyi wana,

anye mo weneco, sunga ne wana wenyu!

15 Ale, Muwaha Mulungu yamulugutizenyi,

mono yawumba wulanga ne yinze.

16 Wulanga wose, yo sawo yo Muwaha Mulungu,

langa yinze, yakawapela wanhu.

17 Kanyhi wafu, si wamukumyaga Muwaha Mulungu,

hamba wo wose yulya, mono yakondya kubita ku yisi ye wafu,

18 Langa sese du mono cili womi,

yo comukumya Muwaha Mulungu,

ku wulawilila lulu baha,

sunga ku cibitilila.

Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!


Zabuli 116

Lilumbo lyo munhu ku soko yo wuponywa mwi'fwa

1 Ane nimwendile Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha kahulikaga,

hambi nha kahuliciza cililo co wulombe wangu.

2 Mwene nha kahulicizaga lono nghumulilila.

3 Zimbali ze lifwa zali zimhilime,

no wogofywe we citenjelo wukanyibata,

nghawa no wugazice hamonga no wukalale.

4 Holeka nghamulilila Muwaha Mulungu nghatya,

“Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nghunena wumhonye!”

5 Muwaha Mulungu yo munghala wuswanu wose,

no wihuwilwa, Mulungu wetu,

yo Mulungu wo lusungu.

6 Muwaha Mulungu kawakamisaga,

wono wasina wutazi,

hambi nha lono nali nili mu mbali,

mwene yamhonya.

7 Holeka go nhumbula yangu, wutule muli'ne,

hambi Muwaha Mulungu nha kanhendela ziswanu.

8 Kamhonya mwi'fwa, kagapata ne mahozi gangu,

yakawa kundima ho ndece kanyhi kugwa mu musutye.

9 Holeka nane lu,

nowa kujenda hali Muwaha Mulungu,

mu yisi ye wanhu womi.

10 Nghawa nihuwiye du,

sunga lono nalongaga nghutya,

“Alu langa ane, nhondo neza kwajilila.”

11 Nhondo sunga nghawa kulonga ko woga nghutya,

“Mbeka wanhu hamba si wehuwilwa yaye.”

12 Alu nomugomolela ci cici Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko ye ziswanu zose zono yanhendeye?

13 Nomulavizya Muwaha Mulungu cilavywa ca divayi,

nimulumbe ku soko ye mhonelo yono yamhonizye.

14 Nomufisiliziza Muwaha Mulungu vipingo vyangu,

ha mwande ye likungugo lyakwe lyose.

15 Muwaha Mulungu kawukalalilaga muno muno,

wufwe wa wono wali ling'ani,

16 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ane nili mwitumami wako,

nili mwitumami wako du nha vyono yenha yaya.

Nagwe wukanoza nghawa mwelecele.

17 Holeka nane lu,

nokulavizya likumbiko lyo wulumbe,

kanyhi nowa nghogopile muno muno.

18 Nane nokufisiliziza zose,

zono nehuwiza, go Muwaha Mulungu,

ha mwande ye likungugo lyako lyose,

19 kuko ku Yelusalemi mu Nyumba yako.

Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!


Zabuli 117

Kumukumya Mulungu

1 Nye nyo nghungugo zose,

mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu!

Nanye nyo wanhu wose mukumyenyi!

2 Hambi lusungu lwakwe kuli'se nha lwinji muno,

no wihuwilwa wakwe nawo, wikalaga cibitilila!

Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!


Zabuli 118

Lilombo lyo wulumbe so wusume

1 Mulumbenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha muswanu.

No lusungu lwakwe nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

2 Wanhu wa Isilayeli walonje watye,

“Lusungu lwakwe lwikalaga cibitilila.”

3 Nawo Wakohani wo Mulungu walonje watye,

“Lusungu lwakwe lwikalaga cibitilila.”

4 Na wose wono wamogopilo Muwaha Mulungu,

nawo walonje watye,

“Lusungu lwakwe lwikalaga cibitilila.”

5 Nane mu wugaye wangu,

nghamulilila Muwaha Mulungu,

nayo yakahulika, yakamhonya.

6 Muwaha Mulungu, yali hamonga nane,

holeka nane si nghogopa cinji co cose,

hambi munhu konoza cici?

7 Muwaha Mulungu yali hamonga nane,

nayo yo yakunhaza,

holeka nowawona walugu wangu wakusumwa.

8 Yili ciba kumwitumbila Muwaha Mulungu,

sinha kumwitumbila munhu.

9 Yili ciba kumwitumbila Muwaha Mulungu,

sinha kuwetumbila walongola we yinze yiyi.

10 Walugu wenji wali wamhilime,

ninga nghawapondanjiziza wose,

mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu.

11 Wali wamhilime mbavu zose,

ninga nane nghawapondanjiza wose,

kwe vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu.

12 Wakahungucila ko winji kotya nzuci,

ninga wakatakamila wose,

nhe cititika mu moto,

nane nghawapondanjiza wose,

kwe vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu.

13 Wakanzumhila, sunga nghawa nili behi,

no kusumwa nawo,

ninga Muwaha Mulungu yakanhaza.

14 Mbeka Muwaha Mulungu,

yo kamhelaga vilungo no wusume,

yo mweneco du mono yamhonizye.

15 Ale, hulicizenyi ta mazi,

ge nyemo no wusume gono gakulawa,

mu mahema ge wanhu wo Mulungu, gakutya,

“Vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu,

vyo vitumile zizi!

16 Vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu,

vyo viletile wusume wo wulugu,

Vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu yali wene,

vyo vitumile zizi!”

17 Ane si nofwa yaye, langa nowumipa,

nane nozanza ze metumo mawaha,

go Muwaha Mulungu.

18 Muwaha Mulungu kandipiciza muno muno,

ninga si yandeceleze nifwe.

19 Ndigulilenyi milango ye Nyumba yo Mulungu,

nane nokwinjila, nimulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

20 Awu wo mulango wo Muwaha Mulungu,

wono wadahile kuwukolela we ling'ani.

21 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukulumba vyono wuhulicize,

hambi nha yo gwegwe du mono wumheye wusume.

22 Libwe lyono walilema wazenji,

lyo lyeza yibwe liwaha lyo musinji.

23 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

yo yetumile nghani yiyino,

nayo yo nghani yo wizine muno muno kuli'se.

24 Ali lyo lizuwa lyo wusume wo Muwaha Mulungu,

holeka cisecelele kanyhi cisangalale.

25 Wuciponye, 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuciponye!

Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu cikukunena wucipele wumoteswe!

26 Yalugutwe yuyo mono yakuziza,

mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu!

Nase cikumulombelenyi wumoteswe,

ku wulawa mu Nyumba yo Muwaha Mulungu.

27 Muwaha Mulungu, yo Mulungu du yali wene,

nayo kacimulicilaga mu wuswanu.

Holeka ale mwibataje manhambi,

mu miwoko yenyu, musangalale,

mujendele hamonga sunga ko ku cikumbicilo.

28 Agwe yo gwegwe Mulungu wangu,

nane nghukulumba,

kanyhi nokwanza zo wuwaha wako.

29 Mulumbenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha muswanu,

no lusungu lwakwe nha lwikalaga cibitilila.


Zabuli 119

Wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

1 Wamoti wono wena mikalile,

yono yisina masutya,

wono webate wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu.

2 Wamoti wono wetumaga zo wuhile wo Mulungu,

wono wamuhulicile kwe zinhumbula zawo zose,

3 wono si wetumaga zo wubi yaye,

langa wene watyatyaga,

zo wutonji wakwe nhondo zose.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wacijiza ze mejizo gako,

ho cigebate ko wihuwilwa muno.

5 Yali yiwe yili ciba le,

hono mikalile yangu yali yisikame,

mu wibata zo wutonji wako!

6 Na hono mbeka nigakamise malajilizo gako,

mbeka si nokozwa cevu yaye.

7 Nane nokukumya kwe nhumbula ye cogopo,

hono niyijize ze ning'aniza yako ye ling'ani.

8 Ane nowutyatya du wutonji wako,

holeka wulece kundeka nili wene yaye.

Cihuliko ca kuli zo wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

9 Alu muzelelo kosuma nhawule,

kuyinozeleza mikalile yakwe?

Langa mu wibata malajilizo gako du.

10 Nghusimbilila muno nghuhulice,

kwe nhumbula yangu yose,

holeka nagwe wulece kundeceleza,

mbene malajilizo gako.

11 Nibate lilajilizo lyako,

mu nhumbula yangu,

ho ndece kukusutizya.

12 Lukumyo lweze kuli'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu!

Wunyijize ze nzila yako.

13 Nane nozanza kwe'zi yiwaha muno,

ze malajilizo gako gose gono wawika.

14 Nane sangalaye muno,

so wutyatya zo wuhile wako,

si nho kuwa no wugoli winji.

15 Nihwesaga du ze mejizo gako,

no kugakamisa malajilizo gako.

16 Nghuwusangalalila muno wutonji wako,

holeka si nolisemwa yaye lilajilizo lyako.

Nyemo yono yili mu wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

17 Wumotyaje 'ne no mwitumami wako,

ho niwe mumi, no kulikamisa lilajilizo lyako.

18 Wunuguze meso gangu,

ho niziwone zo wizine wa mu wulajilize wako.

19 Ane nha nili mukola nzila du mono'nze,

holeka wulece kuhundicila zo wulajilize wako.

20 Nhumbula yangu yitamice muno,

so wusulumila nhondo zose,

kumanya zo wulajilize wako.

21 Agwe nha kuwakalipilaga,

i wanhu wedunhumuzi,

holeka walijitwe wose,

wono wakugabedegala malajilizo gako.

22 Wumbusize maligo gawo,

hamonga no wubedegalwe hambi 'ne,

nha niwibate muno wuhile wako.

23 Sunga ne wawaha nawo wena wuhile wubi kuli'ne,

ninga hamba nhavivyo, ane no mwitumami wako,

nojendelela kwihwesa zo wutonji wako du.

24 Hambi i mejizo gako nha yo nyemo yangu'ne.

Kanyhi go gamhelaga wutonji wuswanu muno.

Wutonji wo wibata wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

25 Nghugalangala hasi mu manghundi nasumwa,

holeka wumhelaje kanyhi wumi,

nha vyono wehuwiza.

26 Nha nakondya kunena kuli zono nasutya,

nagwe wukahulika,

holeka wunyijize zo wutonji wako.

27 Wunyijize nzila yo wibate we malajilizo gako,

nane nozihwesa ze metumo gako go wizine.

28 Alu wusungu nha wanhulila muno so wukalale,

wumhelaje kanyhi vilungo, nha vyono wehuwiza.

29 Wumbusize nzila yo wajilile,

wunyijize ko wupolo ze malajilizo gako.

30 Ane nahagula nzila yo wiling'ano,

kanyhi nibate ne ning'aniza yako.

31 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane nigebate du mejizo gako,

holeka wulece kundeceleza nghozwe cevu!

32 Nane nojendela mu nzila ye malajilizo gako,

hambi agwe nha kocila kumhela wizuce.

Lilombo so wizuce

33 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wunyijize zo wutonji wako,

nane nowukamisiza lukulu vilo vyose.

34 Wumanyize malajilizo gako,

nane ho sume kugebata,

kanyhi nigebate kwe nhumbula yangu yose.

35 Wunhonje nigebate malajilizo gako,

hambi mumo nha mo nghutujila nyemo.

36 Wumhele nyhilu yo kugebatila malajilizo gako,

ninga yilece kuwa so wutuga cambuzi yaye.

37 Wumbusize wuwe wo kuzityatya,

zinghani zo wulele,

wunhonje ho sume kuwumipa.

38 Wufisilizize ane no mwitumami wako,

wihuwizo wako,

wihuwizo wono kuwapelaga wose wono wakogopile.

39 Wumhonye ne matovulo gono nghugogopa,

hambi ning'aniza yako nha swanu muno!

40 Nyendile muno nigebate mejizo gako,

holeka wumhele wumi,

hambi 'gwe nha wuli ling'ani.

Kuwitumbila wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

41 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukunena lusungu lwako lunyizize,

wumhonye nha vyono wehuwiza.

42 Nane nhondo ho nosuma,

kuwapitula wawo wono wakundiganga,

hambi 'ne nha niyihuwiye nghani yako.

43 Wunhaze niwe kuyilonga i mbeka yako,

nhondo zose, hambi witumbilo wangu,

nha wuli mu ning'aniza yako du.

44 Nane nowukamisa wulajilize wako nhondo zose,

cibitilila ne cibitilila.

45 Kanyhi nokwikala nili mwelecele nhondo zose,

hambi nha nigakamise mejizo gako.

46 Kanyhi nowanziza watemi ze malajilizo gako,

hamba si nowona cevu yaye.

47 Ne nyemo yangu yose,

yili mu wibata malajilizo gako,

hambi nha nigendile muno muno.

48 Nigogopile kanyhi nigendile malajilizo gako,

nane nowa kuzihwesa muno ze mejizo gako.

Witumbilo wa muli wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

49 Ale, wukumbucile wihuwizo wako,

wono wanyihuwiza ane no mwitumami wako,

wihuwizo wono wumheye witumbilo.

50 Hamba na mu wugaye wangu, nghatuzwa,

hambi wihuwizo wako nha wukamhela wumi.

51 I wedunhumuzi, wambedegalaga nhondo zose,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

ane si niwubedegalaga wulajilize wako yaye.

52 Nane nizikumbucilaga ze ning'aniza zako,

zo wulawa katali 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nane nghutuga wutuzwe wuwaha muno.

53 Nane nghukolwa muno ne likuwo,

lono nghuwawona i wabi,

wakubena malajilizo gako.

54 Nane lono nili kuno ku wujenzi,

zinyimbo zangu zose,

nghwimba ze malajilizo gako du.

55 Na mu cilo namo,

nghukumbucilaga gwe gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

akuno nghwihwesa ze malajilizo gako du.

56 Nane natuga wulugutwe wuwaha,

vyono nigakamise malajilizo gako.

Wuhidimbazi kuli zo wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

57 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe yo gwegwe du mono nghusaka,

Nane nghwihuwiza vyono nokwibata,

wulajilize wako.

58 Holeka nghukunena kwe nhumbula yose,

wumbonele lusungu,

nha vyono wehuwiza gwegwe!

59 Nayihwesa mijendele yangu,

holeka nihuwiza nizityatye ze mejizo gako.

60 Nane hamba si nocela yaye,

ku wutyatya malajilizo gako,

61 hamba yiwe mitego ye wabi yindadilize,

ninga ane si nowusemwela lukulu wulajilize wako.

62 Nane ninukaga mu cilo mugati,

niwa kukukumya ku soko ye ning'aniza yako

ye ling'ani.

63 Nane nili mwendece kuli wose,

wono wakogopile gwegwe,

kanyhi na kuli wose wono webate ze mejizo gako.

64 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

yinze nha yimemile zo lusungu lwako,

holeka wunyijize zo wutonji wako.

Wunojele wuwaha wo wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

65 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kunhendela viswanu ane no mwitumami wako,

nha vyono wehuwiza gwegwe.

66 Holeka wumhele mahala no wizuce,

hambi 'ne nha nihuwiye malajilizo gako.

67 Lono wali wukali nandipiciza,

netumaga zimbi,

ninga aluno, noyikamisa nghani yako.

68 Agwe nha wuli muswanu,

kanyhi wuli lusungu,

wunyijize zo wutonji wako.

69 Iwedunhumuzi wakumbalalila zinghani zo wulambi,

ninga ane nghamisize du ze mejizo gako.

70 Zinhumbula zawo zimemile wulele du,

ninga ane nghuwusangalalila wulajilize wako.

71 U wulipicizwe wangu wali viswanu kuli'ne,

hambi nha wunoza niyijize zo wutonji wako.

72 Hambi wulajilize wono wawuwika,

nha wuswanu muno wucizize zisawo zose za mono'nze.

Wiling'ano wo wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

73 Gwegwe go mweneco yo wamumba kwe miwoko yako,

ale, wumhele mahala,

niyijize ze malajilizo gako.

74 Nawo wono wakogopile,

wombona kanyhi wosangalala,

ku soko vyono ane niyitumbiye nghani yako.

75 Nane manyile vyono wuling'anize wako,

wo we ling'ani 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

na vyono kanyhi kundipiciza,

vyono 'gwe wuli mwihuwilwa.

76 Lusungu lwako lo lukusinyhimika 'ne,

nha vyono wanyihuwiziza ane no mwitumami wako.

77 Wumbonele lusungu ho nikale mumi,

hambi lilajilizo lyako,

nha yo nyemo yangu'ne.

78 Iwedunhumuzi wakozwe cevu,

hambi nha wambalalila nghani zo wulambi,

ninga ane nozihwesa ze mejizo gako du.

79 Nawo wose wono wakogopile, waze kuli'ne,

ho wazimanye ze mejizo gako.

80 Nane nizityatizye lukulu zo wutonji wako,

ho nize ndece kukozwa cevu.

Kulomba wukombozwe

81 Alu nhondo ane natimba 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghulindilize wuze wumhonye,

no witumbilo wangu wose,

nha wuli mu nghani yako du.

82 Ne meso gangu nha gakatala,

so wulanjilila cono wehuwiza,

sunga neza kuwuza nghutya,

“Alo ho kokuza nili wusindamice?”

83 Hambi nha nezinga,

nawa nha mufuko wa nyhingo mugumilwe mu lyosi,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

nghali si ninawusemwa wutonji wako.

84 Alu ho nolindila sunga nili lo?

Hamba kowalipiciza nili lo wono wakungaza?

85 Iwedunhumuzi wahimbila yikombo,

ho wangwizize mumo,

wawo wo wanhu wono si waliceleleye,

lilajilizo lyako,

86 Malajilizo gako gose nha mehuwilwa,

wanhu wakungaza bwete mbula soko,

holeka wunhaze!

87 Wali weza wali behi no kunyongomeza, ninga ane,

hamba si nobezeza lukulu mejizo gako.

88 Wumhonezye mu nghango yo lusungu lwako,

ho nigakamisize lukulu malajilizo gako.

Wihuwilo wa mu wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

89 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghani yako yikalaga cibitilila,

yikalaga yisikame ku wulanga.

90 No wihuwilwa wako wikalaga mu zindelo zose,

nagwe wuyisimizye yinze hanhu hayo,

nayo hamba si yilawuka.

91 Ne viwumbwa vyose vili baha,

sunga nalu ku soko ye lilajilizo lyako gwegwe,

hambi vinhu vyose nha wetumami wako gwegwe.

92 Na hono lilajilizo lyako,

lyali liwe yo si ndawililo ye nyemo yangu,

mhali nakondya kongomela ku soko yo wugaye wangu.

93 Holeka nane hamba si nogalecela lukulu mejizo gako,

hambi nha mo kumhelelaga wumi wangu.

94 Hambi 'ne nha nili wako, holeka wumhonye,

hambi nha hidimbaye muno mu wibata mejizo gako.

95 I wabi nawo wakusepa ho wambulaje,

ninga ane nghwihwesa ze mejizo gako du.

96 Nakondya kuwumanya mudumo wo wukwilanicile,

ninga malajilizo gako gasina mudumo.

Kuwenda wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

97 Nendile muno lilajilizo lyako!

Kanyhi nghulihwesa misi yose!

98 Lilajilizo lyako,

ane nilityatyaga nhondo zose,

nalyo likumhela mahala menji,

kuciza i walugu wangu.

99 Nane nina wizuce muno,

kuciza ne wejizi wangu wose,

vyono 'ne nghwihwesa muno ze mejizo gako du.

100 Nane niwacizize wizuce sunga ne wakombi wose,

ku soko vyono nigebate mejizo gako.

101 Nane nghwiyimila muno,

ho ndece kutyatya mijendele yo yose mibi,

ho sume kuyibata nghani yako du.

102 Nghali hamba si ninawubedegala yaye,

wijize wako,

hambi nha yo gwegwe du yo wuli mwijizi wangu.

103 Ne mejizo gako nha maswanu muno kuli'ne,

gali muzinzi gacizize sunga no wuci.

104 Na ku soko yo wijize wako,

ane nghutuga wizuce,

holeka niyisulile mikalile yose mibi.

Wuzelu we lilajilizo lyo Muwaha Mulungu

105 Nghani yako yo tala yono yikunhonga,

no wuzelu wono wukumulicila nzila yangu.

106 Nane nilahiza no kukangaza mu wilahize wangu,

vyono nogakamisa mejizo gako ge ling'ani

107 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ane nghugazika muno,

holeka wumhele wumi nha vyono wehuwiza.

108 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wubocele wulombe wo wulumbe wangu,

kanyhi wunyijize ze ning'aniza yako.

109 Wikale wangu nha wuli mu mbali nhondo zose,

ninga hamba si nghulisemwa lilajilizo lyako yaye.

110 Iwabi wanhejile mitego,

ninga ane hamba si nogabedegala yaye mejizo gako.

111 I mejizo gako wo wuhazi wangu nhondo zose,

heya, kanyhi yo nyemo ye nhumbula yangu.

112 Holeka nane nina wuhile mu nhumbula yangu,

wo wutyatya zo wutonji wako cibitilila.

Wudimi wono wuli mu wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

113 Nane niwasulile wanhu wono si wakwendile,

Ninga nilyendile lilajilizo lyako.

114 Agwe yo gwegwe wihundiko wangu ne ngula yangu,

nane niyitumbiye nghani yako.

115 Mbucilenyi 'nye nyo wanhu wabi,

nane ho nigebate malajilizo go Mulungu wangu.

116 Ale, wumhele vilungo nha vyono wehuwiza,

nane ho niwe mumi,

wulece kundeceleza nghozwe cevu mu witumbilo wangu.

117 Wunyegamize, nane nopona,

nane ho niwe ne cihuliko,

kuli zo wutonji wako nhondo zose.

118 Kuwalemaga wose wono wabedegalaga,

zo wutonji wako no wungwele wawo, nha wa bwete du.

119 Wukuwawusa wabi wose we'nze nha takataka,

holeka ane niwendile wutonji wako.
