120 Ane nghucicima so wogopa gwegwe,

memile woga, ku soko ye ning'aniza yako.

Cihuliko kuli zo wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

121 Nituma nghani swanu kanyhi ze ling'ani,

holeka wulece kundeka mu miwoko ye walugu wangu.

122 Wunyihuwizaje vyono konhaza,

ane no mwitumami wako,

i wewoni walece kunyiwonela.

123 Meso gangu nha gakatala,

so wulanjilila mhonelo yako,

no wufisilize we nghani yako ye ling'ani.

124 Wunhendele mu nghango yo lusungu lwako,

ane no mwitumami wako,

wunyijize zo wutonji wako.

125 Ane no mwitumami wako,

wumhele wizuce ho manye mejizo gako.

126 I cipango cafika go Muwaha Mulungu witume,

hambi wanhu nha wakuwubena wulajilize wako.

127 Holeka, nigendile muno malajilizo gako,

kuciza zahabu, heya,

zahabu yono yilenguywe.

128 Holeka nghugatyatya i mejizo gako gose,

kanyhi si niyendile nzila yo wajilile.

Wusaka kuwuhulika wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

129 Mejizo gako ga wizine,

holeka nigebate ne nhumbula yangu yose.

130 Wulolose we mejizo gako, wuletaga wuzelu,

kanyhi wuwapelaga wumanye wono si wamanyile cinji.

131 Ane nghweheleka akuno nyahame no mulomo wangu,

hambi nha nina nyhilu ye malajilizo gako.

132 Wunanje, wumbonele lusungu,

nha vyono kuwatendelaga wono wakwendile.

133 Wusikamye zi nghanzo zangu nha vyono wehuwiza,

kanyhi wulece kundeceleza wubi wuteme muli'ne.

134 Wumhonye mu wutendelwa vibi,

ne wanhu nane ho nigebate mejizo gako.

135 Wumulicile zo wuwe wako,

wunyijize zo wutonji wako.

136 Meso gangu gakuhuluma mahozi nhe kolongo,

ku soko vyono wanhu si wakuwukamisa wulajilize wako.

Ling'ani yo wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

137 Gwe Muwaha Mulungu, wuli mwihuwilwa,

ne zining'aniza zako zo ze mbeka.

138 No wulajilize wako,

wono wawuwika wo we mbeka ne ling'ani.

139 Holeka nghukolwa likuwo,

ku soko walugu wangu,

si wakucelelela zo wulajilize wako.

140 No wihuwizo wako wo we mbeka,

nane no mwitumami wako niwendile.

141 Ninga 'ne vyono nili mudodo,

holeka mbedegaywe, hamba nhavivyo,

ane si nghusemwa ze mejizo gako yaye.

142 Ling'ani yako yo ye ling'ani cibitilila,

no wulajilize wako wo we mbeka.

143 Wugazice no wukalale vyanyagana,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

malajilizo gako gamhelaga wusangalale.

144 No wuhomelezi wako,

wo we ling'ani nhondo zose,

wumhele wizuce, nane ho nowumipa.

Kulomba wukombozwe

145 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukulilila kwe nhumbula yangu yose.

Wumhitule, nane nokwibata ze mejizo gako.

146 Nghukunena wumhonye,

nane ho niwutyatye wutonji wako.

147 Mhinjilaga hakali mitondo muno muno,

nghulomba wutazi wako,

nokwitumbilila lukulu zinghani zako.

148 Nikalaga meso cilo cose,

ho nihwese ze mejizo gako.

149 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuhulice ko wendo wako,

wundajile zo lusungu lwako, wumhonye.

150 I wanhu wabi wono wonhajila mu magazo,

nha wasejelela,

i wanhu wono si webataga zo wulajilize wako.

151 Ninga agwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuli behi nane,

ne malajilizo gako gose, go ge mbeka.

152 Nane nakondya kwiyijiza,

ku wulawa katali ze mejizo gako,

nagwe yo gwegwe du,

mono wagawika gekale cibitilila.

Kulomba wutazi

153 Wulanje ze magazo gangu, wumhonye,

hambi ne nha si semigwe zo wulajilize wako.

154 Wunhetele kanyhi wunghomboze,

wumhonye nha vyono wehuwiza.

155 Iwabi hamba si woponywa yaye,

hambi nha si wakuzicelelela zo wutonji wako.

156 Lusungu lwako nha luwaha 'gwe,

go Muwaha Mulungu,

holeka wundajile zo lusungu lwako wumhonye.

157 Hambi i walugu wangu,

na wono wakumhela magazo, nha wenji,

ninga ane si nolecela lukulu,

kutyatya zo wulajilize wako.

158 Na lono nghuwawona wawo i wagaluci,

mutima wangu hamba si wukwendezwa nawo,

hambi nha si wakugebata malajilizo gako.

159 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, lanje ta,

vyono nigendile mejizo gako!

Holeka wumhonye mu nghango,

yo lusungu lwako lo wendo.

160 Zinghani zako zose yo mbeka,

ne zining'aniza zako ze ling'ani,

zose za cibitilila.

Wusiwanulilwa ku wulajilize wo Muwaha Mulungu

161 I wawaha nawo wamhela magazo du mbula soko,

ninga ane niwogopile wulajilize wako,

kwe nhumbula yangu yose.

162 Nane sangalaye so wihuwizo wako,

nihwana no munhu mono yawene,

cinhu co wuguzi wukamu muno muno.

163 Ane si nizendile hamba hadodo,

zinghani zo wulambi-lambi,

langa'ne niwendile wulajilize wako du.

164 Nane nghulumbaga nghanghulo mupunghati,

mwi'zuwa limonga,

ku soko ye zining'aniza zako ze ling'ani.

165 Wose wono wawendile wulajilize wako,

wena mulamu wono wukwilaniciye muno muno,

na hasina cinji kanyhi cono cowagwisa.

166 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, nghulindiye wumhonye,

nane nghwituma nha vyono genha malajilizo gako.

167 Ane nigebate mejizo gako,

kanyhi nigendile kwe nhumbula yangu yose.

168 Nigakamise malajilizo gako,

hamonga ne mejizo gako,

nagwe nha wuziwene calinze zose zono nitumaga.

Kulomba wutazi

169 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

cililo cangu cikuzize!

Wumhele wizuce mu nghango yo wihuwizo wako.

170 Wulombe wangu wukuzize,

wumhonye nha vyono wehuwiza.

171 Nane nowa kukulumba nhondo zose,

hambi nha wukunyijiza zo wutonji wako.

172 Nowa kwimba ze nghani yako,

hambi malajilizo gako gose,

nha go ge ling'ani.

173 Holeka weze wiwikanize,

nhondo zose kunhaza,

hambi nha nigebate mejizo gako.

174 Nanjiliye muno wumhonye 'gwe

go Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi wulajilize wako,

nha wo wusangalale wangu.

175 Mhelaje wuwumipe, nane ho nghulumbe,

kanyhi ne zining'aniza zako zinhaze.

176 Nghuyumba-yumba, nha ngholo yono yajiliye,

holeka wuze wunondole ane no mwitumami wako,

hambi nha si nigasemwaga malajilizo gako.


Zabuli 120

Kulomba wutazi

1 Lono nali nili mu wugazice,

namucemeleza Muwaha Mulungu, nayo yakahulika.

2 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumhonye kuli wanhu wakonji kanyhi walambi.

3 Anye nyo wanhu walambi,

mwene mopegwa cinhuci no Mulungu?

Hamba Mulungu komulipicizenyi vye wule,

mumo mu wulambi wenyu?

4 Mbeka komulipicizenyi,

kwe zisonga zinghali ze wasilikale,

kanyhi na kwe makala go moto!

5 Sunga no wikala muli'nye, wambuce du,

hambi nha winha wikala mu Mesheki,

hamba kotya nha wikala mu wanhu wa Kedali.

6 Ku wulawa katali,

nekalana ne wanhu wono si wendile mulamu yaye!

7 Ane nili wo mulamu,

lono nghulonga zo mulamu,

wene wakusaka wulugu.


Zabuli 121

Muwaha Mulungu yo mukamisi wetu

1 Ninuye meso gangu nanjile mu matunda,

wutazi wangu wolawa hayi?

2 Wutazi wangu wolawa kuli Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yawumba wulanga ne yinze.

3 Nayo hamba si kokuleka wugwe,

hambi mudimi wako nha si kasinzilaga yaye.

4 Mudimi wa Isilayeli,

si kasinzilaga hamba kugona kose.

5 Muwaha Mulungu yo mukamisi wako,

yali behi nagwe ho yakudime.

6 Ne lizuwa hamba si lyokutamisa yaye mu misi,

hamba no mwezi nawo ku cilo.

7 Muwaha Mulungu kokukamisa muli zimbali

zose, nagwe kowa wupolile du.

8 Muwaha Mulungu kokukamisa mu wulawile wako

na mu winjile wako ku wulawa lulu,

sunga ko ku cibitilila.


Zabuli 122

Lukumyo kuli Yelusalemi

1 Nasangalala waza kunonjela wakutya,

“Ale cibite ku kaya yo Muwaha Mulungu.”

2 Alu ase cakondya kufika,

nalu cimile ha milango ya Yelusalemi!

3 Gwe Yelusalemi, go likaya lyono

lizenjigwe wuzengwe wuswanu muno,

kanyhi wukamile!

4 Ale, mumo mo makungugo gakuza, heya,

makungugo ga Isilayeli,

gakuza gawe kumulumba Muwaha Mulungu,

nha vyono yalajiliza.

5 Na mumo mo mogali mahakama go,

kuwaling'aniziza wanhu mu ling'ani,

i mahakama ge nyumba ye citemi ya Dawudi.

6 Holeka muyilombele Yelusalemi mulamu,

“Nawo wose wono wakwendile, wamoteswe.

7 Mwi'kumbo lyako, muweze no mulamu,

na ku zinyumba zako kuweze kupolile!”

8 Ku soko ye wayangu hamonga ne wandugu zangu,

holeka nghulomba mulamu wuweze muli gwegwe,

agwe go Yelusalemi!

9 Na ku soko ye nyumba yo Muwaha Mulungu wetu,

holeka nghukulombela wutuje wumoteswe!


Zabuli 123

Wulombe so wuwonelwa lusungu

1 Nghulanjiliye go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe mono wikaye kuko ku wulanga kucanyha.

2 Nha vyono wetumami wamwitumbilaga muwaha wawo,

na vyono kanyhi u mwitumami we cicekulu,

kamwitumbilaga mucekulu munya kaya.

Ale, nase nhavivyo vyo cikwitumbiye gwegwe,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, go Mulungu wetu,

sunga wukuciwonela lusungu.

3 Wuciwonele lusungu 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wuciwonele lusungu,

hambi nha cabedegalwa muno muno.

4 Cabedegalwa muno muno, ko muno ne wagoli,

nhondo 'nye, cabedegalwa si, ne wedunhumuzi.


Zabuli 124

Mulungu yo mukamisi we wanhu wakwe

1 “Mhali Muwaha Mulungu yali yalece,

kuwa ku lubavu lwetu,

2 Go Isilayeli wulonje wutye, Muwaha Mulungu,

yali yalece kuwa ku lubavu lwetu,

mu cidaha cicilya cono cahungucilwa ne wanhu,

3 mhali wacimele cili womi lululya,

lono wali wacikolelwa makuwo.

4 Baho langa mhali cisolelezwe,

ne lihwazi lye malenga,

Likolongo lyali lizigubicize nhumbula zetu,

5 ne malenga menji,

go mukukunzo gali gacigubicize!”

6 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe,

mono si yacileceleza,

i walugu wetu wacongomeze.

7 Alu capona nha vyono ndeje yisukulaga,

mu mutego we wapelembi mutego wubeneka,

nase capona.

8 Wutazi wetu wulawaga kuli Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yawumba wulanga ne yinze.


Zabuli 125

Wucitilwe we wanhu wo Mulungu

1 Wono wamwitumbiye Muwaha Mulungu,

wehwanile ne litunda lya Sayuni,

lyono si lisinjisikaga yaye,

langa lisikame vilo vyose.

2 Nha vyono Yelusalemi yipilimwe ne matunda,

ale, no Muwaha Mulungu nayo,

vyo yawapilime wanhu wakwe,

ku wulawa lulu, sunga ko ku cibitilila.

3 Hambi nha si kowaleka i wabi,

ho wateme yisi ya we ling'ani,

ku soko hono wateme,

wowambuza weling'ani nawo wetume wubi.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

weze muswanu kuli wanhu waswanu,

kuli walya du wono webate malajilizo gako.

5 Ninga lono wukuwalipiciza i wanhu wabi,

ale wulipicize sunga,

na wono si wakutyatya zo wutonji wako.

Mulamu weze kuli Isilayeli!


Zabuli 126

Kulomba wukombozwe

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, lono yacigomolela,

kanyhi ku Yelusalemi,

cali nha wanhu wono wali mu ndoto!

2 Baho cikagwa mu seko, cikemba ko wusangalale.

Sunga ne wanhu we makungugo ganji wakawa kutya,

“Muwaha Mulungu mbeka, kawatendela zimbaha!”

3 Na mbeka Muwaha Mulungu yacitendela mezino

mawaha, sunga na sese co weneco,

cikasangalala muno muno.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

cikukunena kanyhi wucimotese,

nha vyono vikolongo vihulumilaga malenga,

mu malolo gono ganyalile.

5 Wono wakuhadika zimbeyu zawo,

wakulila mahozi, wolodudula wasangalaye!

6 Wono wakubita ku wuhadici akuno wakulila,

wowuya wasangalaye wadinghuye ne ndudula yawo.


Zabuli 127

Lukumyo so wuswanu wo Mulungu

1 Muwaha Mulungu hono yalece kuyizenga kaya,

wono wakuyizenga wakwigaza bwete.

Muwaha Mulungu hono yalece kulidima likaya,

wono wakulilindiliza, wokwikala meso bwete.

2 Mulimo wenyu wa bwete.

Mwilawaga kwinuka hakali mitondo,

no kucela kugona, no kulya ndigwa ye lituguti.

Hambi Mulungu nha kawapelaga vyose,

wono yawendile lono wali mu nhongo.

3 I wana co cipegwa,

cono Muwaha Mulungu kacipelaga nawo,

co cipegwa cakwe kuli 'se.

4 Wehwanile nhe zisonga zono musilikale yebate,

vyo wenha wawo wana wono munhu kawatugaga,

lono yakali muzelelo.

5 Holeka mumoti munhu,

mono yamemizye songa nyinji,

hambi nha si kosumilwa lukulu,

kolo yakusitakwa,

no mulugu wakwe mu mahakama.


Zabuli 128

Cihumbilizwa kuli mono yamogopilo Muwaha Mulungu

1 Wamoti wose wono wamogopilo Muwaha Mulungu,

wono wakugatyatya malajilizo gakwe.

2 I milimo yako, yo yokutujila ne visakwa vyako,

kanyhi kowona nyemo no wumoteswe,

3 No mugolece wako, kowa yehwanile mu zabibu,

wono wukulela muno, mu kaya yako,

ne wanago wowa wehwanile vinhambi,

vyo mu zabibu vyono vikutula,

woyipilima meza yako yo wulilo.

4 Heya, nhavivyo vyo komoteswa munhu,

mono yamogopilo Muwaha Mulungu.

5 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakulugute ku wulawa ku Sayuni!

Nagwe wuwuwone wumoteswe wa Yelusalemi,

mu vilo vyose vyo wikale wako.

6 Heya, wikale sunga wuwawone wana we wanago!

Mulamu weze kuli Isilayeli!


Zabuli 129

Lilombo ku soko ye walugu wa Isilayeli

1 Isilayeli yakutya, “Walugu wangu,

wakuwa kungaza kali kenji,

ku wulawilila wuzelelo wangu!”

2 “Heya mbeka, i walugu wangu,

wangazaga wulawilila wuzelelo wangu,

ninga hamba si wasumiza yaye.

3 Wandelemulanga mu mugongo wangu,

wakawunoza nha mugunda wa kulimwa.

4 Ninga Muwaha Mulungu yo we ling'ani,

kamhonya mu wulawa mu wuwanda.”

5 Holeka wasumilwe lukulu kanyhi wakozwe cevu,

wagomolwe ku nyuma,

wawo wose wono walisulile likaya lya Sayuni.

6 Kanyhi wose wehwane mahanze,

gono gali mwi'tembe lye kaya,

gono ganyalaga na lono gakali,

hamba si natalimha,

7 hasina hamba yunji mono kogakongholeza,

yagawope mu madulu madulu.

8 Na hasina hamba yunji wo wose,

mono kokolela baho behi mono kosuma kutya,

“Muwaha Mulungu yamulugutizenyi!

Cikumulugutizizenyi mwi'tagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu!”


Zabuli 130

Kulomba wutazi

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

mu wukatale wangu ane nghukulilila.

2 Holeka go Muwaha, wuhulice cililo cangu,

wuhulicize wulombe wangu wunhaze.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

hono wali wuwe kukumbucilaga zo wubi wetu,

nani 'mbi mono yali yavumilwe nagwe?

4 Ninga 'gwe, kucinenelaga wubi wetu,

ho cikwitumamile kwe cogopo.

5 Niwulindiye wutazi wo Muwaha Mulungu,

na mu nghani yakwe nitumbiye.

6 Nimulindiye Muwaha Mulungu,

kuciza walindilizi vyono walanjililaga,

wele wa hakali mitondo, heya,

kuciza walindilizi wa hakali mitondo.

7 Gwe Isilayeli, wumwitumbile Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi mwene nha yena lusungu,

yewikanize nhondo zose ho yacikomboze.

8 Heya, kowakomboza wanhu wakwe Isilayeli,

mu wulawa mu wubi wawo wose.


Zabuli 131

Wulombe wo witumbilo wo winenenyhezi

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane nakondya kuwuleka widunhumuzi wangu,

nawuleka sunga no wiwoni wangu wose.

Alu, hamba si nghwisacila mawaha,

hamba zo wizine zono zikufutila.

2 Langa 'ne nhulile twii, kanyhi nina no mulamu,

mu nhumbula yangu, nha vyono mwana ketuzaga,

mu mhambaga yo nyina, nhavivyo ne nhumbula yangu,

vyo yitulile muli'ne.

3 Gwe Isilayeli wumwitumbile Muwaha Mulungu,

ku wulawa lulu, sunga ko ku cibitilila.


Zabuli 132

Lukumyo lwe Nyumba yo Mulungu

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, mukumbucile Dawudi,

wukumbucile magazo gose gono yagatuga.

2 Kanyhi 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wukumbucile sunga no wihuwizo wono yakwihuwiza,

no wilahize wono yakwilahiziza,

agwe go Mudaha Vyose, go Mulungu wa Yakobo,

3 nha vyono yatya si kokwinjila mu kaya,

hamba kugona mu citanda cakwe,

4 vyono si kosaka yagone nhongo,

hamba kusinzila,

5 sunga kolo yakondya kukuzenjela,

wikalo wako agwe go Mudaha Vyose,

go Muwaha Mulungu wa Yakobo!

6 Nase cazihulika kuko ku Efulata,

ze Lisanduku lye Cilagane,

cikalyagana mu mawimbi ga ku Yeyalimu.

7 “Heya, alenyi ku kaya yo Mulungu,

cikahembe he 'goda lyakwe lyo wutemi!”

8 Wuze ku Nyumba yako ne Lisanduku lye Cilagane,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ciwe co citanjilo ce vilungo vyako,

nagwe wukekale kuko cibitilila.

9 Ne wakohani wako waweze ling'ani nhondo zose,

ne wehuwilwa wako, nawo, watowe nyhenze zo wusangalale!

10 Na ku soko ya Dawudi mwitumami wako,

go Muwaha Mulungu,

holeka wulece kumulema yuyo mutemi wako,

mono wamuhagula.

11 Hambi nha yo gwegwe mono wamwihuwiza Dawudi,

wihuwizo wono wukangaye,

wono si kowubena yaye, lono watya,

“Nomutemya yumonga wa muli wanago yawe mutemi,

nayo kotema agwe kolo wakondya kufwa.

12 Na hono wanago wasume kwibata cilagane cangu du,

hamonga no kukamisa malajilizo gangu,

gono nawalajiliza, ale ne wana wawo nawo,

nhavivilya wojendelela kuwuhala wutemi wako,

vilo vyose.”

13 Muwaha Mulungu yalihagula likaya lya Sayuni,

yakasaka wuwe wo wikalo wakwe, yakatya,

14 “Ahano langa, ho wowa wo wikalo wangu,

cibitilila, ho hanhu hono notema.

15 Nane nolipela likaya lya Sayuni,

visakwa vyalyo vyose,

kanyhi nowegutya zindigwa iwaciwa wayo.

16 Ne wakohani wayo nawo,

nowamotya mu milimo yawo,

ne wehuwilwa wayo nawo,

wotowa nyhenze zo wusangalale.

17 Na mumo namo, mo nomunozeza yumonga,

wa mu wana wa Dawudi, yawe mutemi mu waha,

kanyhi nowusikamya wutemi,

wo mutemi wangu muhagulwa.

18 Nane nowakoza cevu walugu wakwe,

ninga wutemi wakwe womoteswa muno muno.”


Zabuli 133

Lukumyo kuli wendo wo wundugu

1 Lanje ta vyono yo nghani swanu muno,

kanyhi yono yikwendeza,

hono we zindugu wawe wekalane hamonga ko wumonga.

2 Wakuwa wehwanile mafuta go wuguzi wukamu,

gono gakuhuluma mu mutwe na mu ndevu za Haluni,

gakuhulumila sunga mo mu singo,

mu vivwalo vyakwe vyo wukohani.

3 Gakwihwana buhe ya mwi'tunda lya Helimoni,

yono yigwizaga mu matunda ga Sayuni.

Baho ho hanhu hono Muwaha Mulungu,

yehehuwiziziza u wulugute wakwe.

Heya, wumi we cibitilila.


Zabuli 134

Lucemo lo kumukumya Mulungu

1 Ale nzonyi mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

anye nyo wetumami wakwe wose,

anye wose mono mumwitumamilaga,

ne cilo mu Nyumba yakwe.

2 Mwinule miwoko yenyu,

muyilanzye kono koyili Nyumba yo Mulungu,

mumukumye Muwaha Mulungu!

3 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yawumba wulanga ne yinze,

yamulugutizenyi ku wulawa ku Sayuni,


Zabuli 135

Lwimbo lo lukumyo

1 Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!

Likumyenyi litagwa lyakwe,

anye nyo wetumami wo Muwaha Mulungu,

2 anye mono mwimaga mu Nyumba yo Muwaha Mulungu,

mu Nyumba yo Mulungu wetu.

3 Mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu, hambi nha muswanu,

mulimbile litagwa lyakwe zo lukumyo,

hambi nha vyo yinojeye.

4 Muwaha Mulungu yakehagulila Yakobo,

yawe munhu wakwe, ne wanhu wa Isilayeli,

wawe sawo yakwe mweneco.

5 Manyiye lukulu vyono,

Muwaha Mulungu yo muwaha yacizize,

u Muwaha Mulungu wetu,

yo Yalawiliciye mu milungu yose.

6 Mwene Muwaha Mulungu wetu,

kanozaga nha vyono yendile mweneco,

ku wulanga, hamba mono'nze,

hamba mwi'lenga, sunga na mu visolo vyayo.

7 Mwene kagaletaga mavunde,

ku wulawa ku mhelo ye yinze,

kayiletaga mvula mbaha mu nzila ye mamuli,

kanyhi kayiletaga mbeho mbaha muno,

ku wulawa ha wikalo wayo.

8 Yawawulaga i wana walongozi kulelwa wose,

ku Misili, i wana we wanhu,

sunga ne mana ge zinimu nhavivyo.

9 Nayo yakanoza mapoto,

ne vitanjilo kuli'gwe go Misili,

kuli Falawo hamonga ne wetumami wakwe wose.

10 Yakagongomeza makungugo menji,

yakawawulaga sunga,

ne watemi, wono wali wali vilungo,

11 Sihoni, mutemi we Waamoli,

na Ogu mutemi wa Bashani,

sunga ne watemi wose wa mu Kanani.

12 Yasola zisi zawo, yakawapela wanhu wakwe,

yawapela Isilayeli wuwe wuhazi wawo.

13 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

litagwa lyako lyowa kukumbucilwa cibitilila,

nagwe kokumbucilwa ne wanhu we zindelo zose.

14 Hambi Muwaha Mulungu,

nha kowatetela wanhu wakwe,

kanyhi kowa no lusungu kuli wetumami wakwe.

15 I milungu ye makungugo,

nha milungu ya fweza na zahabu,

minozwe ne miwoko ye wanhu.

16 Yina milomo, ninga hamba si yikulonga yaye,

yina ne meso, ninga hamba si yikuwona ng'o.

17 Ne makutu yina nago,

ninga hamba si yikuhulika bwete,

hamba kwehela kose, si yehelaga yaye.

18 Holeka wose wono wayinoza,

na wose wono wakuyitumbila,

wokwihwana ne milungu yiyo!

19 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu!

Nanye mo wakohani, mo lulelo lwa Haluni,

mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu!

20 Nanye mo Walawi,

mukumyenyi Muwaha Mulungu!

Sunga nanye mono mumogopile,

Muwaha Mulungu, mukumyenyi!

21 Muwaha Mulungu yakumywe mu Sayuni,

yakumywe sunga na mu wikalo wakwe ku Yelusalemi.

Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe!


Zabuli 136

Lwimbo lo wulumbe

1 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

hambi nha muswanu, hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

2 Mumulumbe Mulungu,

mono Yalawiliciye mu milungu yose,

hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

3 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yacizize wawaha wose,

hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

4 Yo mweneco yali wene du,

mono ketumaga mapoto mawaha,

hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

5 Yawuwumba wulanga ko wizuce wakwe mweneco,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

6 Yayitandaza yisi,

mucanyha ye visolo vye malenga,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

7 Yawumba lizuwa, no mwezi, hamonga ne zinyelezi,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

8 Lizuwa liteme mu misi, hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

9 Mwezi ne zinyelezi ziteme ku cilo,

hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

10 Yawawulaga i wana wose,

we Wamisili iwalongozi kulelwa,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

11 Yakawawusa wanhu wa Isilayeli ku Misili,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila,

12 ko muwoko wakwe we vilungo,

hamonga no wuwaha wakwe,

hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

13 Yalitemanula lilenga lya Shamu,

hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila,

14 yakawakoza mumo wanhu wa Isilayeli,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

15 Ninga yakawongomezeza mumo Falawo,

hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

16 Yawatonga wanhu wakwe mu nyika,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

17 Yawawulaga watemi we vilungo,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

18 Yawawulaga watemi we lilumbo,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

19 Yakamuwulaga sunga na Sihoni,

u mutemi we Waamoli, hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila,

20 sunga na Ogu nhavivyo nayo,

u mutemi wa Bashani, hambi lusungu lwakwe,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

21 Yakazisola zisi zawo, yakawapela wanhu wakwe,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila,

22 ho ziwe sawo ya Isilayeli u mwitumami wakwe,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

23 Mwene yacikumbucila mwidaha lyo wusocele wetu,

hambi lusungu lwakwe nha lwikalaga cibitilila,

24 yakaciponya kuli walugu wetu,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

25 Mwene kavilisaga ndigwa viwumbwa vyumi vyose,

hambi lusungu lwakwe nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

26 Mumulumbe Mulungu wa ku wulanga,

hambi lusungu lwakwe, nha lwikalaga cibitilila.


Zabuli 137

Cililo ce Waisilayeli ku yisi yo wujenzi

1 Cikekala mu zi nyenje ze makolongo ga Babiloni,

cikawa kulila, lono cali cayikumbucila Sayuni.

2 Sunga cikagatumbika ne masumbi getu,

mu mibici ya mu yisi yiyo.

3 Kuko nawo wono waciteka, wakawa kusaka ciwembile,

wawo wono wacigazaga,

wakasaka ciwasangalaze, wakutya,

“Mucimbile ta, lwimbo lwa Sayuni!”

4 Alu mwene, ho cokwimba nhawule lwimbo,

lo kumukumya Muwaha Mulungu,

akuno ho cili mu yisi yo wujenzi?

5 Gwe go Yelusalemi,

hono mbeka ane nghusemwe, ciba le hodu,

ndece hamba kutowa ne lisumbi kanyhi!

6 Hono ndece kukukumbucila gwegwe go Yelusalemi,

na hono ndece kukwihwesa agwe go nyemo yangu.

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kuzisemwa yaye zono wazituma Waedomu,

lono Yelusalemi yasolelwa ne walugu,

wukumbucile vyono watya,

“Yimwagulenyi Yelusalemi!

Sunga ne mabwe go musinji gose!”

8 Nagwe go Babiloni kokongomezwa.

Kanyhi yo mumoti munhu mono kokulipiciza,

nha vyono wacitendela 'se!

9 Mono kowasola wanago,

no kuwapondanjizanga mwi'talawe!


Zabuli 138

Lilombo lyo wulumbe

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukulumba kwe nhumbula yangu yose,

nane nokwimba zo lukumyo lwako hali milungu.

2 Nane nohemba niyidomile Nyumba yako yono yelile,

kanyhi nokulumba ku soko yo lusungu lwako,

hamonga no wihuwilwa wako.

Hambi nha kulajila vyono litagwa lyako,

hamonga ne malajilizo gako, vyo vilawiliciye.

3 Nane lono nakulilila, nagwe wukahulika,

kanyhi wukasikamya kwe vilungo vyako.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

i watemi wose wa kono'nze nha wokukumya gwegwe,

hambi nha wakondya kuzihulika zo wihuwizo wako.

5 Nawo wokwimba zo lukumyo lwe metumo gako,

'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ku soko yo wuwaha wo lukumyo lwako.

6 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

hamba wuwe wuli kucanyha muno,

ninga wukuwalanga ko wuswanu i wasocele,

ne wewoni wose, si wosuma kwihundika ha meso gako.

7 Na lono wugaye winji wumhilime, agwe kundimaga,

kuwemilaga walugu wangu mu makuwo gawo,

kanyhi wukuwa kumhonya kwe vilungo vyako.

8 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

kozifisiliza zose zono wehuwiza.

No lusungu lwako 'gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nha lwikalaga cibitilila.

Wuyikondeleze milimo yako yono wayanza.


Zabuli 139

Wumanye wo Mulungu no wukamisi wakwe

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, agwe wamhima,

wakondya kumanya sunga,

zono zili mu nhumbula yangu.

2 Agwe wuzimanyile zose zono nitumaga,

kanyhi wugamanyile sunga,

ne mahweso gangu gose.

3 Kanyhi wukondizye kumbona,

lono nili mu mulimo, hamba hono nyhehile,

wuyimanyile nyipiganza yangu yose.

4 Sunga na lono nghali,

sinalonga cinji co cose,

agwe wakondya kuzimanya zi nyhici zono nolonga.

5 Hambi 'gwe nha wumhilime mbavu zose,

kanyhi kundimaga kwe vilungo vyako.

6 Hambi wumanye wako,

nha wunyhizize mahala gangu,

wumanye wuwo wuli kucanyha muno,

hamba si nosuma kuwuficila.

7 Alu notiziza hayi, ho niwe kutali nagwe?

Hamba nhizize hayi, kono kowa wuli kusina 'gwe?

8 Na hono nitye, ta nhizize ku wulanga,

agwe wuli baho,

na hono nitye, ta nhizize ku yisi ye wafu,

nako agwe wuli kuko du,

9 hamba nitye, nguluce sunga,

kono lisosomocelaga lizuwa,

hamba mbite nghekale kutali muno,

ku lubavu lwa kumwezi.

10 Kuko nako, nha vyo konyagana du,

kowa wuli kuko, ho wusume kunhaza.

11 Na hamba nali sace wutitu wungubize,

no wuzelu wono wumhilime ciwe cilo.

12 Hamba nhavivyo cilo nha si cinji kuli'gwe,

ne cilo kuli'gwe nha yinha misi du,

hambi wutitu no wuzelu nha co cimonga du kuli'gwe.

13 Hambi nha yo gwegwe,

mono wamumba muwili ne nhumbula,

wamumbila mu'nda ya yaya.

14 Nane nghukulumba muno, hambi,

nha wunojeye wupegwe cogopo,

hambi metumo gako nha ga wizine muno,

nane nigamanyiye lukulu mu nhumbula yangu.

15 Na lono mafupa gangu gawumbwaga,

no kulunzizwa mu'nda ya yaya,

na lono nawilucilaga mumo cinyele,

agwe wali wumanyile vyono nili mumo,

16 hambi 'gwe nha wali wumbwene,

na lono nali nghali nalelwa.

Sunga ne vilo vyangu vyose,

wukavyandika mu citabu cako,

lono kwali kukali kusina hamba cinji.

17 Gwe go Mulungu,

mbeka kukamu kumanya ze mahweso gako,

nago si gasumikaga hamba ku wuwalwe kose.

18 Na hono nali nitye nigawale,

gali gawe menji kuciza no musawawa,

Hambi nha lono nghusinhuka du,

nghali nili hamonga nagwe.

19 Yali yiwe yili ciba le go Mulungu,

hono wali wuwawulaje wawo i wabi!

Yali yiwe yili ciba,

wawulaji wawo hono wali wambucile.

20 Hambi nha wakukulonga agwe makani mabi,

kanyhi wakulilonga litagwa lyako makani ga cevu!

21 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

ane niwasulile mbeka,

i wanhu wono si wakwendile,

kanyhi niwabedegaye muno muno,

wono si wakuhulice gwegwe!

22 Holeka niwasuliye lukulu.

No kuwanoza nha walugu wangu.

23 Wulondole mu nhumbula yangu 'gwe go Mulungu,

ho wumanye vyono ninha, wumhime,

wugamanye mahweso gangu.

24 Wunanje hono nina nghani yo yose,

'mbi mu nhumbula yangu,

wunhonje mu nzila ye cibitilila.


Zabuli 140

Kulomba wudimi wo Mulungu

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wumhonye kuli wanhu wabi,

wunyhitile kuli wanhu wawulaji.

2 Hambi wanhu we liwala lilyo,

nha wehwesaga zimbi du nhondo zose,

kanyhi wekalaga wali ciwuma-wuma du nhondo zose.

3 Wena nimi nghali,

zihwanile nha sumu ye zinzoka.

Ne zinghani zawo zihwanile,

ne sumu ye lizoka li nyamwilu.

4 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wunyhitile kuli vilungo vyo mubi,

kanyhi wunyhitile sunga na kuli wawulaji,

wono wena wuhile wo kunyajiliza.

5 I wedunhumuzi wanhega ne mitego yawo,

watandaze milejehe nha nyavu,

kanyhi wahundice mitego yawo,

mu nzila ho wangwize.

6 Nane nghamulonjela Muwaha Mulungu nghatya,

“Agwe yo gwegwe go Mulungu wangu du.”

Holeka wuhulicize lizi lyo wulombe wangu, 'gwe

go Muwaha Mulungu wunhaze.

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

go vilungo vye mhonelo yangu,

kunyhitila mu vilo vyo wulugu.

8 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wulece kuwapela i wabi visakwa vyawo,

kanyhi wulece kuwamotya mu wuhile wawo wubi,

9 Wulece kuwapela wusume walugu wangu,

ne zimbali zawo ziwawuyile weneco!

10 Ne makala go moto gawagwile,

wagwilizwe mu makombo, walece kulawila kanyhi.

11 I wabalalizi si wosuma kwikala mono'nze,

no wubi wuwagane i wawulaji,

no kuwongomeza lwa kamonga!

12 Nane manyiye lukulu,

vyono Muwaha Mulungu komudima musocele,

kanyhi koyitetela ne ling'ani yo muciwa.

13 Mbeka wono wali ling'ani wokulumba muno,

kanyhi wokwikalana na gwegwe.


Zabuli 141

Wulombe we cinya Mihe

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukucemeleza wuze wumhonye mbela!

Wuhulicize lizi lyangu kolo nghukucemeleza!

2 No wulombe wangu wukuficile nha wuvumba,

sunga no winule we mawoko gangu,

wuweze nhe likumbiko lya mwi'daha lye cinya mihe.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wuwudime lulimi lwangu,

wuwice mulindilizi ha mulomo wangu.

4 Wunyhitile kuli zo wituma zimbi,

ndece kwilunza no wajilile wa wetuma zo wavu,

kanyhi ndecele lukulu kuhanga nawo,

mu zindigwa za mu zi sikukuu zawo.

5 Na ku soko ye cambuzi cangu,

nokwenda munhu u muswanu,

yafundile mu nzila yo kunhowa ninga,

si nokwendela lukulu i wabi wanghumye,

hambi 'ne nha nikalaga nghulomba,

ku soko ye metumo gawo mabi.

6 Ninga i wawaha wawo,

kolo wapondwapondwa mwi'talawe baho,

langa ho womanyila,

vyono wulonje wangu wali wa mbeka.

7 Nha vyono vinha vidumulwa-dumulwa vye zingwagu,

ne mafupa gawo gowa geyagaye,

ha mulango we citenjelo.

8 Ninga'ne nghwihuwiye gwegwe du,

go Muwaha Mulungu,

agwe yo gwegwe go wutizizo wangu,

holeka wulece kundeka nili wene.

9 Nghukunena wunyhitile,

kuli mitego yono wanhejile,

wumhonye kuli zi nonga zawo,

zono wakunhega wawo i wanhu wabi.

10 I wabi wegwize weneco mu mitego yawo,

lono ane nghukola mholile.

Kulomba wutazi.


Zabuli 142

1 Nghumucemeleza Muwaha Mulungu yanhaze,

nghumunena akuno nghulila.

2 Nghumuwicila wulilanzice wangu,

nghumuloloseza zo wugazice wangu wose.

3 Lono nili behi kukatala mu nhumbula,

mwene yazimanyile zose ze mijendele yangu.

Nawo walugu wanhejeze no mutego.

4 Na lono nghulanga ako na ako,

si nghumuwona hamba munhu wo wose,

mono komhela wutazi,

hamba ne hanhu ho kutiziza, hose sina,

hamba munhu yumonga bwete hasina,

mono yakumbona nha vyono nane nili munhu.

5 Holeka nane nghukucemeleza agwe,

go Muwaha Mulungu wunhaze.

Hambi nha yo gwegwe du,

go wutizizo wangu,

yo gwegwe du mono wina vyose,

vyono nghuhila mu mikalile yangu.

6 Holeka wuhulice wucemeleze wangu wunhaze,

hambi nha natimba muno,

wumhonye kuli walugu wangu,

hambi nha wali vilungo wanyhizize.

7 Wumbusize magaziko gono gali muli'ne nane,

ho nghulumbile mu wihangulize, wa we ling'ani,

hambi 'gwe nha kunhendela ziswanu.


Zabuli 143

Kulomba wutazi

1 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wuhulice wulombe wangu!

Ku soko ye ling'ani yako,

holeka wuhulice wulombe wangu!

Kanyhi wumhitule ku soko yo wihuwilwa wako.

2 Kanyhi wulece kuning'aniza ane no mwitumami wako,

hambi hali'gwe nha hasina mono yali ling'ani,

hamba yumonga bwete.

3 Hambi mulugu nha kanhyatilila,

kasumila lukulu.

Kawa kunyinjiza hanhu ha citi,

niwa nhe wanhu wono wafwa katali.

4 Holeka na mu nhumbula nakatala,

sunga nalu fwiye yitenene ku soko yo woga.

5 Nalu nghukumbucila ze vilo vyono vyapuluta,

nghwihwesa muli zose zono wazinoza,

nghwihwesa ze metumo gako gose.

6 Nane nghwaliye maganza gangu nghulomba,

nghukaluciye muno,

nehwana yisi nghavu yono yisina malenga.

7 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu wumhitule mbela,

hambi nha natimba muno mu nhumbula yangu.

Wulece kuwa kutali nane,

ho ndece kwihwana na awalya,

wono wakubita ku yisi ye wafu.

8 Wumanyize zo lusungu lwako nhondo zose,

hakali mitondo,

hambi ane nha nghwitumbiye gwegwe du.

Wuhulice wulombe wangu,

wundajile ne nzila yono noyityatya,

9 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ane nghutiziza kuli'gwe,

wumhonye kuli walugu wangu.

10 Wunyijize ho sume kwituma zo wuhile wako,

hambi nha yo gwegwe go Mulungu wangu!

Na so wuswanu wako,

wunhonje mu nzila yono yigolocile.

11 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

wunghamise ho niwe mholile nha vyono wehuwiza,

ale wumhonye mu wugaye ku soko yo wuswanu wako.

12 Na ku soko yo lusungu lwako kuli'ne,

wuwawulagaje walugu wangu,

wuwongomeze wose wono wakunhendela zimbi,

hambi ne nha nili mwitumami wako.


Zabuli 144

Mutemi yakumulumba Mulungu so wusume

1 Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe,

mono yo mukamisi wangu,

kanyhi yo mhonelo yangu,

mono kanyijizaga'ne zo wihomi,

kanyhi kambikanizaga kuli zo wulugu.

2 Mwene yo mudimi wangu mwihuwilwa,

kanyhi yo kanyhitilaga muli zose,

kanyhi yo muponyi wangu.

Mwene yo mudimi wangu mono nimwitumbiye,

Mwene kagasumilizaga makungugo,

no kugawika hasi yangu.

3 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

alo munhu co cici sunga wumukumbucile?

Hamba munhu co cici,

sunga wuwe kwikalaga wukumwihwesa?

4 Hambi munhu nha yehwanile mbeho,

yono yikuputa du,

sunga ne vilo vyakwe,

vihwanile mubeho wono wupulutaga du.

5 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, wudemule wulanga wutime!

Wugapamize matunda, nago golavya lyosi!

6 Wugumile limuli liweyagaze, lete zisonga zako,

wuwahangavye watize!

7 Wuze sunga ho hasi ku wulawa ku wulanga,

wumbuse amuno mu malenga menji,

wumhonye mu vilungo vye wajenzi,

8 wono walongaga zo wulambi nhondo zose,

kanyhi wendile kuhomelela,

no kwilahiza kuli zo wulambi.

9 Gwe go Mulungu, ane nokwimbila lwimbo lupya,

kanyhi nokutowela ndonondo nghwimbile lukumyo.

10 Agwe mono kuwapelaga watemi wusume,

no kumuponya Dawudi mwitumami wako!

11 Wumhonye kuli walugu wangu wawulaji,

wumhonye mu vilungo vye wajenzi,

wono wekalaga wakulonga zo wulambi nhondo zose,

kanyhi wendile kuhomelela no kwilahiza zo wulambi.

12 Ne wana wetu we cilume nawo,

waweze nhe vilimwa vyono vikuwiluka viswanu,

mu wuzelelo wawo, ne wana wetu we cicekulu nawo,

waweze nhe masumbili ga mu zi nhuzi,

gono makakate viswanu muno, mwi'kulu lyo mutemi.

13 Ne madongha getu nago,

gamemywe nhwanga ye mawala gose,

ne zingholo zetu nazo mu migunda yetu,

zilele kali maelufu na maelufu.

14 Nazo zing'ombe zetu,

zeze zina miwili miswanu.

Kanyhi zili vilungo, ho zilele viswanu,

kanyhi halece kuwa na cililo,

co wugazwe mu wikalo wetu.

15 Limotile likungugo lyono,

lyakondya kupegwa zizo!

Wamotile iwanhu wono u Muwaha Mulungu,

yo Mulungu wawo!


Zabuli 145

Lwimbo lo lukumyo

1 Gwe go Mulungu, ane nozanza zo wuwaha wako,

go mutemi wangu, nolikumya litagwa lyako,

nhondo zose cibitilila.

2 Nowa kukulumba nhondo zose,

nolikumya litagwa lyako nhondo zose cibitilila.

3 Muwaha Mulungu yo muwaha mono,

yanojeye kukumywa muno,

no wuwaha wakwe wucizize wumanye wose,

4 Ne zindelo na ndelo,

zojendelela kukumya ze metumo gako,

gono wetuma, no kwanza ze metumo gako mawaha.

5 Zojendelela kuzilonga,

zo wuwaha wo lukumyo lwako,

nane nojendelela kuzihwesa,

ze metumo gako go wizine.

6 Kanyhi wanhu wozilonga,

ze metumo gako go wogofye,

nane nokwanza zo wuwaha wako.

7 Wokwanza zo lukumyo lo winji wo wuswanu wako,

hamonga no kwimba kuli zo wiling'ano wako.

8 Muwaha Mulungu yo munya nheto no lusungu,

mwene si kakolwaga ne likuwo mbela,

yena lusungu luwaha.

9 Muwaha Mulungu, yo muswanu kuli wanhu wose,

kanyhi yali lusungu kuli viwumbwa vyakwe vyose.

10 Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu,

viwumbwa vyako vyose vyokulumba,

ne wanhu wako wehuwilwa nawo wokukumya.

11 Kanyhi wokwanza,

zo lukumyo lo wutemi wako,

no kwanza ze vilungo vyako.

12 Ho wasume kuwamanyiza wanhu,

ze metumo gako mawaha,

hamonga no lukumyo lo wutemi wako.

13 No wutemi wako, wo wutemi we cibitilila,

no wulajilize wako, nha wikalaga cibitilila.

U Muwaha Mulungu nha mwihuwilwa,

mu wihuwizo wakwe,

kanyhi yali lusungu mu nyituma yakwe.

14 Muwaha Mulungu kawatazaga wose,

wono wali mu wugaye,

kanyhi kawenulaga wose wono wagwiye.

15 Viwumbwa vyose vikulanjiliye gwegwe du,

nagwe kuvipelelaga ndigwa mu vipango vyayo.

16 Nagwe kuvipelaga vyose vyono vikwiliye,

mu nghango ye visakwa vyayo.

17 Muwaha Mulungu,

yo yali ling'ani mu zinzila zakwe zose,

kanyhi mwene muswanu muli milimo yakwe yose.

18 Muwaha Mulungu yali behi,

na wose wono wakumucemeleza,

kuli wose wono wakumucemeleza,

kwe nhumbula yo winenenyhezi.

19 Muwaha Mulungu,

kawapelaga visakwa vyawo wose wono wamogopile.

Kacihulikaga cililo cawo no kuwaponya.

20 Muwaha Mulungu kawadimaga wose wono wamwendile,

ninga i wabi, kowongomeza wose.

21 Nokwanza zo lukumyo lo Muwaha Mulungu,

ne viwumbwa vyose,

vilikumye litagwa lyakwe lyono lyelile,

ku wulawa lulu, sunga cibitilila.


Zabuli 146

Lukumyo kuli Mulungu Mukombozi

1 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

Gwe go nhumbula yangu,

wumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

2 Nomulumba Muwaha Mulungu,

mu vilo vyo wikale wangu wose,

nomwimbila Mulungu wangu,

vilo vyose vyono nghali mumi.

3 Mulece kuwetumbila wawaha,

hambi muli wanhu nha musina mhonelo.

4 Hambi nha kolo wakufwa, wakugawuyila manghundi,

kanyhi na mwizuwa lilyo mahweso gawo gakwajilila.

5 Holeka yo mumoti mono yakumunoza Mulungu wa Yakobo,

kuwa yo mutazi wakwe, no witumbilo wakwe,

wukuwa wuli muli Muwaha Mulungu wakwe,

6 mono yawumba wulanga ne yinze, ne lilenga,

sunga na vyose vyono vili mumo.

Mwene kawukamisaga wihuwizo wakwe cibitilila.

7 Mono yakuwanozeza ling'ani wono wakugazwa,

kanyhi yakuwapela ndigwa wono wali nzala,

Muwaha Mulungu kawawopolelaga,

wono wadindiywe mu jela,

8 kanyhi Muwaha Mulungu,

kawanuguzaga meso wono wabofuye.

Muwaha Mulungu kawenulaga wono wagwiye,

Muwaha Mulungu yawendile wono wali ling'ani.

9 Muwaha Mulungu kawadimaga wajenzi,

wono wekaye mu yisi yetu,

kanyhi kawatazaga wacekulu wafwile hamonga,

ne wana wono wasina walezi,

ninga nzila ye wabi, mwene kayibidyangaga.

10 Muwaha Mulungu yatemile cibitilila,

Mulungu wako 'gwe go Sayuni,

yo mutemi muli zindelo zose.

Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!


Zabuli 147

Kumukumya Mulungu u Mudaha Vyose

1 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

Hambi nha viswanu,

kumwimbila Mulungu wetu ze lilumbo, heya,

viswanu kanyhi vyo yinojeye muno kumulumba.

2 Muwaha Mulungu,

yakulizenga likaya lya Yelusalemi,

yakuwakongholeza wapolile Waisilayeli,

wono wali weyagazwa.

3 Yakuwaponya wono wadejepe zinhumbula,

no kuziwopa zinghomele zawo.

4 Mwene kaziwalaga zinyelezi,

no kuzicemela zose matagwa.

5 Muwaha Mulungu wetu,

yo Muwaha mono yalawiliciye,

kanyhi yo we vilungo muno.

No wizuce wakwe wusina ndumo.

6 Muwaha Mulungu kawasimyaga

i wenenenyhezi ninga i wedunhumuzi,

kawagwisaga.

7 Ale, mwimbilenyi Muwaha Mulungu,

zi nyimbo zo wulumbe,

mutowelenyi ndonondo Mulungu wetu!

8 Mwene kawugubicizaga wulanga ne mavunde,

kawa kuyinozelezeza yinze mvula,

hamonga no kucindya mahanze mu matunda.

9 Mwene kazipelaga zi nimu ndigwa yazo,

sunga ne mana ge mahowe lono gakulila.

10 Mwene langa si yakuwa kwendezwa,

ne vilungo vye zifwalasi,

hamba si kasangalalilaga wutung'ati,

we wasilikale,

11 langa mwene kendezwaga,

ne wanhu wono wamogopile,

wanhu wono wetumbilaga lusungu lwakwe.

12 Gwe Yelusalemi,

wumulumbaje Muwaha Mulungu!

Wumulumbe Mulungu wako, 'gwe go Sayuni!

13 Hambi nha kayikamyaga milango yako,

kanyhi kawalugutiza ne wanhu wako.

14 Kawika mulamu mu mipaka yako

kanyhi yakukwigutya no wuhemba wuswanu muno.

15 Mwene kayilajilizaga yinze, ne nghani yakwe

yifisilizwaga musunho muno muno.

16 Kanyhi kalavyaga seluji

yiwa nha wuhamba,

kayanzazaga buhe kotya nha mavu.

17 Kaletaga mvula ye mabwe,

na hasina munhu mono

kosuma kuyikangalila mbeho yayo!

18 No kulavya wulajilize,

malenga gawa kwenjeduka.

Kaletaga mbeho mbaha,

malenga gawa kuhuluma.

19 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu kawamanyizaga,

i wanhu wakwe ze nghani yakwe,

nayo Isilayeli, zo wutonji wakwe

hamonga ne ning'aniza yakwe.

20 Ninga hamba si yawanozeza,

makungugo ganji zono zinha zizi,

holeka makungugo ganji,

hamba si wazimanyile ze malajilizo gakwe.

Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!


Zabuli 148

Yinze yose yimulumbo Mulungu

1 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

Ale, mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu,

wulawa ku wulanga,

mumulumbe anye mono mwikaye ku canyha.

2 Mumulumbe nanye nyo wamalayika wakwe,

mumulumbe nanye nyo lipuga lyose lya ku wulanga.

3 Mumulumbe nanye nyo mwezi ne lizuwa,

Mumulumbe nanye nyo zinyelezi,

zose zono zikung'alala.

4 Mumulumbe nanye nyo wulanga ku canyha,

sunga ne malenga gono gali ku canyha yo wulanga.

5 Litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu linojeye lilumbwe muno,

hambi mwene nha yo yalajiliza na vyose vikawumbwa.

6 Nayo yakavimya hanhu hayo sunga cibitilila,

navyo hamba si vyosuma kumubedegala yaye.

7 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu wulawa kono'nze,

nanye nyo visolo vye lilenga,

hamonga ne mazoka gayo.

8 Mumulumbe nanye nyo moto,

ne mvula ye mabwe hamonga ne seluji,

hamonga nagwe go mbeho yo mukukunzo,

mono wibate wulajilize wakwe.

9 Mumulumbe nanye nyo matunda ne vigongo,

sunga nanye nyo mibici,

ye mikalo hamonga ne mbago yose!

10 Mumulumbe nanye nyo vilimu vya mu mbago,

sunga nanye nyo mitugo yose,

sunga nanye nyo viwumbwa vyono vitambalaga hasi,

hamonga ne zindeje zose!

11 Mumulumbe nanye nyo watemi,

we'nze hamonga ne wanhu wose walongola,

ne walamuzi wose we'nze.

12 Mumulumbe nanye nyo wazelelo ne wahinza,

nyo wakombi hamonga ne wana wadodo!

13 Mulilumbe litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu,

anye nyo wanhu wose hambi litagwa lyakwe,

nha lilawiliciye, no lukumyo lwakwe,

lucizize kono'nze na ku wulanga.

14 Yawapela vilungo i wanhu wakwe,

wose ho wamulumbe mwene,

ne wana wa Isilayeli wono yawendile.

Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!


Zabuli 149

Lwimbo lo lukumyo

1 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

Mumwimbile Muwaha Mulungu lwimbo lupya,

mumukumizye mu mhuga ye wanhu we ling'ani!

2 Wusangalale, 'gwe Isilayeli,

hambi mwene nha yo muwumbi wako,

nanye nyo wekala ku Sayuni, sangalalenyi,

hambi mwene nha yo mutemi wenyu.

3 Mulikumizye litagwa lyakwe,

mu wuvina, mumwimbile kwe zing'oma ne masumbi.

4 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

nha kendezwa ne wanhu wakwe,

kawapelaga i wapolo wusume.

5 Nawo i wehuwilwa, wasecelelele mu lukumyo,

wasangalale cilo cose sunga wele.

6 Nhondo zose weze kwanza,

zo lukumyo luwaha lo Mulungu,

akuno webate ne mimaje mikali,

kose kose mu miwoko yawo,

7 ho wasume kuwalipiciza we makungugo,

no wulipicizwe we wanhu,

8 wawawope watemi wawo ne manyolo,

wawawope ne walongola wawo ne manyolo ge cuma,

9 wawalipicize we makungugo,

nha vyono Mulungu yalajiliza.

Na wuwo wo wusume we wanhu wo Mulungu.

Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!


Zabuli 150

Muwaha Mulungu yalumbwe

1 Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

Mumulumbile Mulungu mu Nyumba yakwe!

Mumulumbe ku soko yo wutemi wakwe ku wulanga!

2 Mumulumbe ku soko ye metumo gakwe,

ge vilungo gono yetumile,

mumulumbe ku soko yo lukumyo lwakwe luwaha.

3 Mumulumbe kwe lizi lye ndulele,

mumulumbe kwe masumbi ne zindonondo!

4 Mumulumbe kwe zing'oma no wuvina,

mumulumbe kwe zifilimbi ne zindonondo!

5 Mumulumbe ne visesele vye mazi mawaha,

mumulumbe ne visesele vilizi muno muno.

6 Ale, viwumbwa vyose vyumi,

vimulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!

Mumulumbe Muwaha Mulungu!
